```
     1→import { type Page, type Locator } from '@playwright/test';
     2→
     3→/**
     4→ * Page object for UploadZone component interactions.
     5→ *
     6→ * Encapsulates file upload functionality via both:
     7→ * - Click to upload (file input)
     8→ * - Drag and drop (not reliable in tests, use setInputFiles instead)
     9→ */
    10→export class UploadZonePage {
    11→  constructor(private readonly page: Page) {}
    12→
    13→  /**
    14→   * Get the upload zone dropzone area.
    15→   */
    16→  dropzone(): Locator {
    17→    return this.page.locator('.upload-zone');
    18→  }
    19→
    20→  /**
    21→   * Get the hidden file input element (used by react-dropzone).
    22→   * Checks both the upload zone and the empty state dropzone.
    23→   * Uses first() as there may be multiple upload zones on the page.
    24→   */
    25→  private fileInput(): Locator {
    26→    return this.page
    27→      .locator('.upload-zone input[type="file"], .empty-state input[type="file"]')
    28→      .first();
    29→  }
    30→
    31→  /**
    32→   * Get the upload zone error message if displayed.
    33→   */
    34→  errorMessage(): Locator {
    35→    return this.page.locator('.upload-zone-error');
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  /**
    39→   * Upload a single file via the file input.
    40→   * This is the most reliable method for Playwright tests.
    41→   *
    42→   * @param filePath - Absolute or relative path to the file
    43→   */
    44→  async uploadFile(filePath: string): Promise<void> {
    45→    await this.fileInput().setInputFiles(filePath);
    46→  }
    47→
    48→  /**
    49→   * Upload multiple files via the file input.
    50→   *
    51→   * @param filePaths - Array of file paths
    52→   */
    53→  async uploadFiles(filePaths: string[]): Promise<void> {
    54→    await this.fileInput().setInputFiles(filePaths);
    55→  }
    56→
    57→  /**
    58→   * Click the dropzone to trigger the file picker dialog.
    59→   * Note: This won't open an actual dialog in headless tests.
    60→   * Use uploadFile() or uploadFiles() instead for actual file upload.
    61→   */
    62→  async clickToUpload(): Promise<void> {
    63→    await this.dropzone().click();
    64→  }
    65→
    66→  /**
    67→   * Check if the dropzone is in uploading state.
    68→   */
    69→  async isUploading(): Promise<boolean> {
    70→    const className = await this.dropzone().getAttribute('class');
    71→    return className?.includes('upload-zone-uploading') ?? false;
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  /**
    75→   * Check if an error message is displayed.
    76→   */
    77→  async hasError(): Promise<boolean> {
    78→    return await this.errorMessage().isVisible();
    79→  }
    80→
    81→  /**
    82→   * Get the error message text if displayed.
    83→   */
    84→  async getErrorText(): Promise<string | null> {
    85→    const isVisible = await this.hasError();
    86→    if (!isVisible) {
    87→      return null;
    88→    }
    89→    return await this.errorMessage().textContent();
    90→  }
    91→
    92→  /**
    93→   * Dismiss the error message by clicking the dismiss button.
    94→   */
    95→  async dismissError(): Promise<void> {
    96→    await this.errorMessage().locator('.upload-zone-error-dismiss').click();
    97→  }
    98→
    99→  /**
   100→   * Wait for upload to complete.
   101→   * Waits for the uploading state to clear.
   102→   */
   103→  async waitForUploadComplete(options?: { timeout?: number }): Promise<void> {
   104→    await this.page.waitForFunction(() => {
   105→      const zone = document.querySelector('.upload-zone');
   106→      return zone && !zone.classList.contains('upload-zone-uploading');
   107→    }, options);
   108→  }
   109→
   110→  /**
   111→   * Get the upload zone text.
   112→   * Returns "Uploading...", "Drop files here", or "Drag files here or click to upload".
   113→   */
   114→  async getZoneText(): Promise<string> {
   115→    return (await this.dropzone().locator('.upload-zone-text').textContent()) ?? '';
   116→  }
   117→
   118→  /**
   119→   * Simulate drag-drop file upload.
   120→   * Note: This is more complex and less reliable than setInputFiles.
   121→   * Prefer uploadFile() for tests.
   122→   *
   123→   * @param filePath - Path to file to upload
   124→   */
   125→  async dragDropFile(filePath: string): Promise<void> {
   126→    // For Playwright, we can't truly simulate drag-drop from OS.
   127→    // Instead, we'll use the file input method which is more reliable.
   128→    // This method exists for API compatibility but delegates to uploadFile.
   129→    await this.uploadFile(filePath);
   130→  }
   131→}
```

```
    38→/** Parse an invite URL to extract token and ephemeral key */
    39→function parseInviteUrl(url: string): { token: string; ephemeralKey: string } {
    40→  // URL format: http://localhost:5173/#/invite/TOKEN?key=KEY
    41→  const hashPart = url.split('#')[1]; // /invite/TOKEN?key=KEY
    42→  const [path, query] = hashPart.split('?');
    43→  const token = path.split('/invite/')[1];
    44→  const params = new URLSearchParams(query);
    45→  const ephemeralKey = params.get('key')!;
    46→  return { token, ephemeralKey };
    47→}
```

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Error utilities for CipherBox API error handling.
     3→ *
     4→ * The Orval-generated custom instance (custom-instance.ts) throws plain Error
     5→ * objects with a `.status` property attached:
     6→ *
     7→ *   const err = new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
     8→ *   (err as Error & { status: number }).status = response.status;
     9→ *   throw err;
    10→ *
    11→ * These utilities detect and extract data from such errors.
    12→ */
    13→
    14→/**
    15→ * Detect HTTP 409 Conflict errors from the API.
    16→ * Used for IPNS publish conflict detection (sequence number mismatch).
    17→ */
    18→export function isConflictError(error: unknown): boolean {
    19→  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') return false;
    20→  // Orval custom-instance throws Error objects with .status attached
    21→  const e = error as Record<string, unknown>;
    22→  if (e.status === 409) return true;
    23→  return false;
    24→}
    25→
    26→/**
    27→ * Extract the current server sequence number from a 409 conflict error body.
    28→ *
    29→ * NOTE: The current custom-instance does not parse the error body --
    30→ * it only attaches the status code. The server sends currentSequenceNumber
    31→ * in the 409 JSON body, but the client cannot read it from the thrown Error.
    32→ * Future improvement: parse the response body in custom-instance and attach
    33→ * it to the error. For now, callers can re-sync via IPNS resolution.
    34→ */
    35→export function getConflictSequenceNumber(_error: unknown): string | undefined {
    36→  // The custom-instance does not attach the response body to the thrown error.
    37→  // Callers should resolve fresh state via resolveIpnsRecord() instead.
    38→  return undefined;
    39→}
    40→
```

```
    20→  marshalIpnsRecord,
    21→  type FolderMetadata,
    22→  type EncryptedFolderMetadata,
    23→  type FolderEntry,
    24→  type FolderChild,
    25→  type FilePointer,
    26→} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    27→import { addToIpfs, fetchFromIpfs } from '../lib/api/ipfs';
    28→import { createAndPublishIpnsRecord, resolveIpnsRecord } from './ipns.service';
    29→import { batchPublishIpnsRecords } from './ipns.service';
    30→import { isConflictError } from '../lib/errors';
    31→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
    32→import type { FolderNode } from '../stores/folder.store';
    33→import type { FileIpnsRecordPayload } from './file-metadata.service';
    34→
    35→/** Maximum folder nesting depth per FOLD-03 */
    36→const MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH = 20;
    37→
    38→/** Safe base64 encoding that avoids call stack overflow from spread operator */
    39→function uint8ToBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
    40→  let binary = '';
    41→  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
    42→    binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
    43→  }
    44→  return btoa(binary);
    45→}
    46→
    47→/**
    48→ * Calculate the depth of a folder from root.
    49→ * Root folder has depth 0, immediate children have depth 1, etc.
    50→ *
    51→ * Used for enforcing FOLD-03 depth limit of 20.
    52→ *
    53→ * @param folderId - Folder ID to calculate depth for (null = root)
    54→ * @param folders - Current folder tree
    55→ * @returns Depth from root (0 for root)
    56→ */
    57→export function getDepth(folderId: string | null, folders: Record<string, FolderNode>): number {
    58→  if (folderId === null) return 0; // root is depth 0
    59→
    60→  let depth = 0;
    61→  let currentId: string | null = folderId;
    62→
    63→  while (currentId !== null) {
    64→    const folder: FolderNode | undefined = folders[currentId];
    65→    if (!folder) break;
    66→    depth++;
    67→    currentId = folder.parentId;
    68→  }
    69→
    70→  return depth;
    71→}
    72→
    73→/**
    74→ * Load a folder's metadata from IPNS.
    75→ *
    76→ * Resolves the folder's IPNS name to get the current metadata CID,
    77→ * fetches and decrypts the metadata, and returns a complete FolderNode.
    78→ * Returns v2 metadata with FolderChild[] children.
    79→ *
    80→ * IMPORTANT: Does NOT eagerly resolve per-file IPNS records during folder load.
    81→ * File metadata is lazy-loaded on download/preview (Pitfall 1 from research).
    82→ *
    83→ * @param folderId - Folder ID (null for root, or UUID)
    84→ * @param folderKey - Decrypted AES-256 key for this folder
    85→ * @param ipnsPrivateKey - Decrypted Ed25519 private key for IPNS
    86→ * @param ipnsName - IPNS name for this folder
    87→ * @param parentId - Parent folder ID (null for root)
    88→ * @param name - Display name for this folder
    89→ */
    90→export async function loadFolder(
    91→  folderId: string | null,
    92→  folderKey: Uint8Array,
    93→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array,
    94→  ipnsName: string,
    95→  parentId: string | null,
    96→  name: string
    97→): Promise<FolderNode> {
    98→  // 1. Resolve IPNS to get current metadata CID
    99→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(ipnsName);
   100→
   101→  // 2. If IPNS resolution returns null, return unloaded folder so it can be retried
   102→  //    This handles newly-created subfolders whose IPNS hasn't propagated yet
   103→  if (!resolved) {
   104→    console.warn(
   105→      `IPNS resolution returned null for folder "${name}" (${ipnsName}). Marking as not loaded for retry.`
   106→    );
   107→    return {
   108→      id: folderId ?? 'root',
   109→      name,
   110→      ipnsName,
   111→      parentId,
   112→      children: [],
   113→      isLoaded: false,
   114→      isLoading: false,
   115→      sequenceNumber: 0n,
   116→      folderKey,
   117→      ipnsPrivateKey,
   118→    };
   119→  }
   120→
   121→  // 3. Fetch encrypted metadata from IPFS and decrypt with folderKey
   122→  const metadata = await fetchAndDecryptMetadata(resolved.cid, folderKey);
   123→
   124→  // 4. Return complete FolderNode with decrypted children
   125→  // FilePointer objects have fileMetaIpnsName instead of inline file data.
   126→  return {
   127→    id: folderId ?? 'root',
   128→    name,
   129→    ipnsName,
   130→    parentId,
   131→    children: metadata.children ?? [],
   132→    isLoaded: true,
   133→    isLoading: false,
   134→    sequenceNumber: resolved.sequenceNumber,
   135→    folderKey,
   136→    ipnsPrivateKey,
   137→  };
   138→}
   139→
   140→/**
   141→ * Create a new subfolder with generated keys.
   142→ *
   143→ * Generates new Ed25519 IPNS keypair and AES-256 folder key,
   144→ * wraps them with the user's public key for storage.
   145→ *
   146→ * @param params.parentFolderId - Parent folder ID (null for root)
   147→ * @param params.name - Folder name
   148→ * @param params.userPublicKey - User's secp256k1 public key for ECIES wrapping
   149→ * @param params.folders - Current folder tree (for depth checking)
   150→ * @returns Created folder entry and decrypted keys
   151→ * @throws Error if depth limit exceeded (FOLD-03)
   152→ */
   153→export async function createFolder(params: {
   154→  parentFolderId: string | null;
   155→  name: string;
   156→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   157→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>;
   158→}): Promise<{
   159→  folder: FolderEntry;
   160→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   161→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
   162→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
   163→  keyEpoch?: number;
   164→}> {
   165→  // 1. Check depth limit (FOLD-03)
   166→  const parentDepth = getDepth(params.parentFolderId, params.folders);
   167→  if (parentDepth >= MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH) {
   168→    throw new Error(`Cannot create folder: maximum depth of ${MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH} exceeded`);
   169→  }
   170→
   171→  // 2. Generate Ed25519 keypair for folder IPNS
   172→  const ipnsKeypair = await generateEd25519Keypair();
   173→  const ipnsName = await deriveIpnsName(ipnsKeypair.publicKey);
   174→
   175→  // 3. Generate random AES-256 folder key
   176→  const folderKey = generateRandomBytes(32);
   177→
   178→  // 4. Wrap keys with user's public key (ECIES encryption)
   179→  // The 64-byte libp2p format private key (privateKey || publicKey) is wrapped
   180→  const ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted = bytesToHex(
   181→    await wrapKey(ipnsKeypair.privateKey, params.userPublicKey)
   182→  );
   183→  const folderKeyEncrypted = bytesToHex(await wrapKey(folderKey, params.userPublicKey));
   184→
   185→  // 5. TEE-02: Encrypt IPNS private key with TEE public key for republishing
   186→  let encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: string | undefined;
   187→  let keyEpoch: number | undefined;
   188→
   189→  const teeKeys = useAuthStore.getState().teeKeys;
   190→  if (teeKeys?.currentPublicKey) {
   191→    const teePublicKey = hexToBytes(teeKeys.currentPublicKey);
   192→    const encryptedKey = await wrapKey(ipnsKeypair.privateKey, teePublicKey);
   193→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey = bytesToHex(encryptedKey);
   194→    keyEpoch = teeKeys.currentEpoch;
   195→  }
   196→
   197→  // 6. Create folder entry for parent's metadata
   198→  const now = Date.now();
   199→  const folder: FolderEntry = {
   200→    type: 'folder',
   201→    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
   202→    name: params.name,
   203→    ipnsName,
   204→    ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   205→    folderKeyEncrypted,
   206→    createdAt: now,
   207→    modifiedAt: now,
   208→  };
   209→
   210→  return {
   211→    folder,
   212→    ipnsPrivateKey: ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
   213→    folderKey,
   214→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   215→    keyEpoch,
   216→  };
   217→}
   218→
   219→/**
   220→ * Update folder metadata and publish to IPNS (v2 format).
   221→ *
   222→ * Encrypts the metadata with the folder key, uploads to IPFS,
   223→ * and publishes an updated IPNS record pointing to the new CID.
   224→ *
   225→ * @param params.folderId - Folder being updated
   226→ * @param params.children - New children array (FolderChild[])
   227→ * @param params.folderKey - Decrypted AES-256 folder key
   228→ * @param params.ipnsPrivateKey - Decrypted Ed25519 IPNS private key
   229→ * @param params.ipnsName - IPNS name for this folder
   230→ * @param params.sequenceNumber - Current sequence number
   231→ * @param params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey - TEE-wrapped key (first publish only)
   232→ * @param params.keyEpoch - TEE key epoch (required with encryptedIpnsPrivateKey)
   233→ * @returns New CID and sequence number
   234→ */
   235→export async function updateFolderMetadata(params: {
   236→  folderId: string;
   237→  children: FolderChild[];
   238→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
   239→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   240→  ipnsName: string;
   241→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
   242→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
   243→  keyEpoch?: number;
   244→}): Promise<{ cid: string; newSequenceNumber: bigint }> {
   245→  // 1. Create v2 folder metadata
   246→  const metadata: FolderMetadata = {
   247→    version: 'v2',
   248→    children: params.children,
   249→  };
   250→
   251→  // 2. Encrypt metadata with folder key
   252→  const encrypted = await encryptFolderMetadata(metadata, params.folderKey);
   253→
   254→  // 3. Upload to IPFS via backend relay
   255→  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(encrypted)], {
   256→    type: 'application/json',
   257→  });
   258→  const { cid } = await addToIpfs(blob);
   259→
   260→  // 4. Publish IPNS record pointing to new CID.
   261→  //    Send the pre-increment sequence as expectedSequenceNumber so the API can
   262→  //    detect concurrent modifications from other devices (conflict detection).
   263→  const newSeq = params.sequenceNumber + 1n;
   264→  await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
   265→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.ipnsPrivateKey,
   266→    ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
   267→    metadataCid: cid,
   268→    sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   269→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   270→    keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
   271→    expectedSequenceNumber: params.sequenceNumber.toString(),
   272→  });
   273→
   274→  return { cid, newSequenceNumber: newSeq };
   275→}
   276→
   277→/**
   278→ * Build a folder IPNS record payload for batch publishing.
   279→ *
   280→ * Creates and signs an IPNS record locally without publishing.
   281→ * Returns the payload that can be included in a batch publish call.
   282→ *
   283→ * @returns Folder IPNS record payload for batch publish
   284→ */
   285→async function buildFolderIpnsRecord(params: {
   286→  children: FolderChild[];
   287→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
   288→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   289→  ipnsName: string;
   290→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
   291→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
   292→  keyEpoch?: number;
   293→}): Promise<{
   294→  cid: string;
   295→  record: FileIpnsRecordPayload & { recordType: 'folder'; expectedSequenceNumber: string };
   296→  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
   297→}> {
   298→  // 1. Create v2 folder metadata
   299→  const metadata: FolderMetadata = {
   300→    version: 'v2',
   301→    children: params.children,
   302→  };
   303→
   304→  // 2. Encrypt metadata with folder key
   305→  const encrypted = await encryptFolderMetadata(metadata, params.folderKey);
   306→
   307→  // 3. Upload to IPFS
   308→  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(encrypted)], { type: 'application/json' });
   309→  const { cid } = await addToIpfs(blob);
   310→
   311→  // 4. Create IPNS record
   312→  const newSeq = params.sequenceNumber + 1n;
   313→  const record = await createIpnsRecord(
   314→    params.ipnsPrivateKey,
   315→    `/ipfs/${cid}`,
   316→    newSeq,
   317→    24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
   318→  );
   319→  const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
   320→  const recordBase64 = uint8ToBase64(recordBytes);
   321→
   322→  return {
   323→    cid,
   324→    record: {
   325→      ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
   326→      recordBase64,
   327→      metadataCid: cid,
   328→      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   329→      keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
   330→      recordType: 'folder',
   331→      // Send pre-increment sequence as expectedSequenceNumber for conflict detection.
   332→      // File records (recordType: 'file') do not set this -- they use last-write-wins.
   333→      expectedSequenceNumber: params.sequenceNumber.toString(),
   334→    },
   335→    newSequenceNumber: newSeq,
   336→  };
   337→}
   338→
   339→/**
   340→ * Rename a folder within its parent.
   341→ *
   342→ * Updates the folder entry's name in the parent's metadata and publishes
   343→ * an updated IPNS record for the parent folder.
   344→ *
   345→ * @param params.folderId - ID of folder to rename
   346→ * @param params.newName - New name for the folder
   347→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder containing this folder
   348→ * @throws Error if folder not found or name collision exists
   349→ */
   350→export async function renameFolder(params: {
   351→  folderId: string;
   352→  newName: string;
   353→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   354→}): Promise<void> {
   355→  // 1. Find folder entry in parent's children
   356→  const children = [...params.parentFolderState.children];
   357→  const folderIndex = children.findIndex((c) => c.type === 'folder' && c.id === params.folderId);
   358→
   359→  if (folderIndex === -1) throw new Error('Folder not found');
   360→
   361→  // 2. Check for name collision
   362→  const nameExists = children.some((c) => c.name === params.newName && c.id !== params.folderId);
   363→  if (nameExists) throw new Error('An item with this name already exists');
   364→
   365→  // 3. Update name and modifiedAt
   366→  const folder = children[folderIndex] as FolderEntry;
   367→  children[folderIndex] = {
   368→    ...folder,
   369→    name: params.newName,
   370→    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   371→  };
   372→
   373→  // 4. Update parent folder metadata and publish
   374→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   375→    folderId: params.parentFolderState.id,
   376→    children,
   377→    folderKey: params.parentFolderState.folderKey,
   378→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.parentFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   379→    ipnsName: params.parentFolderState.ipnsName,
   380→    sequenceNumber: params.parentFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   381→  });
   382→}
   383→
   384→/**
   385→ * Delete a folder and all its contents recursively.
   386→ *
   387→ * For v2 metadata: collects fileMetaIpnsName values from FilePointer children
   388→ * (not CIDs, since CIDs live in per-file IPNS records, not folder metadata).
   389→ * Removes the folder from the parent's metadata, publishes the update.
   390→ * Returns the collected file IPNS names so the caller can resolve them for
   391→ * CID unpinning and TEE unenrollment.
   392→ *
   393→ * @param params.folderId - ID of folder to delete
   394→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder containing this folder
   395→ * @param params.getFolderState - Function to get folder state by ID (for recursion)
   396→ * @param params.unpinCid - Function to unpin a CID from IPFS
   397→ * @throws Error if folder not found
   398→ */
   399→export async function deleteFolder(params: {
   400→  folderId: string;
   401→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   402→  getFolderState: (id: string) => FolderNode | undefined;
   403→  unpinCid: (cid: string) => Promise<void>;
   404→}): Promise<string[]> {
   405→  // 1. Find folder in parent's children
   406→  const children = [...params.parentFolderState.children];
   407→  const folderIndex = children.findIndex((c) => c.type === 'folder' && c.id === params.folderId);
   408→
   409→  if (folderIndex === -1) throw new Error('Folder not found');
   410→
   411→  // 2. Recursively collect file IPNS names for cleanup
   412→  // In v2, file children are FilePointers with fileMetaIpnsName (no inline CID).
   413→  // The caller must resolve each fileMetaIpnsName to get the CID for unpinning.
   414→  const fileIpnsNames: string[] = [];
   415→  const collectFileIpnsNames = (folderId: string) => {
   416→    const folder = params.getFolderState(folderId);
   417→    if (!folder) return;
   418→
   419→    for (const child of folder.children) {
   420→      if (child.type === 'file') {
   421→        const filePointer = child as FilePointer;
   422→        if (filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName) {
   423→          fileIpnsNames.push(filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName);
   424→        }
   425→      } else if (child.type === 'folder') {
   426→        collectFileIpnsNames(child.id);
   427→      }
   428→    }
   429→  };
   430→
   431→  collectFileIpnsNames(params.folderId);
   432→
   433→  // 3. Remove folder from parent's children
   434→  children.splice(folderIndex, 1);
   435→
   436→  // 4. Update parent folder metadata and publish
   437→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   438→    folderId: params.parentFolderState.id,
   439→    children,
   440→    folderKey: params.parentFolderState.folderKey,
   441→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.parentFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   442→    ipnsName: params.parentFolderState.ipnsName,
   443→    sequenceNumber: params.parentFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   444→  });
   445→
   446→  // 5. File CID unpinning and TEE unenrollment deferred to caller
   447→  // The caller (useFolder hook) resolves fileMetaIpnsName -> CID for unpinning.
   448→  // TODO: Phase 14 should add batch unenrollment for orphaned file IPNS records.
   449→
   450→  return fileIpnsNames;
   451→}
   452→
   453→/**
   454→ * Delete a file from its parent folder (v2).
   455→ *
   456→ * Removes the FilePointer from the parent's metadata, publishes the update.
   457→ * Returns the fileMetaIpnsName so the caller can resolve it for CID unpinning
   458→ * and TEE unenrollment.
   459→ *
   460→ * Note: This handles folder metadata update. File CID unpinning is handled
   461→ * by the caller after resolving the file metadata.
   462→ *
   463→ * @param params.fileId - ID of file to delete
   464→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder containing this file
   465→ * @returns fileMetaIpnsName for caller to handle cleanup
   466→ * @throws Error if file not found
   467→ */
   468→export async function deleteFileFromFolder(params: {
   469→  fileId: string;
   470→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   471→}): Promise<{ fileMetaIpnsName: string | undefined }> {
   472→  // 1. Find file in parent's children
   473→  const children = [...params.parentFolderState.children];
   474→  const fileIndex = children.findIndex((c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === params.fileId);
   475→
   476→  if (fileIndex === -1) throw new Error('File not found');
   477→
   478→  // 2. Get fileMetaIpnsName for cleanup
   479→  const filePointer = children[fileIndex] as FilePointer;
   480→  const fileMetaIpnsName = filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName;
   481→
   482→  // 3. Remove file from parent's children
   483→  children.splice(fileIndex, 1);
   484→
   485→  // 4. Update parent folder metadata and publish
   486→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   487→    folderId: params.parentFolderState.id,
   488→    children,
   489→    folderKey: params.parentFolderState.folderKey,
   490→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.parentFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   491→    ipnsName: params.parentFolderState.ipnsName,
   492→    sequenceNumber: params.parentFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   493→  });
   494→
   495→  // 5. TEE unenrollment: no API endpoint available yet.
   496→  // TODO: Phase 14 should expose unenrollIpns via REST API.
   497→  if (fileMetaIpnsName) {
   498→    console.warn(
   499→      `File IPNS record ${fileMetaIpnsName} orphaned after deletion. TEE unenrollment deferred to Phase 14.`
   500→    );
   501→  }
   502→
   503→  return { fileMetaIpnsName };
   504→}
   505→
   506→/**
   507→ * Add a file to a folder after successful upload (v2).
   508→ *
   509→ * Creates a FilePointer from the file's IPNS record and adds it to the folder's
   510→ * children array. Publishes both the file IPNS record and the updated folder
   511→ * metadata via a single batch API call.
   512→ *
   513→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder to add file to
   514→ * @param params.fileId - Pre-generated file UUID
   515→ * @param params.name - Original file name
   516→ * @param params.fileIpnsRecord - File IPNS record payload from createFileMetadata
   517→ * @returns Created file pointer and new sequence number
   518→ * @throws Error if name collision exists
   519→ */
   520→export async function addFileToFolder(params: {
   521→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   522→  fileId: string;
   523→  name: string;
   524→  fileIpnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
   525→  ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: string;
   526→}): Promise<{ filePointer: FilePointer; newSequenceNumber: bigint }> {
   527→  // 1. Check for name collision
   528→  const nameExists = params.parentFolderState.children.some((c) => c.name === params.name);
   529→  if (nameExists) {
   530→    throw new Error('A file with this name already exists');
   531→  }
   532→
   533→  // 2. Create FilePointer (slim reference to per-file IPNS record + wrapped key)
   534→  const now = Date.now();
   535→  const filePointer: FilePointer = {
   536→    type: 'file',
   537→    id: params.fileId,
   538→    name: params.name,
   539→    fileMetaIpnsName: params.fileIpnsRecord.ipnsName,
   540→    ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: params.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   541→    createdAt: now,
   542→    modifiedAt: now,
   543→  };
   544→
   545→  // 3. Add FilePointer to parent's children
   546→  const children: FolderChild[] = [...params.parentFolderState.children, filePointer];
   547→
   548→  // 4. Build folder IPNS record for batch publish
   549→  const folderResult = await buildFolderIpnsRecord({
   550→    children,
   551→    folderKey: params.parentFolderState.folderKey,
   552→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.parentFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   553→    ipnsName: params.parentFolderState.ipnsName,
   554→    sequenceNumber: params.parentFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   555→  });
   556→
   557→  // 5. Batch publish: file IPNS record + folder IPNS record
   558→  const publishResult = await batchPublishIpnsRecords([
   559→    { ...params.fileIpnsRecord, recordType: 'file' },
   560→    folderResult.record,
   561→  ]);
   562→
   563→  if (publishResult.totalFailed > 0) {
   564→    console.error(
   565→      `Batch publish partial failure: ${publishResult.totalFailed}/${publishResult.totalFailed + publishResult.totalSucceeded} records failed`
   566→    );
   567→    throw new Error('Failed to publish one or more IPNS records');
   568→  }
   569→
   570→  return { filePointer, newSequenceNumber: folderResult.newSequenceNumber };
   571→}
   572→
   573→/**
   574→ * Add multiple files to a folder after successful upload (v2 batch).
   575→ *
   576→ * Creates FilePointer objects for all files, checks for name collisions,
   577→ * then publishes all N file IPNS records + 1 folder IPNS record via a
   578→ * single batch API call.
   579→ *
   580→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder to add files to
   581→ * @param params.files - Array of file data with pre-created IPNS records
   582→ * @returns Created file pointers and new sequence number
   583→ * @throws Error if any name collision exists
   584→ */
   585→export async function addFilesToFolder(params: {
   586→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   587→  files: Array<{
   588→    fileId: string;
   589→    name: string;
   590→    fileIpnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
   591→    ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: string;
   592→  }>;
   593→}): Promise<{ filePointers: FilePointer[]; newSequenceNumber: bigint }> {
   594→  // 1. Build a set of existing child names for collision detection
   595→  const existingNames = new Set(params.parentFolderState.children.map((c) => c.name));
   596→
   597→  // 2. Create FilePointer for each file, checking collisions
   598→  const filePointers: FilePointer[] = [];
   599→  const now = Date.now();
   600→
   601→  for (const file of params.files) {
   602→    if (existingNames.has(file.name)) {
   603→      throw new Error(`A file with name "${file.name}" already exists`);
   604→    }
   605→    existingNames.add(file.name);
   606→
   607→    const filePointer: FilePointer = {
   608→      type: 'file',
   609→      id: file.fileId,
   610→      name: file.name,
   611→      fileMetaIpnsName: file.fileIpnsRecord.ipnsName,
   612→      ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: file.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   613→      createdAt: now,
   614→      modifiedAt: now,
   615→    };
   616→    filePointers.push(filePointer);
   617→  }
   618→
   619→  // 3. Build updated children array
   620→  const children: FolderChild[] = [...params.parentFolderState.children, ...filePointers];
   621→
   622→  // 4. Build folder IPNS record
   623→  const folderResult = await buildFolderIpnsRecord({
   624→    children,
   625→    folderKey: params.parentFolderState.folderKey,
   626→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.parentFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   627→    ipnsName: params.parentFolderState.ipnsName,
   628→    sequenceNumber: params.parentFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   629→  });
   630→
   631→  // 5. Batch publish: all N file IPNS records + 1 folder IPNS record (single API call)
   632→  const allRecords = [
   633→    ...params.files.map((f) => ({ ...f.fileIpnsRecord, recordType: 'file' as const })),
   634→    folderResult.record,
   635→  ];
   636→  const publishResult = await batchPublishIpnsRecords(allRecords);
   637→
   638→  if (publishResult.totalFailed > 0) {
   639→    console.error(
   640→      `Batch publish partial failure: ${publishResult.totalFailed}/${publishResult.totalFailed + publishResult.totalSucceeded} records failed`
   641→    );
   642→    throw new Error('Failed to publish one or more IPNS records');
   643→  }
   644→
   645→  return { filePointers, newSequenceNumber: folderResult.newSequenceNumber };
   646→}
   647→
   648→/**
   649→ * Replace file content in v2 metadata (content update).
   650→ *
   651→ * THIS IS THE PRIMARY BENEFIT OF PER-FILE IPNS: content updates publish
   652→ * ONLY the file's IPNS record. The folder metadata is NOT touched because
   653→ * the FilePointer still points to the same fileMetaIpnsName, which now
   654→ * resolves to the updated content.
   655→ *
   656→ * @param params.fileId - ID of file to update
   657→ * @param params.fileIpnsRecord - Updated file IPNS record from updateFileMetadata
   658→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder (used only to find the FilePointer)
   659→ * @returns Void -- no folder metadata update needed
   660→ * @throws Error if file not found
   661→ */
   662→export async function replaceFileInFolder(params: {
   663→  fileId: string;
   664→  fileIpnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
   665→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   666→}): Promise<void> {
   667→  // 1. Verify file exists in parent's children
   668→  const fileExists = params.parentFolderState.children.some(
   669→    (c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === params.fileId
   670→  );
   671→
   672→  if (!fileExists) throw new Error('File not found');
   673→
   674→  // 2. Publish ONLY the file IPNS record (folder metadata untouched!)
   675→  // This is the key optimization: content update does NOT modify folder metadata.
   676→  const publishResult = await batchPublishIpnsRecords([
   677→    { ...params.fileIpnsRecord, recordType: 'file' },
   678→  ]);
   679→
   680→  if (publishResult.totalFailed > 0) {
   681→    throw new Error('Failed to publish file IPNS record');
   682→  }
   683→}
   684→
   685→/**
   686→ * Calculate the maximum depth of a folder's subtree.
   687→ *
   688→ * Used to check if moving a folder would exceed the depth limit.
   689→ *
   690→ * @param folderId - Folder ID to calculate subtree depth for
   691→ * @param folders - Current folder tree
   692→ * @returns Maximum depth in the subtree (0 if folder has no children)
   693→ */
   694→export function calculateSubtreeDepth(
   695→  folderId: string,
   696→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
   697→): number {
   698→  const folder = folders[folderId];
   699→  if (!folder) return 0;
   700→
   701→  let maxChildDepth = 0;
   702→  for (const child of folder.children) {
   703→    if (child.type === 'folder') {
   704→      const childDepth = 1 + calculateSubtreeDepth(child.id, folders);
   705→      maxChildDepth = Math.max(maxChildDepth, childDepth);
   706→    }
   707→  }
   708→
   709→  return maxChildDepth;
   710→}
   711→
   712→/**
   713→ * Check if a folder is a descendant of a potential ancestor.
   714→ *
   715→ * Used to prevent moving a folder into itself or its descendants.
   716→ *
   717→ * @param folderId - Folder to check
   718→ * @param potentialAncestorId - Potential ancestor folder ID
   719→ * @param folders - Current folder tree
   720→ * @returns true if folderId is a descendant of potentialAncestorId
   721→ */
   722→export function isDescendantOf(
   723→  folderId: string,
   724→  potentialAncestorId: string,
   725→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
   726→): boolean {
   727→  let currentId: string | null = folderId;
   728→
   729→  while (currentId !== null) {
   730→    if (currentId === potentialAncestorId) return true;
   731→    const currentFolder: FolderNode | undefined = folders[currentId];
   732→    if (!currentFolder) break;
   733→    currentId = currentFolder.parentId;
   734→  }
   735→
   736→  return false;
   737→}
   738→
   739→/**
   740→ * Move a folder from one parent to another.
   741→ *
   742→ * Uses add-before-remove pattern to prevent data loss on failure.
   743→ * Validates depth limit and prevents moving folder into itself.
   744→ *
   745→ * @param params.folderId - ID of folder to move
   746→ * @param params.sourceFolderState - Current parent folder
   747→ * @param params.destFolderState - Destination parent folder
   748→ * @param params.folders - Full folder tree (for depth calculation)
   749→ * @throws Error if folder not found, name collision, depth limit exceeded,
   750→ *         or attempting to move folder into itself/descendant
   751→ */
   752→export async function moveFolder(params: {
   753→  folderId: string;
   754→  sourceFolderState: FolderNode;
   755→  destFolderState: FolderNode;
   756→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>;
   757→}): Promise<void> {
   758→  // 1. Find folder in source
   759→  const folder = params.sourceFolderState.children.find(
   760→    (c) => c.type === 'folder' && c.id === params.folderId
   761→  ) as FolderEntry | undefined;
   762→
   763→  if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not found');
   764→
   765→  // 2. Check name collision in destination
   766→  const nameExists = params.destFolderState.children.some((c) => c.name === folder.name);
   767→  if (nameExists) throw new Error('An item with this name already exists in destination');
   768→
   769→  // 3. Check depth limit (20 levels max)
   770→  const destDepth = getDepth(params.destFolderState.id, params.folders);
   771→  const folderSubtreeDepth = calculateSubtreeDepth(params.folderId, params.folders);
   772→  if (destDepth + 1 + folderSubtreeDepth > MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH) {
   773→    throw new Error(`Cannot move: would exceed maximum folder depth of ${MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH}`);
   774→  }
   775→
   776→  // 4. Prevent moving folder into itself or its descendants
   777→  if (isDescendantOf(params.destFolderState.id, params.folderId, params.folders)) {
   778→    throw new Error('Cannot move folder into itself or its subfolder');
   779→  }
   780→
   781→  // 5. ADD to destination FIRST (add-before-remove pattern)
   782→  const destChildren: FolderChild[] = [
   783→    ...params.destFolderState.children,
   784→    {
   785→      ...folder,
   786→      modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   787→    },
   788→  ];
   789→
   790→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   791→    folderId: params.destFolderState.id,
   792→    children: destChildren,
   793→    folderKey: params.destFolderState.folderKey,
   794→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.destFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   795→    ipnsName: params.destFolderState.ipnsName,
   796→    sequenceNumber: params.destFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   797→  });
   798→
   799→  // 6. REMOVE from source AFTER destination confirmed
   800→  const sourceChildren = params.sourceFolderState.children.filter(
   801→    (c) => !(c.type === 'folder' && c.id === params.folderId)
   802→  );
   803→
   804→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   805→    folderId: params.sourceFolderState.id,
   806→    children: sourceChildren,
   807→    folderKey: params.sourceFolderState.folderKey,
   808→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.sourceFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   809→    ipnsName: params.sourceFolderState.ipnsName,
   810→    sequenceNumber: params.sourceFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   811→  });
   812→}
   813→
   814→/**
   815→ * Move a file from one folder to another (v2).
   816→ *
   817→ * Uses add-before-remove pattern to prevent data loss on failure.
   818→ * FilePointer contains fileMetaIpnsName which stays the same across moves --
   819→ * no file IPNS record changes needed, only folder metadata changes.
   820→ *
   821→ * @param params.fileId - ID of file to move
   822→ * @param params.sourceFolderState - Current parent folder
   823→ * @param params.destFolderState - Destination folder
   824→ * @throws Error if file not found or name collision exists
   825→ */
   826→export async function moveFile(params: {
   827→  fileId: string;
   828→  sourceFolderState: FolderNode;
   829→  destFolderState: FolderNode;
   830→}): Promise<void> {
   831→  // 1. Find file in source (v2: FilePointer)
   832→  const file = params.sourceFolderState.children.find(
   833→    (c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === params.fileId
   834→  ) as FilePointer | undefined;
   835→
   836→  if (!file) throw new Error('File not found');
   837→
   838→  // 2. Check name collision in destination
   839→  const nameExists = params.destFolderState.children.some((c) => c.name === file.name);
   840→  if (nameExists) throw new Error('An item with this name already exists in destination');
   841→
   842→  // 3. ADD to destination FIRST
   843→  const destChildren: FolderChild[] = [
   844→    ...params.destFolderState.children,
   845→    {
   846→      ...file,
   847→      modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   848→    },
   849→  ];
   850→
   851→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   852→    folderId: params.destFolderState.id,
   853→    children: destChildren,
   854→    folderKey: params.destFolderState.folderKey,
   855→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.destFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   856→    ipnsName: params.destFolderState.ipnsName,
   857→    sequenceNumber: params.destFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   858→  });
   859→
   860→  // 4. REMOVE from source AFTER
   861→  const sourceChildren = params.sourceFolderState.children.filter(
   862→    (c) => !(c.type === 'file' && c.id === params.fileId)
   863→  );
   864→
   865→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   866→    folderId: params.sourceFolderState.id,
   867→    children: sourceChildren,
   868→    folderKey: params.sourceFolderState.folderKey,
   869→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.sourceFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   870→    ipnsName: params.sourceFolderState.ipnsName,
   871→    sequenceNumber: params.sourceFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   872→  });
   873→}
   874→
   875→/**
   876→ * Rename a file within its parent folder (v2).
   877→ *
   878→ * Updates the FilePointer's name in the parent's metadata and publishes
   879→ * an updated IPNS record for the parent folder.
   880→ * File rename does NOT touch the file's own IPNS record -- only folder metadata.
   881→ *
   882→ * @param params.fileId - ID of file to rename
   883→ * @param params.newName - New name for the file
   884→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder containing this file
   885→ * @throws Error if file not found or name collision exists
   886→ */
   887→export async function renameFile(params: {
   888→  fileId: string;
   889→  newName: string;
   890→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   891→}): Promise<void> {
   892→  const children = [...params.parentFolderState.children];
   893→  const fileIndex = children.findIndex((c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === params.fileId);
   894→
   895→  if (fileIndex === -1) throw new Error('File not found');
   896→
   897→  // Check name collision
   898→  const nameExists = children.some((c) => c.name === params.newName && c.id !== params.fileId);
   899→  if (nameExists) throw new Error('An item with this name already exists');
   900→
   901→  const file = children[fileIndex] as FilePointer;
   902→  children[fileIndex] = {
   903→    ...file,
   904→    name: params.newName,
   905→    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   906→  };
   907→
   908→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   909→    folderId: params.parentFolderState.id,
   910→    children,
   911→    folderKey: params.parentFolderState.folderKey,
   912→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.parentFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   913→    ipnsName: params.parentFolderState.ipnsName,
   914→    sequenceNumber: params.parentFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   915→  });
   916→}
   917→
   918→/**
   919→ * Fetch and decrypt folder metadata from IPFS.
   920→ *
   921→ * Used for sync operations when remote IPNS resolves to a different CID.
   922→ * Fetches the encrypted metadata blob from IPFS and decrypts it with the folder key.
   923→ * @param cid - IPFS CID of the encrypted metadata blob
   924→ * @param folderKey - Decrypted AES-256 folder key
   925→ * @returns Decrypted folder metadata (v2)
   926→ * @throws Error if fetch or decryption fails
   927→ */
   928→export async function fetchAndDecryptMetadata(
   929→  cid: string,
   930→  folderKey: Uint8Array
   931→): Promise<FolderMetadata> {
   932→  // 1. Fetch encrypted metadata blob from IPFS
   933→  const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(cid);
   934→
   935→  // 2. Parse as JSON to get EncryptedFolderMetadata (contains iv and data fields)
   936→  const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
   937→  const encrypted: EncryptedFolderMetadata = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
   938→
   939→  // 3. Decrypt using folder key
   940→  const metadata = await decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, folderKey);
   941→
   942→  return metadata;
   943→}
   944→
   945→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   946→// Lazy key rotation integration
   947→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   948→
   949→/**
   950→ * Check if a folder has pending key rotations and execute rotation if needed.
   951→ *
   952→ * Called before folder modifications to ensure the folderKey is rotated
   953→ * after a share has been revoked. This implements lazy rotation: the key
   954→ * is only rotated when the sharer next modifies the folder.
   955→ *
   956→ * If rotation occurs:
   957→ * 1. A new random folderKey is generated
   958→ * 2. The folder metadata is re-encrypted with the new key
   959→ * 3. The new key is re-wrapped for all remaining (non-revoked) share recipients
   960→ * 4. Revoked share records are hard-deleted
   961→ * 5. The new folderKey is returned for the caller to use
   962→ *
   963→ * If no rotation is needed, the original folderKey is returned unchanged.
   964→ *
   965→ * NOTE: The parent folder's folderKeyEncrypted for this child is NOT updated
   966→ * here. The parent metadata update (re-wrapping the new folderKey with the
   967→ * owner's public key) is handled by the caller since it has access to the
   968→ * parent folder context. The caller must update the parent folder's children
   969→ * array with the new folderKeyEncrypted for this folder entry.
   970→ *
   971→ * @param params.folderNode - The folder node being modified
   972→ * @returns The folderKey to use (new if rotated, original if not)
   973→ */
   974→export async function checkAndRotateIfNeeded(params: {
   975→  folderNode: FolderNode;
   976→}): Promise<{ folderKey: Uint8Array; rotated: boolean }> {
   977→  // Lazy import to avoid circular dependency at module evaluation time
   978→  const { checkPendingRotation, executeLazyRotation } = await import('./share.service');
   979→
   980→  const { folderNode } = params;
   981→
   982→  // 1. Check if any revoked shares are pending rotation for this folder
   983→  const hasPending = await checkPendingRotation(folderNode.ipnsName);
   984→  if (!hasPending) {
   985→    return { folderKey: folderNode.folderKey, rotated: false };
   986→  }
   987→
   988→  const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   989→  if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
   990→    // Cannot rotate without owner's keypair -- skip rotation, use existing key
   991→    console.warn('[share] Cannot perform lazy rotation: no vault keypair available');
   992→    return { folderKey: folderNode.folderKey, rotated: false };
   993→  }
   994→
   995→  // 2. Execute lazy rotation: generates new key, re-wraps for remaining recipients,
   996→  //    hard-deletes revoked share records
   997→  const { newFolderKey } = await executeLazyRotation({
   998→    folderIpnsName: folderNode.ipnsName,
   999→    oldFolderKey: folderNode.folderKey,
  1000→    ownerPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
  1001→  });
  1002→
  1003→  // 3. Re-encrypt folder metadata with the new key
  1004→  //    Resolve current IPNS -> fetch encrypted metadata -> decrypt with old key -> re-encrypt with new key
  1005→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderNode.ipnsName);
  1006→  if (!resolved) {
  1007→    // IPNS resolution failed after rotation was committed (share keys already re-wrapped,
  1008→    // revoked shares hard-deleted). Metadata is still encrypted with the old key.
  1009→    // Throw so the caller doesn't proceed with the new key in an inconsistent state.
  1010→    console.error(
  1011→      `[share] IPNS resolution failed after lazy rotation for ${folderNode.ipnsName}. ` +
  1012→        'Share keys were updated but metadata was not re-encrypted.'
  1013→    );
  1014→    throw new Error(
  1015→      'Key rotation failed: could not resolve folder IPNS for metadata re-encryption'
  1016→    );
  1017→  }
  1018→
  1019→  const currentMetadata = await fetchAndDecryptMetadata(resolved.cid, folderNode.folderKey);
  1020→
  1021→  // Re-encrypt and publish with new key.
  1022→  // If another device published concurrently we get a 409 conflict.
  1023→  // The share key rotation (re-wrap + delete) was already committed, so
  1024→  // we return the new key and defer the metadata re-encryption to the next
  1025→  // sync cycle, when the caller will re-read fresh state and retry.
  1026→  try {
  1027→    await updateFolderMetadata({
  1028→      folderId: folderNode.id,
  1029→      children: currentMetadata.children ?? [],
  1030→      folderKey: newFolderKey,
  1031→      ipnsPrivateKey: folderNode.ipnsPrivateKey,
  1032→      ipnsName: folderNode.ipnsName,
  1033→      sequenceNumber: resolved.sequenceNumber,
  1034→    });
  1035→  } catch (err) {
  1036→    if (isConflictError(err)) {
  1037→      // Key rotation committed (shares re-wrapped, revoked shares deleted).
  1038→      // Metadata publish failed due to concurrent modification. On next sync
  1039→      // the folder will be re-read with fresh state; checkAndRotateIfNeeded
  1040→      // will detect the still-pending rotation and retry re-encryption then.
  1041→      console.warn(
  1042→        `[share] Conflict during lazy rotation metadata publish for ${folderNode.ipnsName}. ` +
  1043→          'Will retry on next sync cycle.'
  1044→      );
  1045→      // Return the new key so the caller can use it for the file being uploaded.
  1046→      return { folderKey: newFolderKey, rotated: true };
  1047→    }
  1048→    throw err; // Re-throw non-conflict errors
  1049→  }
  1050→
  1051→  // 4. Re-wrap new folderKey in parent metadata
  1052→  //    The parent needs its child's folderKeyEncrypted updated.
  1053→  //    This is done by the caller since they have access to the parent folder.
  1054→  //    We just return the new key and the rotated flag.
  1055→
  1056→  return { folderKey: newFolderKey, rotated: true };
  1057→}
  1058→
```

```
    28→  constructor(

    35→
    36→  /**

    38→   * and track the folder in the database for TEE republishing
    39→   */
    40→  async publishRecord(userId: string, dto: PublishIpnsDto): Promise<PublishIpnsResponseDto> {
    41→    // Validate base64 record
    42→    let recordBytes: Uint8Array;
    43→    try {
    44→      recordBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(dto.record), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
    45→    } catch {
    46→      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid base64-encoded record');
    47→    }
    48→
    49→    // Save to DB first so resolve always has a fallback, even if delegated
    50→    // routing fails (e.g. rate-limited, network error, DHT propagation delay).
    51→    const folder = await this.upsertFolderIpns(
    52→      userId,
    53→      dto.ipnsName,

    57→      'folder',
    58→      dto.expectedSequenceNumber
    59→    );
    60→
    61→    // Publish to delegated routing API (best-effort — DB is the reliable source)
    62→    try {
    63→      await this.delegatedRouting.publish(dto.ipnsName, recordBytes);
    64→    } catch (error) {
    65→      this.logger.warn(

    67→      );
    68→    }
    69→
    70→    return {

    76→
    77→  /**
    78→   * Batch publish multiple IPNS records with concurrency-limited processing.
    79→   * Supports partial success: individual record failures do not fail the batch.
    80→   * Processes up to 10 records concurrently.
    81→   */
    82→  async publishBatch(
    83→    userId: string,
    84→    dto: BatchPublishIpnsDto
    85→  ): Promise<BatchPublishIpnsResponseDto> {
    86→    const results: PublishIpnsResponseDto[] = [];
    87→    let totalSucceeded = 0;
    88→    let totalFailed = 0;
    89→
    90→    const CONCURRENCY = 10;
    91→
    92→    // Process records in batches of CONCURRENCY
    93→    for (let i = 0; i < dto.records.length; i += CONCURRENCY) {
    94→      const batch = dto.records.slice(i, i + CONCURRENCY);
    95→
    96→      const settled = await Promise.allSettled(
    97→        batch.map(async (entry) => {
    98→          // Validate base64 record
    99→          let recordBytes: Uint8Array;
   100→          try {
   101→            recordBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(entry.record), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   102→          } catch {
   103→            throw new BadRequestException(`Invalid base64-encoded record for ${entry.ipnsName}`);
   104→          }
   105→
   106→          // Save to DB first so resolve always has a fallback
   107→          const folder = await this.upsertFolderIpns(
   108→            userId,
   109→            entry.ipnsName,
   110→            entry.metadataCid,
   111→            entry.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   112→            entry.keyEpoch,
   113→            entry.recordType ?? 'folder',
   114→            entry.expectedSequenceNumber
   115→          );
   116→
   117→          // Publish to delegated routing (best-effort)
   118→          try {
   119→            await this.delegatedRouting.publish(entry.ipnsName, recordBytes);
   120→          } catch (error) {
   121→            this.logger.warn(
   122→              `Delegated routing publish failed for ${entry.ipnsName}, DB record saved: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
   123→            );
   124→          }
   125→
   126→          return {
   127→            success: true,
   128→            ipnsName: entry.ipnsName,
   129→            sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber,
   130→          } as PublishIpnsResponseDto;
   131→        })
   132→      );
   133→
   134→      for (let j = 0; j < settled.length; j++) {
   135→        const result = settled[j];
   136→        if (result.status === 'fulfilled') {
   137→          results.push(result.value);
   138→          totalSucceeded++;
   139→        } else {
   140→          const reason = result.reason;
   141→          // If any record (especially a folder record) has a conflict, fail the entire batch
   142→          if (reason instanceof ConflictException) {
   143→            throw reason;
   144→          }
   145→          const ipnsName = batch[j]?.ipnsName ?? 'unknown';
   146→          this.logger.warn(
   147→            `Batch publish failed for ${ipnsName}: ${reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : String(reason)}`
   148→          );
   149→          results.push({
   150→            success: false,
   151→            ipnsName,
   152→            sequenceNumber: '0',
   153→          });
   154→          totalFailed++;
   155→        }
   156→      }
   157→    }
   158→
   159→    return { results, totalSucceeded, totalFailed };
   160→  }
   161→
   162→  /**
   163→   * Create or update a folder/file IPNS entry.
   164→   * Handles both folder metadata and per-file metadata IPNS records.
   165→   */
   166→  private async upsertFolderIpns(
   167→    userId: string,
   168→    ipnsName: string,
   169→    metadataCid: string,
   170→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string,
   171→    keyEpoch?: number,
   172→    recordType: 'folder' | 'file' = 'folder',
   173→    expectedSequenceNumber?: string
   174→  ): Promise<FolderIpns> {
   175→    const existing = await this.getFolderIpns(userId, ipnsName);
   176→
   177→    // Conflict detection: when expectedSequenceNumber is provided, verify it matches
   178→    if (existing && expectedSequenceNumber !== undefined) {
   179→      const expected = BigInt(expectedSequenceNumber);
   180→      const current = BigInt(existing.sequenceNumber);
   181→      if (expected !== current) {
   182→        throw new ConflictException({
   183→          statusCode: 409,
   184→          message: 'Sequence number mismatch: folder was modified by another device',
   185→          currentSequenceNumber: existing.sequenceNumber,
   186→          expectedSequenceNumber,
   187→        });
   188→      }
   189→    }
   190→
   191→    if (existing) {
   192→      // Update existing entry
   193→      existing.latestCid = metadataCid;
   194→      existing.sequenceNumber = (BigInt(existing.sequenceNumber) + 1n).toString();
   195→      existing.recordType = recordType;
   196→      existing.updatedAt = new Date();
   197→
   198→      // Only update encrypted key if provided (e.g., on key rotation)
   199→      if (encryptedIpnsPrivateKey && keyEpoch !== undefined) {
   200→        existing.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey = Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex');
   201→        existing.keyEpoch = keyEpoch;
   202→      }
   203→
   204→      const saved = await this.folderIpnsRepository.save(existing);
   205→
   206→      // Auto-enroll for TEE republishing when encrypted key is provided
   207→      if (encryptedIpnsPrivateKey && keyEpoch !== undefined) {
   208→        this.republishService
   209→          .enrollFolder(
   210→            userId,
   211→            ipnsName,
   212→            Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex'),
   213→            keyEpoch,
   214→            metadataCid,
   215→            saved.sequenceNumber
   216→          )
   217→          .catch((err) =>
   218→            this.logger.warn(
   219→              `Failed to enroll ${recordType} ${ipnsName} for republishing: ${err.message}`
   220→            )
   221→          );
   222→      }
   223→
   224→      return saved;
   225→    }
   226→
   227→    // Create new entry
   228→    const folder = this.folderIpnsRepository.create({
   229→      userId,
   230→      ipnsName,
   231→      latestCid: metadataCid,
   232→      sequenceNumber: '0',
   233→      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: encryptedIpnsPrivateKey
   234→        ? Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex')
   235→        : null,
   236→      keyEpoch: keyEpoch ?? null,
   237→      isRoot: false, // Root folder is tracked in Vault entity
   238→      recordType,
   239→    });
   240→
   241→    const saved = await this.folderIpnsRepository.save(folder);
   242→
   243→    // Auto-enroll for TEE republishing when encrypted key is provided
   244→    if (encryptedIpnsPrivateKey && keyEpoch !== undefined) {
   245→      this.republishService
   246→        .enrollFolder(
   247→          userId,
   248→          ipnsName,
   249→          Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex'),
   250→          keyEpoch,
   251→          metadataCid,
   252→          saved.sequenceNumber
   253→        )
   254→        .catch((err) =>
   255→          this.logger.warn(
   256→            `Failed to enroll ${recordType} ${ipnsName} for republishing: ${err.message}`
   257→          )
   258→        );
   259→    }
   260→
   261→    return saved;
   262→  }
   263→
   264→  /**
   265→   * Get a folder IPNS entry by user and IPNS name
   266→   */
   267→  async getFolderIpns(userId: string, ipnsName: string): Promise<FolderIpns | null> {
   268→    return this.folderIpnsRepository.findOne({
   269→      where: { userId, ipnsName },
   270→    });
   271→  }
   272→
   273→  /**
   274→   * Get all folder IPNS entries for a user (for TEE republishing)
   275→   */
   276→  async getAllFolderIpns(userId: string): Promise<FolderIpns[]> {
   277→    return this.folderIpnsRepository.find({
   278→      where: { userId },
   279→      order: { createdAt: 'ASC' },
   280→    });
   281→  }
   282→
   283→  /**
   284→   * Resolve an IPNS name to its current CID via delegated routing,
   285→   * falling back to the DB-cached CID when delegated routing is unavailable
   286→   * or when the record is not found in the DHT.
   287→   * Returns null if the IPNS name is not found anywhere (404)
   288→   */
   289→  async resolveRecord(ipnsName: string): Promise<{
   290→    cid: string;
   291→    sequenceNumber: string;
   292→    signatureV2?: string;
   293→    data?: string;
   294→    pubKey?: string;
   295→  } | null> {
   296→    let result: {
   297→      cid: string;
   298→      sequenceNumber: string;
   299→      signatureV2?: string;
   300→      data?: string;
   301→      pubKey?: string;
   302→    } | null = null;
   303→
   304→    try {
   305→      const recordBytes = await this.delegatedRouting.resolve(ipnsName);
   306→      if (recordBytes) {
   307→        result = this.parseIpnsRecordBytes(recordBytes);
   308→        this.logger.debug(`IPNS name resolved successfully: ${ipnsName} -> ${result.cid}`);
   309→      }
   310→    } catch (error) {
   311→      // Fall back to DB cache on BAD_GATEWAY (delegated routing failures)
   312→      if (error instanceof HttpException && error.getStatus() === HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY) {
   313→        this.logger.warn(`Delegated routing failed for ${ipnsName}, falling back to DB cache`);
   314→      } else {
   315→        throw error;
   316→      }
   317→    }
   318→
   319→    // Always check DB cache — it's written synchronously during publish
   320→    // and may be ahead of the network (delegated routing can serve stale records)
   321→    const cached = await this.folderIpnsRepository.findOne({
   322→      where: { ipnsName },
   323→    });
   324→
   325→    if (result && cached?.latestCid) {
   326→      // Both sources available — prefer the one with the higher sequence number
   327→      const networkSeq = BigInt(result.sequenceNumber);
   328→      const dbSeq = BigInt(cached.sequenceNumber);
   329→      if (dbSeq > networkSeq) {
   330→        this.logger.log(
   331→          `DB cache has newer sequence (${dbSeq} > ${networkSeq}) for ${ipnsName}, using DB: ${cached.latestCid}`
   332→        );
   333→        return { cid: cached.latestCid, sequenceNumber: cached.sequenceNumber };
   334→      }
   335→      return result;
   336→    }
   337→
   338→    if (result) {
   339→      return result;
   340→    }
   341→
   342→    // Delegated routing returned null (404) or threw BAD_GATEWAY — try DB cache
   343→    if (cached?.latestCid) {
   344→      this.logger.log(`Resolved ${ipnsName} from DB cache: ${cached.latestCid}`);
   345→      return { cid: cached.latestCid, sequenceNumber: cached.sequenceNumber };
   346→    }
   347→
   348→    return null;
   349→  }
   350→
   351→  /**
   352→   * Parse an IPNS record to extract CID and sequence number
   353→   * Uses inline protobuf decoder — no external dependencies
   354→   */
   355→  private parseIpnsRecordBytes(recordBytes: Uint8Array): {
   356→    cid: string;
   357→    sequenceNumber: string;
   358→    signatureV2?: string;
   359→    data?: string;
   360→    pubKey?: string;
   361→  } {
   362→    try {
   363→      const record = parseIpnsRecord(recordBytes);
   364→
   365→      // Extract CID from the Value field (format: /ipfs/<cid>)
   366→      const valuePath = record.value;
   367→      const cidMatch = valuePath.match(/\/ipfs\/([a-zA-Z0-9]+)/);
   368→      if (!cidMatch) {
   369→        this.logger.error('Failed to extract CID from IPNS record value');
   370→        throw new HttpException('Invalid IPNS record format', HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY);
   371→      }
   372→
   373→      const cid = cidMatch[1];
   374→      const sequenceNumber = String(record.sequence ?? 0n);
   375→
   376→      // Base64-encode signature fields if present
   377→      const signatureV2 = record.signatureV2
   378→        ? Buffer.from(record.signatureV2).toString('base64')
   379→        : undefined;
   380→      const data = record.data ? Buffer.from(record.data).toString('base64') : undefined;
   381→      const pubKey = record.pubKey ? Buffer.from(record.pubKey).toString('base64') : undefined;
   382→
   383→      this.logger.debug(`Parsed IPNS record: cid=${cid}, sequenceNumber=${sequenceNumber}`);
   384→      return { cid, sequenceNumber, signatureV2, data, pubKey };
   385→    } catch (error) {
   386→      if (error instanceof HttpException) {
   387→        throw error;
   388→      }
   389→      this.logger.error(`Failed to parse IPNS record: ${error}`);
   390→      throw new HttpException('Invalid IPNS record format', HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY);
   391→    }
   392→  }
   393→}
   394→
```

```
     1→import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
     2→import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';
     3→import type { FolderNode } from '../stores/folder.store';
     4→import * as folderService from '../services/folder.service';
     5→import { resolveIpnsRecord } from '../services/ipns.service';
     6→
     7→/**
     8→ * Re-sync a specific folder after a 409 conflict.
     9→ *
    10→ * Resolves the folder's IPNS name to get fresh CID + sequenceNumber,
    11→ * fetches and decrypts the metadata, and updates the folder store.
    12→ */
    13→export async function resyncFolder(folderIpnsName: string, folderId: string): Promise<void> {
    14→  const store = useFolderStore.getState();
    15→  const folderNode = store.folders[folderId];
    16→  if (!folderNode) return;
    17→
    18→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderIpnsName);
    19→  if (!resolved) return;
    20→
    21→  const remoteMetadata = await folderService.fetchAndDecryptMetadata(
    22→    resolved.cid,
    23→    folderNode.folderKey
    24→  );
    25→
    26→  store.updateFolderChildren(folderId, remoteMetadata.children ?? []);
    27→  store.updateFolderSequence(folderId, resolved.sequenceNumber);
    28→}
    29→
    30→/** Maximum folder nesting depth per FOLD-03 */
    31→export const MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH = 20;
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * State for folder operations.
    35→ */
    36→export type FolderOperationState = {
    37→  isLoading: boolean;
    38→  error: string | null;
    39→};
    40→
    41→/**
    42→ * Get the root folder state from vault and folder stores.
    43→ * Root folder uses vault keys directly.
    44→ */
    45→export function getRootFolderState(
    46→  vaultStore: ReturnType<typeof useVaultStore.getState>,
    47→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
    48→): FolderNode | null {
    49→  if (!vaultStore.rootFolderKey || !vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair || !vaultStore.rootIpnsName) {
    50→    return null;
    51→  }
    52→
    53→  // If we have an explicit root folder in the tree, use it
    54→  const existingRoot = folders['root'];
    55→  if (existingRoot) return existingRoot;
    56→
    57→  // Otherwise construct from vault state
    58→  return {
    59→    id: 'root',
    60→    name: 'My Vault',
    61→    ipnsName: vaultStore.rootIpnsName,
    62→    parentId: null,
    63→    children: [],
    64→    isLoaded: false,
    65→    isLoading: false,
    66→    sequenceNumber: 0n,
    67→    folderKey: vaultStore.rootFolderKey,
    68→    ipnsPrivateKey: vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey,
    69→  };
    70→}
    71→
    72→/**
    73→ * Calculate folder depth by walking up the tree.
    74→ */
    75→export function calculateFolderDepth(
    76→  folderId: string,
    77→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
    78→): number {
    79→  return folderService.getDepth(folderId, folders);
    80→}
    81→
```

```
     1→/**
     2→ * IPNS Service - Record creation, publishing, and resolution
     3→ *
     4→ * Creates IPNS records locally using @cipherbox/crypto and publishes
     5→ * via the backend API relay to delegated-ipfs.dev.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import {
     9→  createIpnsRecord,
    10→  marshalIpnsRecord,
    11→  verifyEd25519,
    12→  IPNS_SIGNATURE_PREFIX,
    13→  concatBytes,
    14→} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    15→import {
    16→  ipnsControllerPublishRecord,
    17→  ipnsControllerPublishBatch,
    18→  ipnsControllerResolveRecord,
    19→} from '../api/ipns/ipns';
    20→import type { PublishIpnsEntryDtoRecordType } from '../api/models/publishIpnsEntryDtoRecordType';
    21→
    22→/**
    23→ * Create an IPNS record locally and publish via backend.
    24→ *
    25→ * The record is signed locally using the Ed25519 private key, then
    26→ * the backend relays it to the IPFS network via delegated routing.
    27→ *
    28→ * @param params.ipnsPrivateKey - Ed25519 private key (64 bytes in libp2p format)
    29→ * @param params.ipnsName - IPNS name (k51.../bafzaa... format)
    30→ * @param params.metadataCid - CID of the encrypted metadata blob
    31→ * @param params.sequenceNumber - Current sequence number (will be incremented before publish)
    32→ * @param params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey - Hex ECIES-wrapped key for TEE (first publish only)
    33→ * @param params.keyEpoch - TEE key epoch (required with encryptedIpnsPrivateKey)
    34→ * @param params.expectedSequenceNumber - Pre-increment sequence number for conflict detection (folder records only)
    35→ */
    36→export async function createAndPublishIpnsRecord(params: {
    37→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
    38→  ipnsName: string;
    39→  metadataCid: string;
    40→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
    41→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
    42→  keyEpoch?: number;
    43→  expectedSequenceNumber?: string;
    44→}): Promise<{ success: boolean; sequenceNumber: bigint }> {
    45→  // 1. Create IPNS record pointing to /ipfs/{metadataCid}
    46→  // 24 hour lifetime (will be republished by TEE every 3 hours)
    47→  const record = await createIpnsRecord(
    48→    params.ipnsPrivateKey,
    49→    `/ipfs/${params.metadataCid}`,
    50→    params.sequenceNumber,
    51→    24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 // 24 hours in ms
    52→  );
    53→
    54→  // 2. Marshal to bytes for transport
    55→  const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
    56→
    57→  // 3. Base64 encode for API transmission
    58→  const recordBase64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...recordBytes));
    59→
    60→  // 4. Call backend API to relay to IPFS network
    61→  const response = await ipnsControllerPublishRecord({
    62→    ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
    63→    record: recordBase64,
    64→    metadataCid: params.metadataCid,
    65→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
    66→    keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
    67→    expectedSequenceNumber: params.expectedSequenceNumber,
    68→  });
    69→
    70→  return {
    71→    success: response.success,
    72→    sequenceNumber: BigInt(response.sequenceNumber),
    73→  };
    74→}
    75→
    76→/**
    77→ * Batch publish multiple IPNS records in a single API call.
    78→ *
    79→ * Sends all records (folder and/or file) to the batch endpoint,
    80→ * which processes them with concurrency-limited parallelism.
    81→ * Partial success is allowed: individual failures do not fail the batch.
    82→ *
    83→ * @param records - Array of IPNS record payloads to publish
    84→ * @returns Success and failure counts
    85→ */
    86→export async function batchPublishIpnsRecords(
    87→  records: Array<{
    88→    ipnsName: string;
    89→    recordBase64: string;
    90→    metadataCid: string;
    91→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
    92→    keyEpoch?: number;
    93→    recordType?: 'folder' | 'file';
    94→    /** Pre-increment sequence number for conflict detection (folder records only) */
    95→    expectedSequenceNumber?: string;
    96→  }>
    97→): Promise<{ totalSucceeded: number; totalFailed: number }> {
    98→  const response = await ipnsControllerPublishBatch({
    99→    records: records.map((r) => ({
   100→      ipnsName: r.ipnsName,
   101→      record: r.recordBase64,
   102→      metadataCid: r.metadataCid,
   103→      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: r.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   104→      keyEpoch: r.keyEpoch,
   105→      recordType: r.recordType as PublishIpnsEntryDtoRecordType | undefined,
   106→      expectedSequenceNumber: r.expectedSequenceNumber,
   107→    })),
   108→  });
   109→
   110→  return {
   111→    totalSucceeded: response.totalSucceeded,
   112→    totalFailed: response.totalFailed,
   113→  };
   114→}
   115→
   116→/**
   117→ * Verify the Ed25519 signature on an IPNS record.
   118→ * Per IPFS spec, the signature is over "ipns-signature:" + cborData.
   119→ *
   120→ * @param signatureV2 - base64 Ed25519 signature (64 bytes)
   121→ * @param data - base64 CBOR data that was signed
   122→ * @param pubKey - base64 raw Ed25519 public key (32 bytes)
   123→ * @returns true if valid
   124→ */
   125→export async function verifyIpnsSignature(
   126→  signatureV2: string,
   127→  data: string,
   128→  pubKey: string
   129→): Promise<boolean> {
   130→  const sigBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(signatureV2), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   131→  const dataBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(data), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   132→  const pubKeyBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(pubKey), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   133→
   134→  // Per IPFS IPNS spec, signature is over "ipns-signature:" + cborData
   135→  const signedData = concatBytes(IPNS_SIGNATURE_PREFIX, dataBytes);
   136→  return verifyEd25519(sigBytes, signedData, pubKeyBytes);
   137→}
   138→
   139→/**
   140→ * Resolve an IPNS name to its current CID and sequence number.
   141→ *
   142→ * Calls backend API which relays to delegated-ipfs.dev for resolution.
   143→ * When the response includes IPNS signature data (from delegated routing),
   144→ * verifies the Ed25519 signature before trusting the CID.
   145→ *
   146→ * @param ipnsName - IPNS name to resolve (k51.../bafzaa... format)
   147→ * @returns Current CID, sequence number, and signature verification status, or null if not found
   148→ */
   149→export async function resolveIpnsRecord(
   150→  ipnsName: string
   151→): Promise<{ cid: string; sequenceNumber: bigint; signatureVerified: boolean } | null> {
   152→  try {
   153→    const response = await ipnsControllerResolveRecord({ ipnsName });
   154→
   155→    if (!response.success) {
   156→      return null;
   157→    }
   158→
   159→    // Verify IPNS signature if all signature fields are present
   160→    let signatureVerified = false;
   161→    if (response.signatureV2 && response.data && response.pubKey) {
   162→      const valid = await verifyIpnsSignature(response.signatureV2, response.data, response.pubKey);
   163→      if (!valid) {
   164→        throw new Error('IPNS signature verification failed - record may be tampered');
   165→      }
   166→      signatureVerified = true;
   167→    } else {
   168→      console.warn('IPNS resolve returned without signature data, skipping verification');
   169→    }
   170→
   171→    return {
   172→      cid: response.cid,
   173→      sequenceNumber: BigInt(response.sequenceNumber),
   174→      signatureVerified,
   175→    };
   176→  } catch (error) {
   177→    // 404 means IPNS name not found - return null
   178→    // Other errors should propagate (including signature verification failures)
   179→    if (error instanceof Error && (error as Error & { status?: number }).status === 404) {
   180→      return null;
   181→    }
   182→    throw error;
   183→  }
   184→}
   185→
```

```
     1→import { create } from 'zustand';
     2→
     3→type SyncStatus = 'idle' | 'syncing' | 'success' | 'error' | 'conflict';
     4→
     5→type SyncState = {
     6→  // Sync status
     7→  status: SyncStatus;
     8→  lastSyncTime: Date | null;
     9→  syncError: string | null;
    10→
    11→  // Conflict state
    12→  conflictMessage: string | null;
    13→
    14→  // Initial sync tracking
    15→  initialSyncComplete: boolean;
    16→
    17→  // Network status
    18→  isOnline: boolean;
    19→
    20→  // Actions
    21→  startSync: () => void;
    22→  syncSuccess: () => void;
    23→  syncFailure: (error: string) => void;
    24→  setConflict: (message: string) => void;
    25→  clearConflict: () => void;
    26→  completeInitialSync: () => void;
    27→  setOnline: (online: boolean) => void;
    28→  reset: () => void;
    29→};
    30→
    31→/**
    32→ * Sync state store for multi-device sync.
    33→ *
    34→ * Tracks:
    35→ * - Sync status (idle/syncing/success/error/conflict)
    36→ * - Last sync timestamp
    37→ * - Sync errors
    38→ * - Conflict messages (set during 409 re-sync, cleared after retry)
    39→ * - Network online status
    40→ *
    41→ * Used by useSyncPolling hook and SyncIndicator component.
    42→ */
    43→export const useSyncStore = create<SyncState>((set) => ({
    44→  // Initial state
    45→  status: 'idle',
    46→  lastSyncTime: null,
    47→  syncError: null,
    48→  conflictMessage: null,
    49→  initialSyncComplete: false,
    50→  isOnline: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true,
    51→
    52→  // Actions
    53→  startSync: () =>
    54→    set({
    55→      status: 'syncing',
    56→      syncError: null,
    57→    }),
    58→
    59→  syncSuccess: () =>
    60→    set({
    61→      status: 'success',
    62→      lastSyncTime: new Date(),
    63→      syncError: null,
    64→    }),
    65→
    66→  syncFailure: (error) =>
    67→    set({
    68→      status: 'error',
    69→      syncError: error,
    70→    }),
    71→
    72→  setConflict: (message) =>
    73→    set({
    74→      status: 'conflict',
    75→      conflictMessage: message,
    76→    }),
    77→
    78→  clearConflict: () =>
    79→    set({
    80→      conflictMessage: null,
    81→    }),
    82→
    83→  completeInitialSync: () => set({ initialSyncComplete: true }),
    84→
    85→  setOnline: (online) => set({ isOnline: online }),
    86→
    87→  reset: () =>
    88→    set({
    89→      status: 'idle',
    90→      lastSyncTime: null,
    91→      syncError: null,
    92→      conflictMessage: null,
    93→      initialSyncComplete: false,
    94→    }),
    95→}));
    96→
```
