     1→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import { useInterval } from './useInterval';
     3→import { useVisibility } from './useVisibility';
     4→import { useOnlineStatus } from './useOnlineStatus';
     5→import { useSyncStore } from '../stores/sync.store';
     6→import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
     7→
     8→const SYNC_INTERVAL_MS = 30000; // 30 seconds per CONTEXT.md
     9→
    10→/**
    11→ * Orchestrates IPNS polling for multi-device sync.
    12→ *
    13→ * Behavior per CONTEXT.md:
    14→ * - Polls every 30s when tab is visible and online
    15→ * - Pauses polling when tab is backgrounded (saves battery)
    16→ * - Polls immediately when tab regains focus (per RESEARCH.md recommendation)
    17→ * - Polls immediately when coming back online
    18→ * - Updates sync store with status (syncing/success/error)
    19→ *
    20→ * @param onSync - Callback to execute sync logic (resolve IPNS, compare, refresh)
    21→ */
    22→export function useSyncPolling(onSync: () => Promise<void>): void {
    23→  const isVisible = useVisibility();
    24→  const isOnline = useOnlineStatus();
    25→  const { startSync, syncSuccess, syncFailure, completeInitialSync, setOnline } = useSyncStore();
    26→  const { rootIpnsName, isNewVault } = useVaultStore();
    27→
    28→  // Track previous states for edge detection
    29→  const prevOnline = useRef(isOnline);
    30→  const prevVisible = useRef(isVisible);
    31→
    32→  // Guard against concurrent sync runs - multiple triggers can occur (interval + visibility + reconnect)
    33→  const isSyncingRef = useRef(false);
    34→
    35→  // Track whether initial sync has been attempted
    36→  const initialSyncFired = useRef(false);
    37→
    38→  // For new vaults, skip the syncing state — there's nothing to sync
    39→  useEffect(() => {
    40→    if (isNewVault && !useSyncStore.getState().initialSyncComplete) {
    41→      completeInitialSync();
    42→    }
    43→  }, [isNewVault, completeInitialSync]);
    44→
    45→  // Keep sync store's isOnline in sync
    46→  useEffect(() => {
    47→    setOnline(isOnline);
    48→  }, [isOnline, setOnline]);
    49→
    50→  // Wrapped sync callback that updates store state with concurrent execution guard
    51→  const doSync = useCallback(async () => {
    52→    if (!rootIpnsName || !isOnline) return;
    53→
    54→    // Skip if already syncing to prevent race conditions
    55→    if (isSyncingRef.current) return;
    56→    isSyncingRef.current = true;
    57→
    58→    startSync();
    59→    try {
    60→      await onSync();
    61→      syncSuccess();
    62→      // Mark initial sync complete only on success (IPNS resolved)
    63→      if (!useSyncStore.getState().initialSyncComplete) {
    64→        completeInitialSync();
    65→      }
    66→    } catch (error) {
    67→      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Sync failed';
    68→      syncFailure(message);
    69→      // Don't mark initial sync complete on failure — will retry
    70→    } finally {
    71→      isSyncingRef.current = false;
    72→    }
    73→  }, [rootIpnsName, isOnline, onSync, startSync, syncSuccess, syncFailure, completeInitialSync]);
    74→
    75→  // Immediate sync on first mount — don't wait for the 30s interval
    76→  // Skip for new vaults: there's no IPNS record to resolve yet
    77→  useEffect(() => {
    78→    if (rootIpnsName && isOnline && !isNewVault && !initialSyncFired.current) {
    79→      initialSyncFired.current = true;
    80→      doSync();
    81→    }
    82→  }, [rootIpnsName, isOnline, isNewVault, doSync]);
    83→
    84→  // Determine polling delay: null pauses, number runs
    85→  // Per CONTEXT.md: pause when backgrounded (Claude's discretion chose pause)
    86→  const pollDelay = isVisible && isOnline ? SYNC_INTERVAL_MS : null;
    87→
    88→  // Regular interval polling
    89→  useInterval(doSync, pollDelay);
    90→
    91→  // Immediate sync on visibility regain (per RESEARCH.md recommendation)
    92→  useEffect(() => {
    93→    if (isVisible && !prevVisible.current && isOnline) {
    94→      doSync();
    95→    }
    96→    prevVisible.current = isVisible;
    97→  }, [isVisible, isOnline, doSync]);
    98→
    99→  // Immediate sync on reconnect (per CONTEXT.md)
   100→  useEffect(() => {
   101→    if (isOnline && !prevOnline.current) {
   102→      doSync();
   103→    }
   104→    prevOnline.current = isOnline;
   105→  }, [isOnline, doSync]);
   106→}
   107→

     1→import { Browser, BrowserContext, Page } from '@playwright/test';
     2→import { loginViaTestEndpoint, TestAuthState, getPublicKeyHex } from './web3auth-helpers';
     3→
     4→/**
     5→ * A fully authenticated test account with its own browser context.
     6→ * Each account has an isolated session (cookies, localStorage, auth state).
     7→ */
     8→export interface TestAccount {
     9→  name: string;
    10→  email: string;
    11→  context: BrowserContext;
    12→  page: Page;
    13→  authState: TestAuthState;
    14→  /** 0x04-prefixed secp256k1 public key (for sharing) */
    15→  publicKey: string;
    16→}
    17→
    18→/**
    19→ * Create a test account with a unique email and authenticated browser context.
    20→ *
    21→ * Uses the test-login endpoint to bypass Web3Auth. Each account gets:
    22→ * - A unique timestamped email for test isolation
    23→ * - Its own BrowserContext (isolated cookies/storage)
    24→ * - A deterministic secp256k1 keypair (derived from email)
    25→ * - A fully initialized vault
    26→ *
    27→ * @param browser - Playwright browser instance
    28→ * @param name - Human-readable name for this account (e.g., "alice")
    29→ * @param runId - Unique identifier for this test run (timestamp)
    30→ */
    31→export async function createTestAccount(
    32→  browser: Browser,
    33→  name: string,
    34→  runId: string
    35→): Promise<TestAccount> {
    36→  const email = `${name}-e2e-${runId}@test.local`;
    37→  const context = await browser.newContext();
    38→  const page = await context.newPage();
    39→
    40→  const authState = await loginViaTestEndpoint(page, email);
    41→  const publicKey = getPublicKeyHex(authState);
    42→
    43→  return { name, email, context, page, authState, publicKey };
    44→}
    45→
    46→/**
    47→ * Create multiple test accounts sequentially.
    48→ * All accounts share the same runId for test isolation.
    49→ */
    50→export async function createTestAccounts(
    51→  browser: Browser,
    52→  names: string[],
    53→  runId?: string
    54→): Promise<TestAccount[]> {
    55→  const id = runId ?? Date.now().toString();
    56→  // Create accounts sequentially to avoid overwhelming the API
    57→  const accounts: TestAccount[] = [];
    58→  for (const name of names) {
    59→    accounts.push(await createTestAccount(browser, name, id));
    60→  }
    61→  return accounts;
    62→}
    63→
    64→/**
    65→ * Close all test account contexts.
    66→ * Call this in afterAll to clean up browser contexts.
    67→ */
    68→export async function closeTestAccounts(accounts: TestAccount[]): Promise<void> {
    69→  for (const account of accounts) {
    70→    await account.context.close();
    71→  }
    72→}
    73→
    74→/**
    75→ * Navigate a test account's page to the shared files view.
    76→ * Always navigates away first to force a component remount and fresh data fetch.
    77→ */
    78→export async function navigateToShared(account: TestAccount): Promise<void> {
    79→  // Navigate away first to ensure the SharedFileBrowser remounts and re-fetches
    80→  const currentUrl = account.page.url();
    81→  if (currentUrl.includes('/shared')) {
    82→    await account.page.evaluate(() => {
    83→      window.location.hash = '#/files';
    84→    });
    85→    await account.page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 30000 });
    86→  }
    87→  await account.page.evaluate(() => {
    88→    window.location.hash = '#/shared';
    89→  });
    90→  await account.page.waitForURL('**/shared', { timeout: 30000 });
    91→}
    92→
    93→/**
    94→ * Navigate a test account's page to their own files.
    95→ * Always navigates away first to force a component remount and fresh data fetch.
    96→ */
    97→export async function navigateToFiles(account: TestAccount): Promise<void> {
    98→  const currentUrl = account.page.url();
    99→  if (currentUrl.includes('/files')) {
   100→    await account.page.evaluate(() => {
   101→      window.location.hash = '#/shared';
   102→    });
   103→    await account.page.waitForURL('**/shared', { timeout: 30000 });
   104→  }
   105→  await account.page.evaluate(() => {
   106→    window.location.hash = '#/files';
   107→  });
   108→  await account.page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 30000 });
   109→}

     1→import { Page } from '@playwright/test';
     2→import {
     3→  initializeVault,
     4→  encryptVaultKeys,
     5→  decryptVaultKeys,
     6→  deriveIpnsName,
     7→  hexToBytes,
     8→  bytesToHex,
     9→} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    10→import * as ed from '@noble/ed25519';
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Test credentials from environment variables.
    14→ * These are loaded from .env locally or GitHub Secrets in CI.
    15→ *
    16→ * With Core Kit, the E2E flow uses CipherBox's own login UI:
    17→ * 1. Enter email in CipherBox email input
    18→ * 2. Backend sends OTP (static OTP in test mode)
    19→ * 3. Enter OTP code
    20→ * 4. CipherBox JWT -> Core Kit loginWithJWT -> backend auth -> vault init
    21→ */
    22→export const TEST_CREDENTIALS = {
    23→  email: process.env.WEB3AUTH_TEST_EMAIL || '',
    24→  otp: process.env.WEB3AUTH_TEST_OTP || '',
    25→};
    26→
    27→/**
    28→ * Serializable auth state for a test account.
    29→ * Arrays are used because Uint8Array can't cross the page.evaluate boundary.
    30→ *
    31→ * @security Contains raw ECDSA private key material (`privateKeyArr`).
    32→ * Never pass this object to console.log(), JSON.stringify(), or any
    33→ * persistent storage — treat it the same as a plaintext private key.
    34→ */
    35→export interface TestAuthState {
    36→  accessToken: string;
    37→  email: string;
    38→  publicKeyHex: string;
    39→  publicKeyArr: number[];
    40→  /** @security Raw ECDSA secp256k1 private key — never log or serialize. */
    41→  privateKeyArr: number[];
    42→  rootFolderKeyArr: number[];
    43→  rootIpnsPublicKeyArr: number[];
    44→  rootIpnsPrivateKeyArr: number[];
    45→  rootIpnsName: string;
    46→  vaultId: string;
    47→}
    48→
    49→/**
    50→ * Cached auth state from test-login endpoint (singleton for backward compat).
    51→ * Used to re-inject state after page reload (since test-login bypasses
    52→ * Core Kit, there's no persistent session to restore from).
    53→ */
    54→let cachedTestAuthState: TestAuthState | null = null;
    55→
    56→/**
    57→ * Performs email login through CipherBox's custom login UI.
    58→ *
    59→ * When TEST_LOGIN_SECRET is set, uses the /auth/test-login endpoint to bypass
    60→ * Core Kit entirely. This decouples E2E tests from Web3Auth infrastructure.
    61→ *
    62→ * Otherwise falls back to the full Core Kit flow through CipherBox's login UI.
    63→ *
    64→ * @param page - Playwright page instance
    65→ * @param email - Email address for login
    66→ * @param otp - Optional OTP code (defaults to TEST_CREDENTIALS.otp)
    67→ */
    68→export async function loginViaEmail(page: Page, email: string, otp?: string): Promise<void> {
    69→  // Use test-login endpoint when available (bypasses Core Kit completely)
    70→  if (process.env.TEST_LOGIN_SECRET) {
    71→    const state = await loginViaTestEndpoint(page, email);
    72→    cachedTestAuthState = state;
    73→    return;
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  const otpCode = otp || TEST_CREDENTIALS.otp;
    77→
    78→  // Navigate to login page
    79→  await page.goto('/');
    80→
    81→  // Step 1: Enter email in CipherBox's own email input
    82→  const emailInput = page.locator('[data-testid="email-input"]');
    83→  await emailInput.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 15000 });
    84→  await emailInput.fill(email);
    85→
    86→  // Step 2: Click [SEND OTP] button
    87→  const sendOtpButton = page.locator('[data-testid="send-otp-button"]');
    88→  await sendOtpButton.click();
    89→
    90→  // Step 3: Wait for OTP input to appear (backend sends OTP, form transitions)
    91→  const otpInput = page.locator('[data-testid="otp-input"]');
    92→  await otpInput.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 15000 });
    93→
    94→  // Step 4: Enter the test OTP code
    95→  await otpInput.fill(otpCode);
    96→
    97→  // Step 5: Click [VERIFY] button
    98→  const verifyButton = page.locator('[data-testid="verify-button"]');
    99→  await verifyButton.click();
   100→
   101→  // Step 6: Wait for login to complete and redirect to files page
   102→  // Core Kit loginWithJWT + backend auth + vault init can take time
   103→  await page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 60000 });
   104→}
   105→
   106→/**
   107→ * Test-login endpoint flow that bypasses Core Kit entirely.
   108→ *
   109→ * 1. Calls POST /auth/test-login -> tokens + deterministic secp256k1 keypair
   110→ * 2. Handles vault init/load using @cipherbox/crypto in Node.js
   111→ * 3. Injects all state into Zustand stores via page.evaluate
   112→ * 4. Navigates to /files
   113→ *
   114→ * Returns the auth state so callers can manage per-account state
   115→ * (e.g., for multi-account sharing tests).
   116→ */
   117→export async function loginViaTestEndpoint(page: Page, email: string): Promise<TestAuthState> {
   118→  const apiBase = process.env.API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:3000';
   119→  const secret = process.env.TEST_LOGIN_SECRET!;
   120→
   121→  // 1. Authenticate via test-login endpoint
   122→  const loginResponse = await page.request.post(`${apiBase}/auth/test-login`, {
   123→    data: { email, secret },
   124→  });
   125→
   126→  if (!loginResponse.ok()) {
   127→    const body = await loginResponse.text();
   128→    throw new Error(`Test login failed (${loginResponse.status()}): ${body}`);
   129→  }
   130→
   131→  const { accessToken, refreshToken, publicKeyHex, privateKeyHex } = await loginResponse.json();
   132→  const publicKey = hexToBytes(publicKeyHex);
   133→  const privateKey = hexToBytes(privateKeyHex);
   134→
   135→  // 2. Handle vault: load existing or initialize new
   136→  const vaultResponse = await page.request.get(`${apiBase}/vault`, {
   137→    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
   138→  });
   139→
   140→  let vaultId: string;
   141→  let rootFolderKey: Uint8Array;
   142→  let rootIpnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   143→  let rootIpnsName: string;
   144→
   145→  if (vaultResponse.ok()) {
   146→    // Existing vault — decrypt keys
   147→    const vault = await vaultResponse.json();
   148→    vaultId = vault.id;
   149→    rootIpnsName = vault.rootIpnsName;
   150→
   151→    const decrypted = await decryptVaultKeys(
   152→      {
   153→        encryptedRootFolderKey: hexToBytes(vault.encryptedRootFolderKey),
   154→        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: hexToBytes(vault.encryptedRootIpnsPrivateKey),
   155→      },
   156→      privateKey
   157→    );
   158→
   159→    rootFolderKey = decrypted.rootFolderKey;
   160→    rootIpnsPrivateKey = decrypted.rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey;
   161→  } else {
   162→    // New user — initialize vault
   163→    const newVault = await initializeVault(privateKey);
   164→    const encrypted = await encryptVaultKeys(newVault, publicKey);
   165→    rootIpnsName = await deriveIpnsName(newVault.rootIpnsKeypair.publicKey);
   166→
   167→    const initResponse = await page.request.post(`${apiBase}/vault/init`, {
   168→      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
   169→      data: {
   170→        ownerPublicKey: publicKeyHex,
   171→        encryptedRootFolderKey: bytesToHex(encrypted.encryptedRootFolderKey),
   172→        encryptedRootIpnsPrivateKey: bytesToHex(encrypted.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey),
   173→        rootIpnsName,
   174→      },
   175→    });
   176→
   177→    if (!initResponse.ok()) {
   178→      const body = await initResponse.text();
   179→      throw new Error(`Vault init failed (${initResponse.status()}): ${body}`);
   180→    }
   181→
   182→    const initResult = await initResponse.json();
   183→    vaultId = initResult.id;
   184→    rootFolderKey = newVault.rootFolderKey;
   185→    rootIpnsPrivateKey = newVault.rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey;
   186→  }
   187→
   188→  // Derive IPNS public key from private key (deterministic Ed25519 derivation)
   189→  const rootIpnsPublicKey = await ed.getPublicKeyAsync(rootIpnsPrivateKey);
   190→
   191→  const authState: TestAuthState = {
   192→    accessToken,
   193→    email,
   194→    publicKeyHex,
   195→    publicKeyArr: Array.from(publicKey),
   196→    privateKeyArr: Array.from(privateKey),
   197→    rootFolderKeyArr: Array.from(rootFolderKey),
   198→    rootIpnsPublicKeyArr: Array.from(rootIpnsPublicKey),
   199→    rootIpnsPrivateKeyArr: Array.from(rootIpnsPrivateKey),
   200→    rootIpnsName,
   201→    vaultId,
   202→  };
   203→
   204→  // 2.5. Set refresh token cookie in the browser context for session persistence
   205→  const url = new URL(apiBase);
   206→  await page.context().addCookies([
   207→    {
   208→      name: 'refresh_token',
   209→      value: refreshToken,
   210→      domain: url.hostname,
   211→      path: '/auth',
   212→      httpOnly: true,
   213→      secure: false, // dev mode
   214→      sameSite: 'Lax',
   215→    },
   216→  ]);
   217→
   218→  // 3. Navigate to app so we can inject state into Zustand stores
   219→  await page.goto('/');
   220→
   221→  await injectAuthState(page, authState);
   222→
   223→  // 5. Navigate to files page via hash router (no full reload — preserves Zustand state)
   224→  await page.evaluate(() => {
   225→    window.location.hash = '#/files';
   226→  });
   227→  await page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 30000 });
   228→
   229→  return authState;
   230→}
   231→
   232→/**
   233→ * Inject auth state into Zustand stores on a page.
   234→ * Works with both the singleton cache (backward compat) and explicit state (multi-account).
   235→ */
   236→export async function injectAuthState(page: Page, state: TestAuthState): Promise<void> {
   237→  // Wait for the app to load and expose stores (dev mode only)
   238→  await page.waitForFunction(
   239→    () => !!(window as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).__ZUSTAND_STORES,
   240→    {
   241→      timeout: 15000,
   242→    }
   243→  );
   244→
   245→  // Inject auth + vault state into Zustand stores
   246→  // Arrays are used because Uint8Array can't be serialized across page.evaluate boundary
   247→  await page.evaluate((data) => {
   248→    const stores = (window as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).__ZUSTAND_STORES as {
   249→      auth: { setState: (s: Record<string, unknown>) => void };
   250→      vault: { getState: () => { setVaultKeys: (k: Record<string, unknown>) => void } };
   251→    };
   252→
   253→    stores.auth.setState({
   254→      accessToken: data.accessToken,
   255→      isAuthenticated: true,
   256→      lastAuthMethod: 'email_passwordless',
   257→      userEmail: data.email,
   258→      vaultKeypair: {
   259→        publicKey: new Uint8Array(data.publicKeyArr),
   260→        privateKey: new Uint8Array(data.privateKeyArr),
   261→      },
   262→    });
   263→
   264→    stores.vault.getState().setVaultKeys({
   265→      rootFolderKey: new Uint8Array(data.rootFolderKeyArr),
   266→      rootIpnsKeypair: {
   267→        publicKey: new Uint8Array(data.rootIpnsPublicKeyArr),
   268→        privateKey: new Uint8Array(data.rootIpnsPrivateKeyArr),
   269→      },
   270→      rootIpnsName: data.rootIpnsName,
   271→      vaultId: data.vaultId,
   272→    });
   273→  }, state);
   274→}
   275→
   276→/**
   277→ * Re-inject test auth state after a page reload.
   278→ * Since test-login bypasses Core Kit, there's no persistent session —
   279→ * this function restores Zustand stores from the cached first login,
   280→ * refreshes the access token via the HTTP-only cookie, then navigates
   281→ * to /files.
   282→ *
   283→ * Call this after page.reload() in tests that use the test-login flow.
   284→ * For tests using the real Core Kit flow, this is a no-op (Core Kit
   285→ * auto-restores its session).
   286→ *
   287→ * Accepts explicit state for multi-account tests. Falls back to
   288→ * singleton cache for backward compat with existing tests.
   289→ */
   290→export async function reinjectTestAuthAfterReload(
   291→  page: Page,
   292→  state?: TestAuthState
   293→): Promise<void> {
   294→  const authState = state ?? cachedTestAuthState;
   295→
   296→  // If not using test-login, Core Kit handles session restore
   297→  if (!process.env.TEST_LOGIN_SECRET || !authState) return;
   298→
   299→  // Try to refresh the access token via the cookie
   300→  const apiBase = process.env.API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:3000';
   301→  const refreshResponse = await page.request.post(`${apiBase}/auth/refresh`);
   302→
   303→  if (refreshResponse.ok()) {
   304→    const { accessToken, email } = await refreshResponse.json();
   305→    authState.accessToken = accessToken;
   306→    if (email) authState.email = email;
   307→  }
   308→
   309→  await injectAuthState(page, authState);
   310→
   311→  // Navigate to files via hash router
   312→  await page.evaluate(() => {
   313→    window.location.hash = '#/files';
   314→  });
   315→  await page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 30000 });
   316→}
   317→
   318→/**
   319→ * Gets the current storage state (cookies and localStorage).
   320→ * Useful for saving authenticated session state.
   321→ */
   322→export async function getStorageState(page: Page): Promise<object> {
   323→  return await page.context().storageState();
   324→}
   325→
   326→/**
   327→ * Waits for the user to be authenticated by checking for auth indicators.
   328→ * This is useful after manual login or when loading storage state.
   329→ */
   330→export async function waitForAuthentication(page: Page): Promise<void> {
   331→  // Wait for user menu to appear (indicator of authenticated state)
   332→  // Phase 6.3: Logout moved to UserMenu dropdown
   333→  await page.waitForSelector('[data-testid="user-menu"]', { timeout: 15000 });
   334→}
   335→
   336→/**
   337→ * Extract the 0x04-prefixed public key hex from a TestAuthState.
   338→ * This is the format needed for the ShareDialog recipient input.
   339→ * The test-login endpoint returns bare hex (04...) so we prepend 0x.
   340→ */
   341→export function getPublicKeyHex(state: TestAuthState): string {
   342→  return state.publicKeyHex.startsWith('0x') ? state.publicKeyHex : '0x' + state.publicKeyHex;
   343→}
   344→

     1→import { create } from 'zustand';
     2→import type { FolderChild } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
     3→
     4→/**
     5→ * A folder node in the in-memory folder tree.
     6→ * Contains decrypted folder data and navigation state.
     7→ */
     8→export type FolderNode = {
     9→  /** UUID for internal reference (null string 'root' for root folder) */
    10→  id: string;
    11→  /** Folder name */
    12→  name: string;
    13→  /** IPNS name for this folder (k51.../bafzaa... format) */
    14→  ipnsName: string;
    15→  /** Parent folder ID (null for root) */
    16→  parentId: string | null;
    17→  /** Decrypted children (files and subfolders) */
    18→  children: FolderChild[];
    19→  /** Has metadata been fetched and decrypted? */
    20→  isLoaded: boolean;
    21→  /** Is metadata currently being fetched? */
    22→  isLoading: boolean;
    23→  /** Current IPNS sequence number */
    24→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
    25→  /** Decrypted AES-256 folder key */
    26→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
    27→  /** Decrypted Ed25519 IPNS private key */
    28→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
    29→};
    30→
    31→/**
    32→ * Breadcrumb entry for navigation.
    33→ */
    34→type Breadcrumb = {
    35→  id: string;
    36→  name: string;
    37→};
    38→
    39→type FolderState = {
    40→  // Folder tree indexed by id
    41→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>;
    42→
    43→  // Current navigation
    44→  currentFolderId: string | null; // null = root
    45→  breadcrumbs: Breadcrumb[];
    46→
    47→  // Publishing state
    48→  pendingPublishes: Set<string>; // folder IDs with pending IPNS publishes
    49→
    50→  // Actions
    51→  setFolder: (folder: FolderNode) => void;
    52→  updateFolderChildren: (folderId: string, children: FolderChild[]) => void;
    53→  updateFolderSequence: (folderId: string, sequenceNumber: bigint) => void;
    54→  setCurrentFolder: (folderId: string | null) => void;
    55→  setBreadcrumbs: (breadcrumbs: Breadcrumb[]) => void;
    56→  addPendingPublish: (folderId: string) => void;
    57→  removePendingPublish: (folderId: string) => void;
    58→  removeFolder: (folderId: string) => void;
    59→  updateFolderName: (folderId: string, newName: string) => void;
    60→  clearFolders: () => void;
    61→};
    62→
    63→/**
    64→ * Folder store for managing folder tree state.
    65→ *
    66→ * Maintains the in-memory representation of the folder hierarchy
    67→ * with decrypted folder keys and metadata.
    68→ *
    69→ * SECURITY: All folder keys are memory-only - never persisted to storage.
    70→ * Call clearFolders() on logout to zero-fill all key material.
    71→ */
    72→export const useFolderStore = create<FolderState>((set, get) => ({
    73→  // State
    74→  folders: {},
    75→  currentFolderId: null,
    76→  breadcrumbs: [],
    77→  pendingPublishes: new Set<string>(),
    78→
    79→  // Actions
    80→  setFolder: (folder) =>
    81→    set((state) => ({
    82→      folders: { ...state.folders, [folder.id]: folder },
    83→    })),
    84→
    85→  updateFolderChildren: (folderId, children) =>
    86→    set((state) => {
    87→      const folder = state.folders[folderId];
    88→      if (!folder) return state;
    89→
    90→      return {
    91→        folders: {
    92→          ...state.folders,
    93→          [folderId]: { ...folder, children, isLoaded: true, isLoading: false },
    94→        },
    95→      };
    96→    }),
    97→
    98→  updateFolderSequence: (folderId, sequenceNumber) =>
    99→    set((state) => {
   100→      const folder = state.folders[folderId];
   101→      if (!folder) return state;
   102→
   103→      return {
   104→        folders: {
   105→          ...state.folders,
   106→          [folderId]: { ...folder, sequenceNumber },
   107→        },
   108→      };
   109→    }),
   110→
   111→  setCurrentFolder: (folderId) => set({ currentFolderId: folderId }),
   112→
   113→  setBreadcrumbs: (breadcrumbs) => set({ breadcrumbs }),
   114→
   115→  addPendingPublish: (folderId) =>
   116→    set((state) => {
   117→      const newPending = new Set(state.pendingPublishes);
   118→      newPending.add(folderId);
   119→      return { pendingPublishes: newPending };
   120→    }),
   121→
   122→  removePendingPublish: (folderId) =>
   123→    set((state) => {
   124→      const newPending = new Set(state.pendingPublishes);
   125→      newPending.delete(folderId);
   126→      return { pendingPublishes: newPending };
   127→    }),
   128→
   129→  // Remove a folder from local state (after deletion)
   130→  // Also clears the folder's key material from memory
   131→  removeFolder: (folderId) =>
   132→    set((state) => {
   133→      const folder = state.folders[folderId];
   134→      if (!folder) return state;
   135→
   136→      // Zero-fill keys before removing
   137→      if (folder.folderKey) folder.folderKey.fill(0);
   138→      if (folder.ipnsPrivateKey) folder.ipnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
   139→
   140→      const { [folderId]: _removed, ...remainingFolders } = state.folders;
   141→      void _removed; // Intentionally destructured to remove from object
   142→      return { folders: remainingFolders };
   143→    }),
   144→
   145→  // Update a folder's name in local state
   146→  updateFolderName: (folderId, newName) =>
   147→    set((state) => {
   148→      const folder = state.folders[folderId];
   149→      if (!folder) return state;
   150→
   151→      return {
   152→        folders: {
   153→          ...state.folders,
   154→          [folderId]: { ...folder, name: newName },
   155→        },
   156→      };
   157→    }),
   158→
   159→  // [SECURITY: MEDIUM-02] Zero-fill sensitive key material before clearing
   160→  clearFolders: () => {
   161→    const state = get();
   162→
   163→    // Best-effort memory clearing - overwrite all folder keys with zeros
   164→    for (const folder of Object.values(state.folders)) {
   165→      if (folder.folderKey) {
   166→        folder.folderKey.fill(0);
   167→      }
   168→      if (folder.ipnsPrivateKey) {
   169→        folder.ipnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
   170→      }
   171→    }
   172→
   173→    set({
   174→      folders: {},
   175→      currentFolderId: null,
   176→      breadcrumbs: [],
   177→      pendingPublishes: new Set<string>(),
   178→    });
   179→  },
   180→}));
   181→
   182→// Expose store on window for E2E testing (development only)
   183→if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && import.meta.env.DEV) {
   184→  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   185→  (window as any).__ZUSTAND_FOLDER_STORE__ = useFolderStore;
   186→}
   187→
```

     1→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
     2→
     3→// Use VITE_API_URL if set, otherwise default to same-origin /api path for deployed environments
     4→const BASE_URL =
     5→  import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ||
     6→  (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? `${window.location.origin}/api` : '/api');
     7→
     8→export const customInstance = async <T>(config: {
     9→  url: string;
    10→  method: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE' | 'PATCH';
    11→  params?: Record<string, string>;
    12→  data?: unknown;
    13→  headers?: Record<string, string>;
    14→  signal?: AbortSignal;
    15→  responseType?: 'json' | 'blob' | 'text';
    16→}): Promise<T> => {
    17→  const { url, method, params, data, headers, signal, responseType = 'json' } = config;
    18→
    19→  // Get auth token from store
    20→  const accessToken = useAuthStore.getState().accessToken;
    21→
    22→  const queryString = params ? '?' + new URLSearchParams(params).toString() : '';
    23→
    24→  const response = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${url}${queryString}`, {
    25→    method,
    26→    headers: {
    27→      // Only set Content-Type for JSON requests with body
    28→      ...(data && responseType === 'json' ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}),
    29→      ...(accessToken ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` } : {}),
    30→      ...headers,
    31→    },
    32→    body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    33→    signal,
    34→  });
    35→
    36→  if (!response.ok) {
    37→    const err = new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
    38→    (err as Error & { status: number }).status = response.status;
    39→    throw err;
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  // Return appropriate response type
    43→  if (responseType === 'blob') {
    44→    return response.blob() as Promise<T>;
    45→  } else if (responseType === 'text') {
    46→    return response.text() as Promise<T>;
    47→  }
    48→  // Responses with no body (204 No Content, 201 with void return, etc.)
    49→  // Read as text first to avoid JSON parse error on empty bodies
    50→  const text = await response.text();
    51→  if (!text.trim()) {
    52→    return undefined as T;
    53→  }
    54→  return JSON.parse(text);
    55→};
    56→
    57→export default customInstance;
    58→
```

     1→import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     2→import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
     3→import { unwrapKey, hexToBytes, type FolderEntry } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
     4→import { useFolderStore, type FolderNode } from '../stores/folder.store';
     5→import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
     6→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
     7→import { loadFolder } from '../services/folder.service';
     8→
     9→/**
    10→ * Breadcrumb entry for navigation.
    11→ */
    12→export type Breadcrumb = {
    13→  id: string;
    14→  name: string;
    15→};
    16→
    17→/**
    18→ * Hook return type.
    19→ */
    20→type UseFolderNavigationReturn = {
    21→  currentFolderId: string;
    22→  currentFolder: FolderNode | null;
    23→  breadcrumbs: Breadcrumb[];
    24→  isLoading: boolean;
    25→  navigateTo: (folderId: string) => Promise<void>;
    26→  navigateUp: () => void;
    27→};
    28→
    29→/**
    30→ * Get the root folder from vault state or folder store.
    31→ */
    32→function getRootFolder(
    33→  vaultStore: ReturnType<typeof useVaultStore.getState>,
    34→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
    35→): FolderNode | null {
    36→  // If we have an explicit root folder in the tree, use it
    37→  const existingRoot = folders['root'];
    38→  if (existingRoot) return existingRoot;
    39→
    40→  // Otherwise construct from vault state
    41→  if (!vaultStore.rootFolderKey || !vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair || !vaultStore.rootIpnsName) {
    42→    return null;
    43→  }
    44→
    45→  return {
    46→    id: 'root',
    47→    name: 'My Vault',
    48→    ipnsName: vaultStore.rootIpnsName,
    49→    parentId: null,
    50→    children: [],
    51→    isLoaded: false,
    52→    isLoading: false,
    53→    sequenceNumber: 0n,
    54→    folderKey: vaultStore.rootFolderKey,
    55→    ipnsPrivateKey: vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey,
    56→  };
    57→}
    58→
    59→/**
    60→ * Build breadcrumb trail from current folder to root.
    61→ */
    62→function buildBreadcrumbs(
    63→  folderId: string,
    64→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>,
    65→  vaultStore: ReturnType<typeof useVaultStore.getState>
    66→): Breadcrumb[] {
    67→  const breadcrumbs: Breadcrumb[] = [];
    68→  let currentId: string | null = folderId;
    69→
    70→  while (currentId) {
    71→    const currentFolder: FolderNode | null =
    72→      currentId === 'root' ? getRootFolder(vaultStore, folders) : folders[currentId];
    73→    if (!currentFolder) break;
    74→
    75→    breadcrumbs.unshift({ id: currentFolder.id, name: currentFolder.name });
    76→    currentId = currentFolder.parentId;
    77→  }
    78→
    79→  return breadcrumbs;
    80→}
    81→
    82→/**
    83→ * Hook for managing folder navigation state.
    84→ *
    85→ * Provides current folder, breadcrumb trail, and navigation functions.
    86→ * Integrates with folder store for folder tree state and vault store
    87→ * for root folder keys.
    88→ *
    89→ * @example
    90→ * ```tsx
    91→ * function FileBrowser() {
    92→ *   const { currentFolder, breadcrumbs, navigateTo, navigateUp } = useFolderNavigation();
    93→ *
    94→ *   return (
    95→ *     <div>
    96→ *       <Breadcrumbs items={breadcrumbs} onNavigate={navigateTo} />
    97→ *       <FileList items={currentFolder?.children ?? []} />
    98→ *     </div>
    99→ *   );
   100→ * }
   101→ * ```
   102→ */
   103→export function useFolderNavigation(): UseFolderNavigationReturn {
   104→  // Get folder ID from URL params - defaults to 'root' when not present
   105→  const { folderId } = useParams<{ folderId?: string }>();
   106→  const navigate = useNavigate();
   107→  const currentFolderId = folderId ?? 'root';
   108→
   109→  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
   110→  // Track latest navigation target to prevent stale completions from race conditions
   111→  const latestNavTarget = useRef<string | null>(null);
   112→
   113→  // Subscribe to stores
   114→  const folders = useFolderStore((state) => state.folders);
   115→  const setFolder = useFolderStore((state) => state.setFolder);
   116→  const setBreadcrumbs = useFolderStore((state) => state.setBreadcrumbs);
   117→
   118→  // Vault state for root folder
   119→  const rootFolderKey = useVaultStore((state) => state.rootFolderKey);
   120→  const rootIpnsKeypair = useVaultStore((state) => state.rootIpnsKeypair);
   121→  const rootIpnsName = useVaultStore((state) => state.rootIpnsName);
   122→
   123→  // Get vault state snapshot for helper functions
   124→  const vaultState = useVaultStore.getState();
   125→
   126→  // Get current folder node
   127→  const currentFolder =
   128→    currentFolderId === 'root' ? getRootFolder(vaultState, folders) : folders[currentFolderId];
   129→
   130→  // Build breadcrumbs whenever current folder changes (memoized to avoid recreating array)
   131→  const breadcrumbs = useMemo(
   132→    () => buildBreadcrumbs(currentFolderId, folders, vaultState),
   133→    [currentFolderId, folders, vaultState]
   134→  );
   135→
   136→  // Initialize root folder in store if vault is ready but root not in store
   137→  useEffect(() => {
   138→    if (rootFolderKey && rootIpnsKeypair && rootIpnsName && !folders['root']) {
   139→      const rootFolder = getRootFolder(vaultState, folders);
   140→      if (rootFolder) {
   141→        setFolder(rootFolder);
   142→      }
   143→    }
   144→  }, [rootFolderKey, rootIpnsKeypair, rootIpnsName, folders, setFolder, vaultState]);
   145→
   146→  // Update store breadcrumbs when local breadcrumbs change
   147→  useEffect(() => {
   148→    setBreadcrumbs(breadcrumbs);
   149→  }, [breadcrumbs, setBreadcrumbs]);
   150→
   151→  /**
   152→   * Navigate to a specific folder using react-router.
   153→   * Browser history is automatically managed.
   154→   * Loads subfolder metadata (IPNS resolve + decrypt) if not yet loaded.
   155→   */
   156→  const navigateTo = useCallback(
   157→    async (targetFolderId: string) => {
   158→      // Track this navigation to detect stale completions from rapid clicks
   159→      latestNavTarget.current = targetFolderId;
   160→
   161→      // Root is always constructible from vault state — just navigate
   162→      if (targetFolderId === 'root') {
   163→        navigate('/files');
   164→        return;
   165→      }
   166→
   167→      // Use getState() to avoid stale Zustand closures in async callback
   168→      const currentFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   169→      const targetFolder = currentFolders[targetFolderId];
   170→
   171→      // If already loaded, just navigate
   172→      if (targetFolder?.isLoaded) {
   173→        navigate(`/files/${targetFolderId}`);
   174→        return;
   175→      }
   176→
   177→      // Find the FolderEntry for this subfolder in any loaded parent's children
   178→      let folderEntry: FolderEntry | undefined;
   179→      let parentId: string | null = null;
   180→
   181→      for (const [fId, fNode] of Object.entries(currentFolders)) {
   182→        if (!fNode.isLoaded) continue;
   183→        const match = fNode.children.find(
   184→          (c): c is FolderEntry => c.type === 'folder' && c.id === targetFolderId
   185→        );
   186→        if (match) {
   187→          folderEntry = match;
   188→          parentId = fId;
   189→          break;
   190→        }
   191→      }
   192→
   193→      if (!folderEntry) {
   194→        console.error(
   195→          `Cannot load folder ${targetFolderId}: no parent with its FolderEntry is loaded`
   196→        );
   197→        return;
   198→      }
   199→
   200→      // Get user's ECIES private key for unwrapping
   201→      const vaultKeypair = useAuthStore.getState().vaultKeypair;
   202→      if (!vaultKeypair) {
   203→        console.error('Cannot load folder: no vault keypair available');
   204→        return;
   205→      }
   206→
   207→      // Insert placeholder BEFORE navigating so the component re-render
   208→      // finds the folder node already in the store (avoids undefined flash)
   209→      setIsLoading(true);
   210→      const loadingPlaceholder: FolderNode = {
   211→        id: targetFolderId,
   212→        name: folderEntry.name,
   213→        ipnsName: folderEntry.ipnsName,
   214→        parentId,
   215→        children: [],
   216→        isLoaded: false,
   217→        isLoading: true,
   218→        sequenceNumber: 0n,
   219→        folderKey: new Uint8Array(0),
   220→        ipnsPrivateKey: new Uint8Array(0),
   221→      };
   222→      useFolderStore.getState().setFolder(loadingPlaceholder);
   223→
   224→      // Now navigate — the placeholder is already in the store
   225→      navigate(`/files/${targetFolderId}`);
   226→
   227→      try {
   228→        // Unwrap keys using user's ECIES private key
   229→        const folderKey = await unwrapKey(
   230→          hexToBytes(folderEntry.folderKeyEncrypted),
   231→          vaultKeypair.privateKey
   232→        );
   233→        const ipnsPrivateKey = await unwrapKey(
   234→          hexToBytes(folderEntry.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted),
   235→          vaultKeypair.privateKey
   236→        );
   237→
   238→        // Load folder metadata from IPNS (retry if IPNS hasn't propagated yet)
   239→        const MAX_RETRIES = 3;
   240→        const RETRY_DELAY_MS = 2000;
   241→        let folderNode: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof loadFolder>> | null = null;
   242→
   243→        for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= MAX_RETRIES; attempt++) {
   244→          if (latestNavTarget.current !== targetFolderId) return;
   245→
   246→          folderNode = await loadFolder(
   247→            targetFolderId,
   248→            folderKey,
   249→            ipnsPrivateKey,
   250→            folderEntry.ipnsName,
   251→            parentId,
   252→            folderEntry.name
   253→          );
   254→
   255→          // If loaded successfully or this is the last attempt, stop retrying
   256→          if (folderNode.isLoaded || attempt === MAX_RETRIES) break;
   257→
   258→          // IPNS not propagated yet — wait and retry
   259→          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, RETRY_DELAY_MS));
   260→        }
   261→
   262→        // Only apply result if this is still the latest navigation
   263→        if (latestNavTarget.current !== targetFolderId) return;
   264→
   265→        useFolderStore.getState().setFolder(folderNode!);
   266→      } catch (err) {
   267→        console.error('Failed to load subfolder:', err);
   268→        // Only clean up if this is still the latest navigation
   269→        if (latestNavTarget.current !== targetFolderId) return;
   270→        // Remove the loading placeholder and navigate back to parent.
   271→        // Without this navigation, the URL stays at /files/<uuid> with no
   272→        // folder in the store, rendering a broken empty ~/root fallback.
   273→        useFolderStore.getState().removeFolder(targetFolderId);
   274→        setIsLoading(false);
   275→        latestNavTarget.current = parentId ?? 'root';
   276→        if (parentId) {
   277→          navigate(`/files/${parentId}`);
   278→        } else {
   279→          navigate('/files');
   280→        }
   281→      } finally {
   282→        // Only clear loading if this is still the latest navigation
   283→        if (latestNavTarget.current === targetFolderId) {
   284→          setIsLoading(false);
   285→        }
   286→      }
   287→    },
   288→    [navigate]
   289→  );
   290→
   291→  /**
   292→   * Navigate up to parent folder.
   293→   */
   294→  const navigateUp = useCallback(() => {
   295→    if (currentFolder?.parentId) {
   296→      navigateTo(currentFolder.parentId);
   297→    } else if (currentFolderId !== 'root') {
   298→      navigateTo('root');
   299→    }
   300→  }, [currentFolder, currentFolderId, navigateTo]);
   301→
   302→  return {
   303→    currentFolderId,
   304→    currentFolder,
   305→    breadcrumbs,
   306→    isLoading,
   307→    navigateTo,
   308→    navigateUp,
   309→  };
   310→}
   311→

   162→  /**
   163→   * Create or update a folder/file IPNS entry.
   164→   * Handles both folder metadata and per-file metadata IPNS records.
   165→   */
   166→  private async upsertFolderIpns(
   167→    userId: string,
   168→    ipnsName: string,
   169→    metadataCid: string,
   170→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string,
   171→    keyEpoch?: number,
   172→    recordType: 'folder' | 'file' = 'folder',
   173→    expectedSequenceNumber?: string
   174→  ): Promise<FolderIpns> {
   175→    const existing = await this.getFolderIpns(userId, ipnsName);
   176→
   177→    // Conflict detection: when expectedSequenceNumber is provided, verify it matches
   178→    if (existing && expectedSequenceNumber !== undefined) {
   179→      const expected = BigInt(expectedSequenceNumber);
   180→      const current = BigInt(existing.sequenceNumber);
   181→      if (expected !== current) {
   182→        throw new ConflictException({
   183→          statusCode: 409,
   184→          message: 'Sequence number mismatch: folder was modified by another device',
   185→          currentSequenceNumber: existing.sequenceNumber,
   186→          expectedSequenceNumber,
   187→        });
   188→      }
   189→    }
   190→
   191→    if (existing) {
   192→      // Update existing entry
   193→      existing.latestCid = metadataCid;
   194→      existing.sequenceNumber = (BigInt(existing.sequenceNumber) + 1n).toString();
   195→      existing.recordType = recordType;
   196→      existing.updatedAt = new Date();
   197→
   198→      // Only update encrypted key if provided (e.g., on key rotation)
   199→      if (encryptedIpnsPrivateKey && keyEpoch !== undefined) {
   200→        existing.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey = Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex');
   201→        existing.keyEpoch = keyEpoch;
   202→      }
   203→
   204→      const saved = await this.folderIpnsRepository.save(existing);
   205→
   206→      // Auto-enroll for TEE republishing when encrypted key is provided
   207→      if (encryptedIpnsPrivateKey && keyEpoch !== undefined) {
   208→        this.republishService
   209→          .enrollFolder(
   210→            userId,
   211→            ipnsName,
   212→            Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex'),
   213→            keyEpoch,
   214→            metadataCid,
   215→            saved.sequenceNumber
   216→          )
   217→          .catch((err) =>
   218→            this.logger.warn(
   219→              `Failed to enroll ${recordType} ${ipnsName} for republishing: ${err.message}`
   220→            )
   221→          );
   222→      }
   223→
   224→      return saved;
   225→    }
   226→
   227→    // Create new entry
   228→    const folder = this.folderIpnsRepository.create({
   229→      userId,
   230→      ipnsName,
   231→      latestCid: metadataCid,
   232→      sequenceNumber: '0',
   233→      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: encryptedIpnsPrivateKey
   234→        ? Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex')
   235→        : null,
   236→      keyEpoch: keyEpoch ?? null,
   237→      isRoot: false, // Root folder is tracked in Vault entity
   238→      recordType,
   239→    });
   240→
   241→    const saved = await this.folderIpnsRepository.save(folder);
   242→
   243→    // Auto-enroll for TEE republishing when encrypted key is provided
   244→    if (encryptedIpnsPrivateKey && keyEpoch !== undefined) {
   245→      this.republishService
   246→        .enrollFolder(
   247→          userId,
   248→          ipnsName,
   249→          Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex'),
   250→          keyEpoch,
   251→          metadataCid,
   252→          saved.sequenceNumber
   253→        )
   254→        .catch((err) =>
   255→          this.logger.warn(
   256→            `Failed to enroll ${recordType} ${ipnsName} for republishing: ${err.message}`
   257→          )
   258→        );
   259→    }

   220→ * Update folder metadata and publish to IPNS (v2 format).
   221→ *
   222→ * Encrypts the metadata with the folder key, uploads to IPFS,
   223→ * and publishes an updated IPNS record pointing to the new CID.
   224→ *
   225→ * @param params.folderId - Folder being updated
   226→ * @param params.children - New children array (FolderChild[])
   227→ * @param params.folderKey - Decrypted AES-256 folder key
   228→ * @param params.ipnsPrivateKey - Decrypted Ed25519 IPNS private key
   229→ * @param params.ipnsName - IPNS name for this folder
   230→ * @param params.sequenceNumber - Current sequence number
   231→ * @param params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey - TEE-wrapped key (first publish only)
   232→ * @param params.keyEpoch - TEE key epoch (required with encryptedIpnsPrivateKey)
   233→ * @returns New CID and sequence number
   234→ */
   235→export async function updateFolderMetadata(params: {
   236→  folderId: string;
   237→  children: FolderChild[];
   238→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
   239→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   240→  ipnsName: string;
   241→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
   242→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
   243→  keyEpoch?: number;
   244→}): Promise<{ cid: string; newSequenceNumber: bigint }> {
   245→  // 1. Create v2 folder metadata
   246→  const metadata: FolderMetadata = {
   247→    version: 'v2',
   248→    children: params.children,
   249→  };
   250→
   251→  // 2. Encrypt metadata with folder key
   252→  const encrypted = await encryptFolderMetadata(metadata, params.folderKey);
   253→
   254→  // 3. Upload to IPFS via backend relay
   255→  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(encrypted)], {
   256→    type: 'application/json',
   257→  });
   258→  const { cid } = await addToIpfs(blob);
   259→
   260→  // 4. Publish IPNS record pointing to new CID.
   261→  //    Send the pre-increment sequence as expectedSequenceNumber so the API can
   262→  //    detect concurrent modifications from other devices (conflict detection).
   263→  const newSeq = params.sequenceNumber + 1n;
   264→  await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
   265→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.ipnsPrivateKey,
   266→    ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
   267→    metadataCid: cid,
   268→    sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   269→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   270→    keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
   271→    expectedSequenceNumber: params.sequenceNumber.toString(),
   272→  });
   273→
   274→  return { cid, newSequenceNumber: newSeq };
   275→}
   276→
   277→/**
   278→ * Build a folder IPNS record payload for batch publishing.
   279→ *
   280→ * Creates and signs an IPNS record locally without publishing.
   281→ * Returns the payload that can be included in a batch publish call.
   282→ *
   283→ * @returns Folder IPNS record payload for batch publish
   284→ */
   285→async function buildFolderIpnsRecord(params: {
   286→  children: FolderChild[];
   287→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
   288→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   289→  ipnsName: string;
   290→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
   291→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
   292→  keyEpoch?: number;
   293→}): Promise<{
   294→  cid: string;
   295→  record: FileIpnsRecordPayload & { recordType: 'folder'; expectedSequenceNumber: string };
   296→  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
   297→}> {
   298→  // 1. Create v2 folder metadata
   299→  const metadata: FolderMetadata = {
   300→    version: 'v2',
   301→    children: params.children,
   302→  };
   303→
   304→  // 2. Encrypt metadata with folder key
   305→  const encrypted = await encryptFolderMetadata(metadata, params.folderKey);
   306→
   307→  // 3. Upload to IPFS
   308→  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(encrypted)], { type: 'application/json' });
   309→  const { cid } = await addToIpfs(blob);
   310→
   311→  // 4. Create IPNS record
   312→  const newSeq = params.sequenceNumber + 1n;
   313→  const record = await createIpnsRecord(
   314→    params.ipnsPrivateKey,
   315→    `/ipfs/${cid}`,
   316→    newSeq,
   317→    24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
   318→  );
   319→  const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
   320→  const recordBase64 = uint8ToBase64(recordBytes);
   321→
   322→  return {
   323→    cid,
   324→    record: {
   325→      ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
   326→      recordBase64,
   327→      metadataCid: cid,
   328→      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   329→      keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
   330→      recordType: 'folder',
   331→      // Send pre-increment sequence as expectedSequenceNumber for conflict detection.
   332→      // File records (recordType: 'file') do not set this -- they use last-write-wins.
   333→      expectedSequenceNumber: params.sequenceNumber.toString(),
   334→    },
   335→    newSequenceNumber: newSeq,
   336→  };
   337→}
   338→

     1→import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
     2→import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';

     4→import * as folderService from '../services/folder.service';

     7→/**
     8→ * Re-sync a specific folder after a 409 conflict.
     9→ *
    10→ * Resolves the folder's IPNS name to get fresh CID + sequenceNumber,
    11→ * fetches and decrypts the metadata, and updates the folder store.
    12→ */
    13→export async function resyncFolder(folderIpnsName: string, folderId: string): Promise<void> {
    14→  const store = useFolderStore.getState();
    15→  const folderNode = store.folders[folderId];
    16→  if (!folderNode) return;
    17→
    18→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderIpnsName);
    19→  if (!resolved) return;
    20→
    21→  const remoteMetadata = await folderService.fetchAndDecryptMetadata(
    22→    resolved.cid,
    23→    folderNode.folderKey
    24→  );
    25→
    26→  store.updateFolderChildren(folderId, remoteMetadata.children ?? []);
    27→  store.updateFolderSequence(folderId, resolved.sequenceNumber);
    28→}
    29→
    30→/** Maximum folder nesting depth per FOLD-03 */
    31→export const MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH = 20;
    32→

    34→ * State for folder operations.

    36→export type FolderOperationState = {
    37→  isLoading: boolean;

    39→};
    40→
    41→/**
    42→ * Get the root folder state from vault and folder stores.
    43→ * Root folder uses vault keys directly.
    44→ */
    45→export function getRootFolderState(
    46→  vaultStore: ReturnType<typeof useVaultStore.getState>,
    47→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
    48→): FolderNode | null {
    49→  if (!vaultStore.rootFolderKey || !vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair || !vaultStore.rootIpnsName) {
    50→    return null;
    51→  }
    52→
    53→  // If we have an explicit root folder in the tree, use it
    54→  const existingRoot = folders['root'];
    55→  if (existingRoot) return existingRoot;
    56→
    57→  // Otherwise construct from vault state
    58→  return {
    59→    id: 'root',
    60→    name: 'My Vault',
    61→    ipnsName: vaultStore.rootIpnsName,
    62→    parentId: null,
    63→    children: [],
    64→    isLoaded: false,
    65→    isLoading: false,
    66→    sequenceNumber: 0n,
    67→    folderKey: vaultStore.rootFolderKey,
    68→    ipnsPrivateKey: vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey,
    69→  };
    70→}

    74→ */
    75→export function calculateFolderDepth(
    76→  folderId: string,

    80→   * Processes up to 10 records concurrently.
    81→   */
    82→  async publishBatch(
    83→    userId: string,
    84→    dto: BatchPublishIpnsDto
    85→  ): Promise<BatchPublishIpnsResponseDto> {
    86→    const results: PublishIpnsResponseDto[] = [];
    87→    let totalSucceeded = 0;
    88→    let totalFailed = 0;
    89→
    90→    const CONCURRENCY = 10;
    91→
    92→    // Process records in batches of CONCURRENCY
    93→    for (let i = 0; i < dto.records.length; i += CONCURRENCY) {
    94→      const batch = dto.records.slice(i, i + CONCURRENCY);
    95→
    96→      const settled = await Promise.allSettled(
    97→        batch.map(async (entry) => {
    98→          // Validate base64 record
    99→          let recordBytes: Uint8Array;
   100→          try {
   101→            recordBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(entry.record), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   102→          } catch {
   103→            throw new BadRequestException(`Invalid base64-encoded record for ${entry.ipnsName}`);
   104→          }
   105→
   106→          // Save to DB first so resolve always has a fallback
   107→          const folder = await this.upsertFolderIpns(
   108→            userId,
   109→            entry.ipnsName,
   110→            entry.metadataCid,
   111→            entry.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   112→            entry.keyEpoch,
   113→            entry.recordType ?? 'folder',
   114→            entry.expectedSequenceNumber
   115→          );
   116→
   117→          // Publish to delegated routing (best-effort)
   118→          try {
   119→            await this.delegatedRouting.publish(entry.ipnsName, recordBytes);
   120→          } catch (error) {
   121→            this.logger.warn(
   122→              `Delegated routing publish failed for ${entry.ipnsName}, DB record saved: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
   123→            );
   124→          }
   125→
   126→          return {
   127→            success: true,
   128→            ipnsName: entry.ipnsName,
   129→            sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber,
   130→          } as PublishIpnsResponseDto;
   131→        })
   132→      );
   133→
   134→      for (let j = 0; j < settled.length; j++) {
   135→        const result = settled[j];
   136→        if (result.status === 'fulfilled') {
   137→          results.push(result.value);
   138→          totalSucceeded++;
   139→        } else {
   140→          const reason = result.reason;
   141→          // If any record (especially a folder record) has a conflict, fail the entire batch
   142→          if (reason instanceof ConflictException) {
   143→            throw reason;
   144→          }
   145→          const ipnsName = batch[j]?.ipnsName ?? 'unknown';
   146→          this.logger.warn(
   147→            `Batch publish failed for ${ipnsName}: ${reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : String(reason)}`
   148→          );
   149→          results.push({
   150→            success: false,
   151→            ipnsName,
   152→            sequenceNumber: '0',
   153→          });
   154→          totalFailed++;
   155→        }
   156→      }
   157→    }
   158→
   159→    return { results, totalSucceeded, totalFailed };

    24→@Injectable()

    40→  async publishRecord(userId: string, dto: PublishIpnsDto): Promise<PublishIpnsResponseDto> {
    41→    // Validate base64 record

    45→    } catch {
    46→      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid base64-encoded record');
    47→    }
    48→
    49→    // Save to DB first so resolve always has a fallback, even if delegated
    50→    // routing fails (e.g. rate-limited, network error, DHT propagation delay).

    57→      'folder',

    59→    );

    61→    // Publish to delegated routing API (best-effort — DB is the reliable source)
    62→    try {
    63→      await this.delegatedRouting.publish(dto.ipnsName, recordBytes);
    64→    } catch (error) {
    65→      this.logger.warn(

    67→      );
    68→    }

    70→    return {
    71→      success: true,
    72→      ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    73→      sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber,
    74→    };
    75→  }
    76→
    77→  /**
    78→   * Batch publish multiple IPNS records with concurrency-limited processing.
    79→   * Supports partial success: individual record failures do not fail the batch.
    80→   * Processes up to 10 records concurrently.
