    46→        }
    47→
    48→        // Get user's ECIES keypair for vault cryptographic operations (public + private keys stored in memory after login)
    49→        // The public key is used here for key wrapping; the private key remains client-side for decryption operations.
    50→        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
    51→          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
    52→        }
    53→        const userPublicKey = auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey;
    54→
    55→        // Create the folder (generates keys, wraps with user public key, TEE-encrypts IPNS key)
    56→        const { folder, ipnsPrivateKey, folderKey, encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, keyEpoch } =
    57→          await folderService.createFolder({
    58→            parentFolderId: parentId,
    59→            name,
    60→            userPublicKey,
    61→            folders,
    62→          });
    63→
    64→        // Get parent folder state
    65→        const parentFolder =
    66→          parentId && folders[parentId] ? folders[parentId] : getRootFolderState(vault, folders);
    67→
    68→        if (!parentFolder) {
    69→          throw new Error('Parent folder not found or vault not initialized');
    70→        }
    71→
    72→        // Add to parent's children
    73→        const newChildren = [...parentFolder.children, folder];
    74→
    75→        // Update parent folder metadata and publish IPNS (with conflict retry)
    76→        const performParentUpdate = async () => {
    77→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
    78→          const freshParent =
    79→            parentId && freshState.folders[parentId]
    80→              ? freshState.folders[parentId]
    81→              : getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders);
    82→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
    83→          const freshChildren = [...freshParent.children, folder];
    84→          const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
    85→            folderId: freshParent.id,
    86→            children: freshChildren,
    87→            folderKey: freshParent.folderKey,
    88→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshParent.ipnsPrivateKey,
    89→            ipnsName: freshParent.ipnsName,
    90→            sequenceNumber: freshParent.sequenceNumber,
    91→          });
    92→          return { children: freshChildren, newSequenceNumber };
    93→        };
    94→
    95→        let finalChildren = newChildren;
    96→        let parentSequence = parentFolder.sequenceNumber;
    97→        try {
    98→          const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
    99→            folderId: parentFolder.id,
   100→            children: newChildren,
   101→            folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   102→            ipnsPrivateKey: parentFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,
   103→            ipnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   104→            sequenceNumber: parentFolder.sequenceNumber,
   105→          });
   106→          parentSequence = newSequenceNumber;
   107→        } catch (err) {
   108→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   109→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   110→          try {
   111→            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);
   112→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   113→            const result = await performParentUpdate();
   114→            finalChildren = result.children;
   115→            parentSequence = result.newSequenceNumber;
   116→          } catch (retryErr) {
   117→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   118→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   119→            }
   120→            throw retryErr;
   121→          } finally {
   122→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   123→          }
   124→        }
   125→
   126→        // First publish for the new folder's own IPNS record with TEE-encrypted key
   127→        // This sends encryptedIpnsPrivateKey to backend for TEE republish enrollment
   128→        const { newSequenceNumber: newFolderSequence } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   129→          folderId: folder.id,
   130→          children: [],
   131→          folderKey,
   132→          ipnsPrivateKey,
   133→          ipnsName: folder.ipnsName,
   134→          sequenceNumber: 0n,
   135→          encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   136→          keyEpoch,
   137→        });
   138→
   139→        // Update local state - add new folder to tree and persist sequence
   140→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentFolder.id, finalChildren);
   141→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentFolder.id, parentSequence);
   142→
   143→        // Also add the new folder node to the store (with its decrypted keys)
   144→        const newFolderNode: FolderNode = {
   145→          id: folder.id,
   146→          name: folder.name,
   147→          ipnsName: folder.ipnsName,
   148→          parentId: parentFolder.id,
   149→          children: [],
   150→          isLoaded: true,
   151→          isLoading: false,
   152→          sequenceNumber: newFolderSequence,
   153→          folderKey,
   154→          ipnsPrivateKey,
   155→        };
   156→        useFolderStore.getState().setFolder(newFolderNode);
   157→
   158→        // Post-create: re-wrap new subfolder key for share recipients (fire-and-forget)
   159→        reWrapForRecipients({
   160→          folderIpnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   161→          folders: useFolderStore.getState().folders,
   162→          currentFolderId: parentFolder.id,
   163→          newItems: [{ keyType: 'folder', itemId: folder.id, plaintextKey: folderKey }],
   164→        }).catch((err) => {
   165→          console.warn('[share] Post-create subfolder re-wrapping failed:', err);
   166→        });
   167→
   168→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   169→        return folder;
   170→      } catch (err) {
   171→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to create folder';
   172→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   173→        throw err;
   174→      }
   175→    },
   176→    []
   177→  );
   178→
   179→  /**
   180→   * Rename a file or folder.
   181→   *
   182→   * @param itemId - ID of item to rename
   183→   * @param itemType - 'file' or 'folder'
   184→   * @param newName - New name
   185→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID
   186→   */
   187→  const handleRename = useCallback(
   188→    async (
   189→      itemId: string,
   190→      itemType: 'file' | 'folder',
   191→      newName: string,
   192→      parentId: string
   193→    ): Promise<void> => {
   194→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   195→      try {
   196→        const getParentFolder = () => {
   197→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   198→          return parentId === 'root'
   199→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   200→            : freshState.folders[parentId];
   201→        };
   202→
   203→        const parentFolder = getParentFolder();
   204→        if (!parentFolder) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   205→
   206→        const performRename = async () => {
   207→          const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   208→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   209→          if (itemType === 'folder') {
   210→            await folderService.renameFolder({
   211→              folderId: itemId,
   212→              newName,
   213→              parentFolderState: freshParent,
   214→            });
   215→          } else {
   216→            await folderService.renameFile({
   217→              fileId: itemId,
   218→              newName,
   219→              parentFolderState: freshParent,
   220→            });
   221→          }
   222→        };
   223→
   224→        try {
   225→          await performRename();
   226→        } catch (err) {
   227→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   228→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   229→          try {
   230→            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);
   231→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   232→            await performRename();
   233→          } catch (retryErr) {
   234→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   235→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   236→            }
   237→            throw retryErr;
   238→          } finally {
   239→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   240→          }
   241→        }
   242→
   243→        if (itemType === 'folder') {
   244→          // Update local folder state name
   245→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderName(itemId, newName);
   246→        }
   247→
   248→        // Update parent's children with new name using fresh state
   249→        const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   250→        if (freshParent) {
   251→          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.map((child) => {
   252→            if (child.id === itemId) {
   253→              return { ...child, name: newName, modifiedAt: Date.now() };
   254→            }
   255→            return child;
   256→          });
   257→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   258→        }
   259→
   260→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   261→      } catch (err) {
   262→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to rename';
   263→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   264→        throw err;
   265→      }
   266→    },
   267→    []
   268→  );
   269→
   270→  /**
   271→   * Move a file or folder to a different parent.
   272→   *
   273→   * @param itemId - ID of item to move
   274→   * @param itemType - 'file' or 'folder'
   275→   * @param sourceParentId - Current parent folder ID
   276→   * @param destParentId - Destination parent folder ID
   277→   */
   278→  const handleMove = useCallback(
   279→    async (
   280→      itemId: string,
   281→      itemType: 'file' | 'folder',
   282→      sourceParentId: string,
   283→      destParentId: string
   284→    ): Promise<void> => {
   285→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   286→      try {
   287→        const getSourceFolder = () => {
   288→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   289→          return sourceParentId === 'root'
   290→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   291→            : freshState.folders[sourceParentId];
   292→        };
   293→
   294→        const getDestFolder = () => {
   295→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   296→          return destParentId === 'root'
   297→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   298→            : freshState.folders[destParentId];
   299→        };
   300→
   301→        const sourceFolder = getSourceFolder();
   302→        const destFolder = getDestFolder();
   303→        if (!sourceFolder || !destFolder) {
   304→          throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   305→        }
   306→
   307→        const performMove = async () => {
   308→          const freshSource = getSourceFolder();
   309→          const freshDest = getDestFolder();
   310→          if (!freshSource || !freshDest) throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   311→          const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   312→          if (itemType === 'folder') {
   313→            await folderService.moveFolder({
   314→              folderId: itemId,
   315→              sourceFolderState: freshSource,
   316→              destFolderState: freshDest,
   317→              folders,
   318→            });
   319→          } else {
   320→            await folderService.moveFile({
   321→              fileId: itemId,
   322→              sourceFolderState: freshSource,
   323→              destFolderState: freshDest,
   324→            });
   325→          }
   326→        };
   327→
   328→        try {
   329→          await performMove();
   330→        } catch (err) {
   331→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   332→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   333→          try {
   334→            // Re-sync both source and destination folders
   335→            await Promise.all([
   336→              resyncFolder(sourceFolder.ipnsName, sourceFolder.id),
   337→              sourceFolder.id !== destFolder.id
   338→                ? resyncFolder(destFolder.ipnsName, destFolder.id)
   339→                : Promise.resolve(),
   340→            ]);
   341→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   342→            await performMove();
   343→          } catch (retryErr) {
   344→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   345→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   346→            }
   347→            throw retryErr;
   348→          } finally {
   349→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   350→          }
   351→        }
   352→
   353→        // Save the moved item BEFORE updating source children (otherwise it's already gone)
   354→        const freshSourceBeforeUpdate = getSourceFolder();
   355→        const movedItem = freshSourceBeforeUpdate?.children.find((c) => c.id === itemId);
   356→
   357→        if (itemType === 'folder') {
   358→          // Update the moved folder's parentId in local state
   359→          const freshFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   360→          const movedFolder = freshFolders[itemId];
   361→          if (movedFolder) {
   362→            useFolderStore.getState().setFolder({
   363→              ...movedFolder,
   364→              parentId: destParentId,
   365→            });
   366→          }
   367→        }
   368→
   369→        // Update source folder's children (remove item)
   370→        if (freshSourceBeforeUpdate) {
   371→          const updatedSourceChildren = freshSourceBeforeUpdate.children.filter(
   372→            (c) => c.id !== itemId
   373→          );
   374→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(sourceParentId, updatedSourceChildren);
   375→        }
   376→
   377→        // Update dest folder's children (add item)
   378→        const freshDest = getDestFolder();
   379→        if (movedItem && freshDest) {
   380→          const updatedDestChildren = [
   381→            ...freshDest.children,
   382→            { ...movedItem, modifiedAt: Date.now() },
   383→          ];
   384→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(destParentId, updatedDestChildren);
   385→        }
   386→
   387→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   388→      } catch (err) {
   389→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to move';
   390→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   391→        throw err;
   392→      }
   393→    },
   394→    []
   395→  );
   396→
   397→  /**
   398→   * Move multiple files/folders to a destination in a single batch.
   399→   *
   400→   * Uses add-before-remove pattern. Publishes IPNS once for the destination
   401→   * and once for the source (2 total), regardless of how many items are moved.
   402→   *
   403→   * @param items - Array of { id, type } to move
   404→   * @param sourceParentId - Current parent folder ID (all items must share the same parent)
   405→   * @param destParentId - Destination parent folder ID
   406→   */
   407→  const handleMoveItems = useCallback(
   408→    async (
   409→      items: Array<{ id: string; type: 'file' | 'folder' }>,
   410→      sourceParentId: string,
   411→      destParentId: string
   412→    ): Promise<void> => {
   413→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   414→      try {
   415→        const getSourceFolder = () => {
   416→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   417→          return sourceParentId === 'root'
   418→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   419→            : freshState.folders[sourceParentId];
   420→        };
   421→        const getDestFolder = () => {
   422→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   423→          return destParentId === 'root'
   424→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   425→            : freshState.folders[destParentId];
   426→        };
   427→
   428→        const sourceFolder = getSourceFolder();
   429→        const destFolder = getDestFolder();
   430→
   431→        if (!sourceFolder || !destFolder) {
   432→          throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   433→        }
   434→
   435→        const itemIds = new Set(items.map((i) => i.id));
   436→        const now = Date.now();
   437→
   438→        // Validate batch move preconditions against current folder state.
   439→        // Must be called before each attempt (initial + retry after resync)
   440→        // because concurrent changes may introduce name collisions or depth violations.
   441→        const validateBatchMove = () => {
   442→          const currentSource = getSourceFolder();
   443→          const currentDest = getDestFolder();
   444→          if (!currentSource || !currentDest) {
   445→            throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   446→          }
   447→
   448→          const currentMovedChildren = currentSource.children.filter((c) => itemIds.has(c.id));
   449→          if (currentMovedChildren.length !== itemIds.size) {
   450→            throw new Error(
   451→              'One or more selected items no longer exist. Please refresh and retry.'
   452→            );
   453→          }
   454→          const batchNames = new Set<string>();
   455→          for (const child of currentMovedChildren) {
   456→            // Intra-batch duplicate name check
   457→            if (batchNames.has(child.name)) {
   458→              throw new Error(`Multiple selected items share the name "${child.name}"`);
   459→            }
   460→            batchNames.add(child.name);
   461→
   462→            // Name collision check against destination
   463→            const nameExists = currentDest.children.some((c) => c.name === child.name);
   464→            if (nameExists) {
   465→              throw new Error(`An item named "${child.name}" already exists in the destination`);
   466→            }
   467→
   468→            if (child.type === 'folder') {
   469→              // Prevent moving folder into itself or descendant
   470→              const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   471→              if (folderService.isDescendantOf(currentDest.id, child.id, folders)) {
   472→                throw new Error(`Cannot move "${child.name}" into itself or its subfolder`);
   473→              }
   474→
   475→              // Depth limit check
   476→              const destDepth = folderService.getDepth(currentDest.id, folders);
   477→              const subtreeDepth = folderService.calculateSubtreeDepth(child.id, folders);
   478→              if (destDepth + 1 + subtreeDepth > MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH) {
   479→                throw new Error(
   480→                  `Cannot move "${child.name}": would exceed maximum folder depth of ${MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH}`
   481→                );
   482→              }
   483→            }
   484→          }
   485→        };
   486→
   487→        validateBatchMove();
   488→
   489→        const performBatchMove = async (): Promise<{
   490→          destSeq: bigint;
   491→          sourceSeq: bigint;
   492→          destChildren: FolderNode['children'];
   493→          sourceChildren: FolderNode['children'];
   494→        }> => {
   495→          const freshSource = getSourceFolder();
   496→          const freshDest = getDestFolder();
   497→          if (!freshSource || !freshDest) throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   498→
   499→          // Rebuild moved children from fresh source state
   500→          const freshMoved = freshSource.children.filter((c) => itemIds.has(c.id));
   501→          if (freshMoved.length !== itemIds.size) {
   502→            throw new Error(
   503→              'One or more selected items no longer exist. Please refresh and retry.'
   504→            );
   505→          }
   506→
   507→          // ADD all to destination FIRST (add-before-remove pattern)
   508→          const destChildren = [
   509→            ...freshDest.children,
   510→            ...freshMoved.map((c) => ({ ...c, modifiedAt: now })),
   511→          ];
   512→
   513→          const { newSequenceNumber: destSeq } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   514→            folderId: freshDest.id,
   515→            children: destChildren,
   516→            folderKey: freshDest.folderKey,
   517→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshDest.ipnsPrivateKey,
   518→            ipnsName: freshDest.ipnsName,
   519→            sequenceNumber: freshDest.sequenceNumber,
   520→          });
   521→
   522→          // REMOVE all from source AFTER destination confirmed
   523→          const sourceChildren = freshSource.children.filter((c) => !itemIds.has(c.id));
   524→
   525→          const { newSequenceNumber: sourceSeq } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   526→            folderId: freshSource.id,
   527→            children: sourceChildren,
   528→            folderKey: freshSource.folderKey,
   529→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshSource.ipnsPrivateKey,
   530→            ipnsName: freshSource.ipnsName,
   531→            // Re-read sequence in case source === dest parent was updated above
   532→            sequenceNumber: freshSource.id === freshDest.id ? destSeq : freshSource.sequenceNumber,
   533→          });
   534→
   535→          return { destSeq, sourceSeq, destChildren, sourceChildren };
   536→        };
   537→
   538→        let result: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof performBatchMove>>;
   539→        try {
   540→          result = await performBatchMove();
   541→        } catch (err) {
   542→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   543→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   544→          try {
   545→            await Promise.all([
   546→              resyncFolder(sourceFolder.ipnsName, sourceFolder.id),
   547→              sourceFolder.id !== destFolder.id
   548→                ? resyncFolder(destFolder.ipnsName, destFolder.id)
   549→                : Promise.resolve(),
   550→            ]);
   551→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   552→            // Re-validate after resync — destination may have new children or depth changes
   553→            validateBatchMove();
   554→            result = await performBatchMove();
   555→          } catch (retryErr) {
   556→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   557→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   558→            }
   559→            throw retryErr;
   560→          } finally {
   561→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   562→          }
   563→        }
   564→
   565→        // Update local state
   566→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   567→        store.updateFolderChildren(sourceParentId, result.sourceChildren);
   568→        store.updateFolderChildren(destParentId, result.destChildren);
   569→        store.updateFolderSequence(destParentId, result.destSeq);
   570→        store.updateFolderSequence(sourceParentId, result.sourceSeq);
   571→
   572→        for (const item of items) {
   573→          if (item.type === 'folder') {
   574→            const movedFolder = useFolderStore.getState().folders[item.id];
   575→            if (movedFolder) {
   576→              store.setFolder({ ...movedFolder, parentId: destParentId });
   577→            }
   578→          }
   579→        }
   580→
   581→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   582→      } catch (err) {
   583→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to move items';
   584→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   585→        throw err;
   586→      }
   587→    },
   588→    []
   589→  );
   590→
   591→  /**
   592→   * Delete a file or folder.
   593→   *
   594→   * @param itemId - ID of item to delete
   595→   * @param itemType - 'file' or 'folder'
   596→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID
   597→   */
   598→  const handleDelete = useCallback(
   599→    async (itemId: string, itemType: 'file' | 'folder', parentId: string): Promise<void> => {
   600→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   601→      try {
   602→        const getParentFolder = () => {
   603→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   604→          return parentId === 'root'
   605→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   606→            : freshState.folders[parentId];
   607→        };
   608→
   609→        const parentFolder = getParentFolder();
   610→        if (!parentFolder) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   611→
   612→        const performDelete = async () => {
   613→          const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   614→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   615→          if (itemType === 'folder') {
   616→            const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   617→            await folderService.deleteFolder({
   618→              folderId: itemId,
   619→              parentFolderState: freshParent,
   620→              getFolderState: (id) => folders[id],
   621→              unpinCid: unpinFromIpfs,
   622→            });
   623→          } else {
   624→            await folderService.deleteFileFromFolder({
   625→              fileId: itemId,
   626→              parentFolderState: freshParent,
   627→            });
   628→          }
   629→        };
   630→
   631→        try {
   632→          await performDelete();
   633→        } catch (err) {
   634→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   635→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   636→          try {
   637→            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);
   638→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   639→            await performDelete();
   640→          } catch (retryErr) {
   641→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   642→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   643→            }
   644→            throw retryErr;
   645→          } finally {
   646→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   647→          }
   648→        }
   649→
   650→        if (itemType === 'folder') {
   651→          // Remove folder and all loaded descendants from local state
   652→          const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   653→          const removeRecursive = (folderId: string) => {
   654→            const node = store.folders[folderId];
   655→            if (node) {
   656→              for (const child of node.children) {
   657→                if (child.type === 'folder') removeRecursive(child.id);
   658→              }
   659→            }
   660→            store.removeFolder(folderId);
   661→          };
   662→          removeRecursive(itemId);
   663→        }
   664→
   665→        // Update parent's children (remove item) using fresh state
   666→        const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   667→        if (freshParent) {
   668→          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.filter((c) => c.id !== itemId);
   669→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   670→        }
   671→
   672→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   673→      } catch (err) {
   674→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete';
   675→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   676→        throw err;
   677→      }
   678→    },
   679→    []
   680→  );
   681→
   682→  /**
   683→   * Delete multiple files/folders in a single IPNS publish.
   684→   *
   685→   * Removes all items from parent's children array, collects CIDs to unpin,
   686→   * then publishes metadata and IPNS once for the entire batch.
   687→   *
   688→   * @param items - Array of { id, type } to delete
   689→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID (all items must share the same parent)
   690→   */
   691→  const handleDeleteItems = useCallback(
   692→    async (
   693→      items: Array<{ id: string; type: 'file' | 'folder' }>,
   694→      parentId: string
   695→    ): Promise<void> => {
   696→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   697→      try {
   698→        const getParentFolder = () => {
   699→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   700→          return parentId === 'root'
   701→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   702→            : freshState.folders[parentId];
   703→        };
   704→
   705→        const parentFolder = getParentFolder();
   706→        if (!parentFolder) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   707→
   708→        const itemIds = new Set(items.map((i) => i.id));
   709→
   710→        // Collect nested folder IDs to remove from store
   711→        // In v2, file children are FilePointers (no inline CID). File IPNS/TEE
   712→        // enrollments will expire naturally (24h IPNS lifetime). Phase 14 adds cleanup.
   713→        const folderIdsToRemove: string[] = [];
   714→
   715→        const collectFolderIds = (folderId: string) => {
   716→          folderIdsToRemove.push(folderId);
   717→          const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   718→          const folder = folders[folderId];
   719→          if (!folder) return;
   720→          for (const child of folder.children) {
   721→            if (child.type === 'folder') {
   722→              collectFolderIds(child.id);
   723→            }
   724→          }
   725→        };
   726→
   727→        for (const item of items) {
   728→          if (item.type === 'folder') {
   729→            collectFolderIds(item.id);
   730→          }
   731→        }
   732→
   733→        const performBatchDelete = async (): Promise<{
   734→          updatedChildren: typeof parentFolder.children;
   735→          newSequenceNumber: bigint;
   736→        }> => {
   737→          const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   738→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   739→
   740→          // Remove all items from parent's children in one pass
   741→          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.filter((c) => !itemIds.has(c.id));
   742→
   743→          // Single IPNS publish for the entire batch
   744→          const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   745→            folderId: freshParent.id,
   746→            children: updatedChildren,
   747→            folderKey: freshParent.folderKey,
   748→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshParent.ipnsPrivateKey,
   749→            ipnsName: freshParent.ipnsName,
   750→            sequenceNumber: freshParent.sequenceNumber,
   751→          });
   752→
   753→          return { updatedChildren, newSequenceNumber };
   754→        };
   755→
   756→        let updatedChildren: typeof parentFolder.children;
   757→        let newSequenceNumber = parentFolder.sequenceNumber;
   758→        try {
   759→          const result = await performBatchDelete();
   760→          updatedChildren = result.updatedChildren;
   761→          newSequenceNumber = result.newSequenceNumber;
   762→        } catch (err) {
   763→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   764→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   765→          try {
   766→            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);
   767→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   768→            const result = await performBatchDelete();
   769→            updatedChildren = result.updatedChildren;
   770→            newSequenceNumber = result.newSequenceNumber;
   771→          } catch (retryErr) {
   772→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   773→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   774→            }
   775→            throw retryErr;
   776→          } finally {
   777→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   778→          }
   779→        }
   780→
   781→        // Update local state -- remove all nested folders from store
   782→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   783→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   784→        store.updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   785→        for (const folderId of folderIdsToRemove) {
   786→          store.removeFolder(folderId);
   787→        }
   788→
   789→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   790→      } catch (err) {
   791→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete items';
   792→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   793→        throw err;
   794→      }
   795→    },
   796→    []
   797→  );
   798→
   799→  return {
   800→    ...state,
   801→    createFolder: handleCreate,
   802→    renameItem: handleRename,
   803→    moveItem: handleMove,
   804→    moveItems: handleMoveItems,
   805→    deleteItem: handleDelete,
   806→    deleteItems: handleDeleteItems,
   807→  };
   808→}
   809→

   722→export function isDescendantOf(
   723→  folderId: string,
   724→  potentialAncestorId: string,
   725→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
   726→): boolean {
   727→  let currentId: string | null = folderId;
   728→
   729→  while (currentId !== null) {
   730→    if (currentId === potentialAncestorId) return true;
   731→    const currentFolder: FolderNode | undefined = folders[currentId];
   732→    if (!currentFolder) break;
   733→    currentId = currentFolder.parentId;
   734→  }
   735→
   736→  return false;
   737→}

   739→/**
   740→ * Move a folder from one parent to another.
   741→ *
   742→ * Uses add-before-remove pattern to prevent data loss on failure.

   745→ * @param params.folderId - ID of folder to move
   746→ * @param params.sourceFolderState - Current parent folder

   750→ *         or attempting to move folder into itself/descendant

   752→export async function moveFolder(params: {
   753→  folderId: string;
   754→  sourceFolderState: FolderNode;
   755→  destFolderState: FolderNode;
   756→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>;
   757→}): Promise<void> {
   758→  // 1. Find folder in source
   759→  const folder = params.sourceFolderState.children.find(
   760→    (c) => c.type === 'folder' && c.id === params.folderId
   761→  ) as FolderEntry | undefined;
   762→
   763→  if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not found');
   764→
   765→  // 2. Check name collision in destination
   766→  const nameExists = params.destFolderState.children.some((c) => c.name === folder.name);
   767→  if (nameExists) throw new Error('An item with this name already exists in destination');
   768→
   769→  // 3. Check depth limit (20 levels max)
   770→  const destDepth = getDepth(params.destFolderState.id, params.folders);
   771→  const folderSubtreeDepth = calculateSubtreeDepth(params.folderId, params.folders);
   772→  if (destDepth + 1 + folderSubtreeDepth > MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH) {
   773→    throw new Error(`Cannot move: would exceed maximum folder depth of ${MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH}`);
   774→  }
   775→
   776→  // 4. Prevent moving folder into itself or its descendants
   777→  if (isDescendantOf(params.destFolderState.id, params.folderId, params.folders)) {
   778→    throw new Error('Cannot move folder into itself or its subfolder');
   779→  }
   780→
   781→  // 5. ADD to destination FIRST (add-before-remove pattern)
   782→  const destChildren: FolderChild[] = [

   786→      modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   787→    },
   788→  ];

   802→  );
   803→
   804→  await updateFolderMetadata({

   219→/**
   220→ * Update folder metadata and publish to IPNS (v2 format).
   221→ *
   222→ * Encrypts the metadata with the folder key, uploads to IPFS,
   223→ * and publishes an updated IPNS record pointing to the new CID.
   224→ *
   225→ * @param params.folderId - Folder being updated
   226→ * @param params.children - New children array (FolderChild[])
   227→ * @param params.folderKey - Decrypted AES-256 folder key
   228→ * @param params.ipnsPrivateKey - Decrypted Ed25519 IPNS private key
   229→ * @param params.ipnsName - IPNS name for this folder
   230→ * @param params.sequenceNumber - Current sequence number
   231→ * @param params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey - TEE-wrapped key (first publish only)
   232→ * @param params.keyEpoch - TEE key epoch (required with encryptedIpnsPrivateKey)
   233→ * @returns New CID and sequence number
   234→ */
   235→export async function updateFolderMetadata(params: {
   236→  folderId: string;
   237→  children: FolderChild[];
   238→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
   239→  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   240→  ipnsName: string;
   241→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
   242→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
   243→  keyEpoch?: number;
   244→}): Promise<{ cid: string; newSequenceNumber: bigint }> {
   245→  // 1. Create v2 folder metadata
   246→  const metadata: FolderMetadata = {
   247→    version: 'v2',
   248→    children: params.children,
   249→  };
   250→
   251→  // 2. Encrypt metadata with folder key
   252→  const encrypted = await encryptFolderMetadata(metadata, params.folderKey);
   253→
   254→  // 3. Upload to IPFS via backend relay
   255→  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(encrypted)], {
   256→    type: 'application/json',
   257→  });
   258→  const { cid } = await addToIpfs(blob);
   259→
   260→  // 4. Publish IPNS record pointing to new CID.
   261→  //    Send the pre-increment sequence as expectedSequenceNumber so the API can
   262→  //    detect concurrent modifications from other devices (conflict detection).
   263→  const newSeq = params.sequenceNumber + 1n;

   265→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.ipnsPrivateKey,
   266→    ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
   267→    metadataCid: cid,

   269→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   270→    keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
   271→    expectedSequenceNumber: params.sequenceNumber.toString(),
   272→  });
   273→
   274→  return { cid, newSequenceNumber: newSeq };
   275→}

   361→  // 2. Check for name collision
   362→  const nameExists = children.some((c) => c.name === params.newName && c.id !== params.folderId);
   363→  if (nameExists) throw new Error('An item with this name already exists');
   364→
   365→  // 3. Update name and modifiedAt
   366→  const folder = children[folderIndex] as FolderEntry;
   367→  children[folderIndex] = {
   368→    ...folder,
   369→    name: params.newName,
   370→    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   371→  };
   372→
   373→  // 4. Update parent folder metadata and publish
   374→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   375→    folderId: params.parentFolderState.id,
   376→    children,
   377→    folderKey: params.parentFolderState.folderKey,
   378→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.parentFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   379→    ipnsName: params.parentFolderState.ipnsName,
   380→    sequenceNumber: params.parentFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   381→  });
   382→}
   383→
   384→/**
   385→ * Delete a folder and all its contents recursively.
   386→ *
   387→ * For v2 metadata: collects fileMetaIpnsName values from FilePointer children
   388→ * (not CIDs, since CIDs live in per-file IPNS records, not folder metadata).
   389→ * Removes the folder from the parent's metadata, publishes the update.
   390→ * Returns the collected file IPNS names so the caller can resolve them for
   391→ * CID unpinning and TEE unenrollment.
   392→ *
   393→ * @param params.folderId - ID of folder to delete
   394→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder containing this folder
   395→ * @param params.getFolderState - Function to get folder state by ID (for recursion)
   396→ * @param params.unpinCid - Function to unpin a CID from IPFS
   397→ * @throws Error if folder not found
   398→ */
   399→export async function deleteFolder(params: {
   400→  folderId: string;
   401→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   402→  getFolderState: (id: string) => FolderNode | undefined;
   403→  unpinCid: (cid: string) => Promise<void>;
   404→}): Promise<string[]> {
   405→  // 1. Find folder in parent's children
   406→  const children = [...params.parentFolderState.children];
   407→  const folderIndex = children.findIndex((c) => c.type === 'folder' && c.id === params.folderId);
   408→
   409→  if (folderIndex === -1) throw new Error('Folder not found');
   410→
   411→  // 2. Recursively collect file IPNS names for cleanup
   412→  // In v2, file children are FilePointers with fileMetaIpnsName (no inline CID).
   413→  // The caller must resolve each fileMetaIpnsName to get the CID for unpinning.
   414→  const fileIpnsNames: string[] = [];
   415→  const collectFileIpnsNames = (folderId: string) => {
   416→    const folder = params.getFolderState(folderId);
   417→    if (!folder) return;
   418→
   419→    for (const child of folder.children) {
   420→      if (child.type === 'file') {
   421→        const filePointer = child as FilePointer;
   422→        if (filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName) {
   423→          fileIpnsNames.push(filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName);
   424→        }
   425→      } else if (child.type === 'folder') {
   426→        collectFileIpnsNames(child.id);
   427→      }
   428→    }
   429→  };
   430→
   431→  collectFileIpnsNames(params.folderId);
   432→
   433→  // 3. Remove folder from parent's children
   434→  children.splice(folderIndex, 1);
   435→
   436→  // 4. Update parent folder metadata and publish
   437→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   438→    folderId: params.parentFolderState.id,
   439→    children,
   440→    folderKey: params.parentFolderState.folderKey,
   441→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.parentFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   442→    ipnsName: params.parentFolderState.ipnsName,
   443→    sequenceNumber: params.parentFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   444→  });
   445→
   446→  // 5. File CID unpinning and TEE unenrollment deferred to caller
   447→  // The caller (useFolder hook) resolves fileMetaIpnsName -> CID for unpinning.
   448→  // TODO: Phase 14 should add batch unenrollment for orphaned file IPNS records.
   449→
   450→  return fileIpnsNames;
   451→}
   452→
   453→/**
   454→ * Delete a file from its parent folder (v2).
   455→ *
   456→ * Removes the FilePointer from the parent's metadata, publishes the update.
   457→ * Returns the fileMetaIpnsName so the caller can resolve it for CID unpinning
   458→ * and TEE unenrollment.
   459→ *
   460→ * Note: This handles folder metadata update. File CID unpinning is handled
   461→ * by the caller after resolving the file metadata.
   462→ *
   463→ * @param params.fileId - ID of file to delete
   464→ * @param params.parentFolderState - Parent folder containing this file
   465→ * @returns fileMetaIpnsName for caller to handle cleanup
   466→ * @throws Error if file not found
   467→ */
   468→export async function deleteFileFromFolder(params: {
   469→  fileId: string;
   470→  parentFolderState: FolderNode;
   471→}): Promise<{ fileMetaIpnsName: string | undefined }> {
   472→  // 1. Find file in parent's children
   473→  const children = [...params.parentFolderState.children];
   474→  const fileIndex = children.findIndex((c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === params.fileId);
   475→
   476→  if (fileIndex === -1) throw new Error('File not found');
   477→
   478→  // 2. Get fileMetaIpnsName for cleanup
   479→  const filePointer = children[fileIndex] as FilePointer;
   480→  const fileMetaIpnsName = filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName;
   481→
   482→  // 3. Remove file from parent's children
   483→  children.splice(fileIndex, 1);
   484→
   485→  // 4. Update parent folder metadata and publish
   486→  await updateFolderMetadata({
   487→    folderId: params.parentFolderState.id,
   488→    children,
   489→    folderKey: params.parentFolderState.folderKey,
   490→    ipnsPrivateKey: params.parentFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,
   491→    ipnsName: params.parentFolderState.ipnsName,
   492→    sequenceNumber: params.parentFolderState.sequenceNumber,
   493→  });
   494→
   495→  // 5. TEE unenrollment: no API endpoint available yet.
   496→  // TODO: Phase 14 should expose unenrollIpns via REST API.
   497→  if (fileMetaIpnsName) {
   498→    console.warn(
   499→      `File IPNS record ${fileMetaIpnsName} orphaned after deletion. TEE unenrollment deferred to Phase 14.`
   500→    );
   501→  }
   502→
   503→  return { fileMetaIpnsName };
   504→}
   505→

   507→ * Add a file to a folder after successful upload (v2).
   508→ *
   509→ * Creates a FilePointer from the file's IPNS record and adds it to the folder's

Prevent moving folder into itself or its descendants\n  if (isDescendantOf(params.destFolderState.id, params.folderId, params.folders)) {\n    throw new Error('Cannot move folder into itself or its subfolder');\n  }\n\n  // 5. ADD to destination FIRST (add-before-remove pattern)\n  const destChildren: FolderChild[] = [\n    ...params.destFolderState.children,\n    {\n      ...folder,\n      modifiedAt: Date.now(),\n    },\n  ];\n\n  const { newSequenceNumber: destSequenceNumber } = await updateFolderMetadata({\n    folderId: params.destFolderState.id,\n    children: destChildren,\n    folderKey: params.destFolderState.folderKey,\n    ipnsPrivateKey: params.destFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,\n    ipnsName: params.destFolderState.ipnsName,\n    sequenceNumber: params.destFolderState.sequenceNumber,\n  });\n\n  // 6. REMOVE from source AFTER destination confirmed\n  const sourceChildren = params.sourceFolderState.children.filter(\n    (c) => !(c.type === 'folder' && c.id === params.folderId)\n  );\n\n  const { newSequenceNumber: sourceSequenceNumber } = await updateFolderMetadata({\n    folderId: params.sourceFolderState.id,\n    children: sourceChildren,\n    folderKey: params.sourceFolderState.folderKey,\n    ipnsPrivateKey: params.sourceFolderState.ipnsPrivateKey,\n    ipnsName: params.sourceFolderState.ipnsName,\n    sequenceNumber: params.sourceFolderState.sequenceNumber,\n  });\n\n  return { destSequenceNumber, sourceSequenceNumber };\n}"
}

Please try again.');\n            }\n            throw retryErr;\n          } finally {\n            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();\n          }\n        }\n\n        if (itemType === 'folder') {\n          // Update local folder state name\n          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderName(itemId, newName);\n        }\n\n        // Update parent's children with new name using fresh state\n        const freshParent = getParentFolder();\n        if (freshParent) {\n          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.map((child) => {\n            if (child.id === itemId) {\n              return { ...child, name: newName, modifiedAt: Date.now() };\n            }\n            return child;\n          });\n          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);\n        }",
  "new_string": "        const performRename = async (): Promise<{ newSequenceNumber: bigint }> => {\n          const freshParent = getParentFolder();\n          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');\n          if (itemType === 'folder') {\n            return await folderService.renameFolder({\n              folderId: itemId,\n              newName,\n              parentFolderState: freshParent,\n            });\n          } else {\n            return await folderService.renameFile({\n              fileId: itemId,\n              newName,\n              parentFolderState: freshParent,\n            });\n          }\n        };\n\n        let renameResult: { newSequenceNumber: bigint };\n        try {\n          renameResult = await performRename();\n        } catch (err) {\n          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;\n          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');\n          try {\n            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);\n            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));\n            renameResult = await performRename();\n          } catch (retryErr) {\n            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {\n              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');\n            }\n            throw retryErr;\n          } finally {\n            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();\n          }\n        }\n\n        if (itemType === 'folder') {\n          // Update local folder state name\n          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderName(itemId, newName);\n        }\n\n        // Update parent's children with new name using fresh state\n        const freshParent = getParentFolder();\n        if (freshParent) {\n          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.map((child) => {\n            if (child.id === itemId) {\n              return { ...child, name: newName, modifiedAt: Date.now() };\n            }\n            return child;\n          });\n          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);\n        }\n\n        // Persist the new sequence number so subsequent operations use the correct value\n        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentId, renameResult.newSequenceNumber);"
}

Please try again.');\n            }\n            throw retryErr;\n          } finally {\n            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();\n          }\n        }\n\n        // Save the moved item BEFORE updating source children (otherwise it's already gone)\n        const freshSourceBeforeUpdate = getSourceFolder();\n        const movedItem = freshSourceBeforeUpdate?.children.find((c) => c.id === itemId);\n\n        if (itemType === 'folder') {\n          // Update the moved folder's parentId in local state\n          const freshFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;\n          const movedFolder = freshFolders[itemId];\n          if (movedFolder) {\n            useFolderStore.getState().setFolder({\n              ...movedFolder,\n              parentId: destParentId,\n            });\n          }\n        }\n\n        // Update source folder's children (remove item)\n        if (freshSourceBeforeUpdate) {\n          const updatedSourceChildren = freshSourceBeforeUpdate.children.filter(\n            (c) => c.id !== itemId\n          );\n          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(sourceParentId, updatedSourceChildren);\n        }\n\n        // Update dest folder's children (add item)\n        const freshDest = getDestFolder();\n        if (movedItem && freshDest) {\n          const updatedDestChildren = [\n            ...freshDest.children,\n            { ...movedItem, modifiedAt: Date.now() },\n          ];\n          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(destParentId, updatedDestChildren);\n        }",

Please try again.');\n            }\n            throw retryErr;\n          } finally {\n            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();\n          }\n        }\n\n        if (itemType === 'folder') {\n          // Remove folder and all loaded descendants from local state\n          const store = useFolderStore.getState();\n          const removeRecursive = (folderId: string) => {\n            const node = store.folders[folderId];\n            if (node) {\n              for (const child of node.children) {\n                if (child.type === 'folder') removeRecursive(child.id);\n              }\n            }\n            store.removeFolder(folderId);\n          };\n          removeRecursive(itemId);\n        }\n\n        // Update parent's children (remove item) using fresh state\n        const freshParent = getParentFolder();\n        if (freshParent) {\n          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.filter((c) => c.id !== itemId);\n          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);\n        }",
  "new_string": "        const performDelete = async (): Promise<{ newSequenceNumber: bigint }> => {\n          const freshParent = getParentFolder();\n          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');\n          if (itemType === 'folder') {\n            const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;\n            const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.deleteFolder({\n              folderId: itemId,\n              parentFolderState: freshParent,\n              getFolderState: (id) => folders[id],\n              unpinCid: unpinFromIpfs,\n            });\n            return { newSequenceNumber };\n          } else {\n            const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.deleteFileFromFolder({\n              fileId: itemId,\n              parentFolderState: freshParent,\n            });\n            return { newSequenceNumber };\n          }\n        };\n\n        let deleteResult: { newSequenceNumber: bigint };\n        try {\n          deleteResult = await performDelete();\n        } catch (err) {\n          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;\n          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');\n          try {\n            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);\n            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));\n            deleteResult = await performDelete();\n          } catch (retryErr) {\n            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {\n              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');\n            }\n            throw retryErr;\n          } finally {\n            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();\n          }\n        }\n\n        if (itemType === 'folder') {\n          // Remove folder and all loaded descendants from local state\n          const store = useFolderStore.getState();\n          const removeRecursive = (folderId: string) => {\n            const node = store.folders[folderId];\n            if (node) {\n              for (const child of node.children) {\n                if (child.type === 'folder') removeRecursive(child.id);\n              }\n            }\n            store.removeFolder(folderId);\n          };\n          removeRecursive(itemId);\n        }\n\n        // Update parent's children (remove item) using fresh state\n        const freshParent = getParentFolder();\n        if (freshParent) {\n          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.filter((c) => c.id !== itemId);\n          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);\n        }\n\n        // Persist the new sequence number so subsequent operations use the correct value\n        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentId, deleteResult.newSequenceNumber);"
}

## Summary: Sequence Number Tracking in CipherBox Desktop Rust Code

After thoroughly analyzing the desktop Rust codebase (`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/`), here's what I found regarding sequence number tracking after folder mutations:

### **Key Finding: Desktop Code Does NOT Have the Web Client Bug**

The desktop code **correctly updates its local sequence number cache** after publishing folder metadata. It avoids the bug that exists in the web client.

---

## 1. **How Sequence Numbers Are Stored**

### **PublishCoordinator (Central Authority)**
File: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/mod.rs` (lines 118-209)

The `PublishCoordinator` struct maintains **per-IPNS-name sequence number cache**:

```rust
pub struct PublishCoordinator {
    /// Per-IPNS-name sequence number cache (monotonically increasing).
    seq_cache: std::sync::Mutex<HashMap<String, u64>>,
    /// Per-IPNS-name publish locks to serialize concurrent publishes.
    publish_locks: std::sync::Mutex<HashMap<String, Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<()>>>>,
}
```

**Key Methods:**
- `resolve_sequence()` — Fetches from server OR returns cached value with max(resolved, cached)
- `record_publish()` — **Updates cache with the newly published sequence number**
- `update_cache()` — Monotonic-only update (never decreases)

### **Where It's Used**
- Stored in `CipherBoxFS.publish_coordinator: Arc<PublishCoordinator>` (shared across all FUSE operations and background threads)
- Sync daemon maintains a separate `HashMap<String, u64>` for polling (but doesn't use it for publish operations)

**This is NOT stored in:**
- Inode table (inode.rs) — no sequence field
- Metadata cache (cache.rs) — only stores FolderMetadata + CID, not sequence
- Folder metadata itself — v2 FolderMetadata schema has no sequence field
- AppState — no persistent sequence storage

---

## 2. **Sequence Number Updates After Publishing**

### **The Publishing Flow**

File: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/mod.rs` (lines 306-473)

When a folder mutation (rename, move, delete, create) is published:

```rust
fn spawn_metadata_publish(...) {
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let result = rt.block_on(async {
            // 1. Acquire per-folder publish lock (serialization)
            let lock = coordinator.get_lock(&ipns_name);
            let _guard = lock.lock().await;

            // 2. Resolve CURRENT sequence from server (with cache fallback)
            let seq = coordinator.resolve_sequence(&api, &ipns_name).await?;

            // 3. Create new IPNS record with seq+1
            let new_seq = seq + 1;
            let record = crate::crypto::ipns::create_ipns_record(
                &ipns_key_arr, &value, new_seq, 86_400_000,
            )?;

            // 4. Publish to server with expected_sequence_number = seq
            match crate::api::ipns::publish_ipns(&api, &req).await? {
                crate::api::ipns::PublishResult::Success => {
                    // ✓ CRITICAL: Update cache with new sequence number
                    coordinator.record_publish(&ipns_name, new_seq);  // LINE 362
                    log::info!("Background metadata publish succeeded");
                }
                crate::api::ipns::PublishResult::Conflict { current_sequence_number } => {
                    // ... handle conflict with retry ...
                    // On successful retry:
                    coordinator.record_publish(&ipns_name, retry_seq);  // LINE 430
                }
            }
        });
    });
}
```

**The sequence number is updated in TWO places:**
1. **Line 362** — `coordinator.record_publish(&ipns_name, new_seq);` on success
2. **Line 430** — `coordinator.record_publish(&ipns_name, retry_seq);` on successful retry after conflict

### **Per-Mutation Handlers**

Each mutation type calls `update_folder_metadata()` or queues metadata publish:

**File: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/write_ops.rs`**

- **handle_create()** (line 243): `fs.mutated_folders.insert(parent, now);` → triggers publish via debounce
- **handle_unlink()** (line 297): `fs.update_folder_metadata(parent);` → immediate publish
- **handle_mkdir()** (line 429-530): Spawns background publish, **calls `coordinator.record_publish()` on success**
- **handle_rmdir()** (line 603): `fs.update_folder_metadata(parent);` → immediate publish
- **handle_rename()** (lines 786-800): Calls `fs.update_folder_metadata()` for old/new parents

All of these funnel through `update_folder_metadata()` or `spawn_metadata_publish()`, which both call `coordinator.record_publish()`.

---

## 3. **mkdir-Specific Sequence Number Handling**

File: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/write_ops.rs` (lines 429-530)

**New folder initial publish (line 455-466):**
```rust
// New folder initial publish: sequence 0, no conflict check needed
let req = crate::api::ipns::IpnsPublishRequest {
    ipns_name: ipns_name_clone.clone(),
    record: record_b64,
    metadata_cid: initial_cid,
    encrypted_ipns_private_key: encrypted_ipns_for_tee,
    key_epoch: tee_key_epoch,
    expected_sequence_number: None,  // No conflict check for seq 0
};
match crate::api::ipns::publish_ipns(&api, &req).await? {
    crate::api::ipns::PublishResult::Success => {
        coordinator.record_publish(&ipns_name_clone, 0);  // ✓ Cache seq 0
        log::info!("New folder IPNS published: {}", ipns_name_clone);
    }
    crate::api::ipns::PublishResult::Conflict { .. } => {
        // Sequence 0 should never conflict
        log::warn!("Unexpected conflict on new folder IPNS publish for {}", ipns_name_clone);
    }
}
```

**Parent folder publish after mkdir (line 506-523):**
```rust
match crate::api::ipns::publish_ipns(&api, &parent_req).await? {
    crate::api::ipns::PublishResult::Success => {
        coordinator.record_publish(&parent_ipns_name, new_seq);  // ✓ Update parent cache
        if let Some(old) = parent_old_cid {
            let _ = crate::api::ipfs::unpin_content(&api, &old).await;
        }
        log::info!("Parent metadata published after mkdir");
    }
    crate::api::ipns::PublishResult::Conflict { current_sequence_number } => {
        log::warn!(
            "Conflict on parent publish after mkdir (expected seq {}, server has {})",
            seq, current_sequence_number
        );
        // Do NOT call record_publish() — let next debounced publish pick up fresh seq
    }
}
```

---

## 4. **Conflict Resolution with Sequence Number Merge**

File: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/mod.rs` (lines 372-462)

When a 409 Conflict is detected during publish:

```rust
crate::api::ipns::PublishResult::Conflict { current_sequence_number } => {
    // 1. Re-fetch fresh sequence from server
    let fresh_seq = coordinator.resolve_sequence(&api, &ipns_name).await?;

    // 2. Fetch remote metadata
    let remote_bytes = crate::api::ipfs::fetch_content(&api, &remote_cid).await?;
    let remote_metadata = crate::fuse::decrypt::decrypt_metadata_from_ipfs_public(
        &remote_bytes, &folder_key,
    )?;

    // 3. Merge local + remote (additive, preserves both)
    let merged_metadata = merge_folder_children(&metadata, remote_metadata);

    // 4. Retry with fresh sequence
    let retry_seq = fresh_seq + 1;
    let retry_record = crate::crypto::ipns::create_ipns_record(
        &ipns_key_arr, &retry_value, retry_seq, 86_400_000,
    )?;

    match crate::api::ipns::publish_ipns(&api, &retry_req).await? {
        crate::api::ipns::PublishResult::Success => {
            // ✓ CRITICAL: Update cache with retry sequence number
            coordinator.record_publish(&ipns_name, retry_seq);  // LINE 430
            
            log::info!(
                "Conflict resolved for {} after retry (seq {})",
                ipns_name, retry_seq
            );
        }
        crate::api::ipns::PublishResult::Conflict { .. } => {
            // Persistent conflict — do not loop, let next poll retry
            log::error!(
                "Persistent conflict for {} after retry. \
                Another device is actively modifying this folder. \
                Changes will resync on next polling cycle.",
                ipns_name
            );
        }
    }
}
```

**Key difference from web client:** The desktop code **calls `coordinator.record_publish()` TWICE**:
1. After successful initial publish (line 362)
2. After successful conflict retry (line 430)

This ensures the cache is always updated to the server's acknowledged sequence.

---

## 5. **Sync Daemon Polling**

File: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/sync/mod.rs` (lines 174-215)

The background sync daemon polls IPNS every 30 seconds using **sequence number comparison** (NOT CID):

```rust
async fn poll(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
    let root_ipns_name = self.root_ipns_name.read().await
        .clone()
        .ok_or_else(|| "Root IPNS name not available".to_string())?;

    // Resolve root folder IPNS
    let resolve_result = crate::api::ipns::resolve_ipns(&self.api, &root_ipns_name).await?;

    let new_seq = resolve_result.sequence_number.parse::<u64>()?;

    let cached_seq = self.cached_sequence_numbers
        .get(&root_ipns_name)
        .copied()
        .unwrap_or(0);

    if new_seq != cached_seq {
        log::info!(
            "IPNS change detected for root folder: seq {} -> {}",
            cached_seq,
            new_seq
        );
        self.cached_sequence_numbers.insert(root_ipns_name.clone(), new_seq);

        // Metadata cache has 30s TTL, so next FUSE readdir/lookup will fetch fresh
        log::info!(
            "Root folder metadata changed (CID: {}). Cache will refresh on next access.",
            resolve_result.cid
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

**Important:** The sync daemon maintains a **separate `cached_sequence_numbers`** HashMap for polling. This is NOT the same as `PublishCoordinator.seq_cache`. The sync daemon's cache is **read-only** for polling; it does NOT update after publishes (no `record_publish()` call).

---

## 6. **Comparison: Desktop vs Web Client**

| Aspect | Desktop | Web Client |
|--------|---------|-----------|
| **Sequence cache location** | `PublishCoordinator.seq_cache` (shared Arc) | `folderStore.sequenceNumbers` (Zustand state) |
| **Updates on publish success** | ✓ YES — `record_publish()` (line 362) | ✗ BUG — No update after publish |
| **Updates on conflict retry** | ✓ YES — `record_publish()` (line 430) | ✗ N/A (web has different conflict handling) |
| **Monotonic enforcement** | ✓ YES — `update_cache()` uses `max()` | ✗ No — can rollback if server has lower |
| **Per-IPNS serialization** | ✓ YES — publish locks prevent races | ✓ YES — `folderMutations` hook mutex |
| **Sync daemon polling** | ✓ Detects changes, triggers FUSE cache TTL | ✓ Detects changes, triggers Zustand refresh |

---

## 7. **Root Cause: Why Desktop Doesn't Have the Bug**

1. **Publishing is non-blocking** — Desktop spawns background threads for publish ops (lines 316-472)
2. **Coordinator tracks all publishes** — Every `spawn_metadata_publish()` call ends with `record_publish()`, making the cache update guaranteed
3. **Shared Arc reference** — All FUSE operations and background threads reference the same `PublishCoordinator` instance (no stale closures)
4. **No intermediate state** — The cache is updated AFTER server acknowledgment (success or retry), not before

---

## 8. **Potential Issue (If Any)**

The only minor gap is in the **sync daemon** (line 37-38):
```rust
/// Cached IPNS sequence numbers: ipns_name -> last known sequence_number.
cached_sequence_numbers: HashMap<String, u64>,
```

This is updated **only during polling** (line 204-205), NOT after publishes. So if a publish happens and the sync daemon hasn't polled yet, the daemon won't know about it. **But this is intentional** — the sync daemon's cache is for detecting REMOTE changes, not tracking local publishes. The `PublishCoordinator` is the authority for local publishes.

---

## **Files Involved**

| File | Purpose | Key Lines |
|------|---------|-----------|
| `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/mod.rs` | PublishCoordinator + spawn_metadata_publish | 118-209, 306-473 |
| `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/write_ops.rs` | Mutation handlers (mkdir, rename, unlink, etc) | 243, 297, 429-530, 603, 786-800 |
| `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/sync/mod.rs` | Background polling (separate cache) | 37-38, 174-215 |
| `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/registry/mod.rs` | Device registry (uses same pattern) | 90, 107 |

**Verdict:** The desktop Rust code **correctly updates sequence numbers after folder metadata publishes**, avoiding the bug present in the web client.

   986→    // Re-navigate to documents to drag the file
   987→    await navigateIntoFolder(documentsFolder);
   988→
   989→    // Actually move - we need to move from documents to workspace first
   990→    // since images folder is a sibling, not child
   991→    await navigateBack(); // Back to workspace
   992→
   993→    // Move file from documents (need to be in documents to drag from there)
   994→    await navigateIntoFolder(documentsFolder);
   995→
   996→    // File should be visible in documents
   997→    expect(await fileList.isItemVisible(fileToMove)).toBe(true);
   998→
   999→    // Use context menu to move to images folder (sibling folder)
  1000→    await fileList.rightClickItem(fileToMove);
```

> AGENT

Now I can confirm the issue. Test 4.13 calls `deleteItem(multiselectFolder)` which uses single-item `handleDelete` — this doesn't update the root folder's sequence number. Then test 5.1 tries to move a file from root to workspace, which calls `handleMove`. The `moveFile` service function calls `updateFolderMetadata` for the root folder with a stale sequence number → 409 → dialog stays open → test timeout.

My fix resolves this by making `handleDelete` update the sequence number after the delete operation, so by the time test 5.1 runs, the root folder's sequence is up-to-date.

Let me also look at the `deleteItem` helper to confirm it uses `handleDelete`.

{
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfix(web): update sequence numbers after single-item folder mutations\n\nhandleMove, handleRename, and handleDelete in useFolderMutations did not\ncall updateFolderSequence after successful IPNS publishes, leaving stale\nsequence numbers in the Zustand store. The next operation on the same\nfolder would send an outdated expectedSequenceNumber, triggering a 409\nconflict that the UI swallowed silently (e.g. move dialog staying open).\n\nChanges:\n- folder.service: moveFolder, moveFile, renameFolder, renameFile,\n  deleteFolder, deleteFileFromFolder now return new sequence numbers\n  from their updateFolderMetadata calls\n- useFolderMutations: handleMove, handleRename, handleDelete capture\n  returned sequences and call store.updateFolderSequence()\n\nThis aligns single-item operations with the batch operations\n(handleMoveItems, handleDeleteItems) which already tracked sequences.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit the fix"
}

> AGENT

Desktop E2E is still running. Here's a summary of what was done:

**Web E2E fix (PR #256):**
- **Root cause:** Single-item folder operations (`handleMove`, `handleRename`, `handleDelete`) didn't update sequence numbers in the Zustand store after successful IPNS publishes. This caused subsequent operations to send stale `expectedSequenceNumber` → 409 conflicts.
- **Specific failure path:** Test 4.13 (single delete) → root folder sequence stale → Test 5.1 (move file) → 409 → error caught silently → dialog stays open → timeout.
- **Fix:** Updated 6 `folder.service.ts` functions to return new sequence numbers, and 3 `useFolderMutations.ts` hooks to persist them via `updateFolderSequence()`. This aligns single-item operations with the batch operations that already tracked sequences correctly.

**Desktop E2E:** The explore agent confirmed the desktop Rust code does NOT have this bug — its `PublishCoordinator` correctly calls `record_publish()` after every successful IPNS publish. The desktop E2E run (22637255139) is still in progress against current `main`.

   103→# Bumps the server-side sequence number for a given IPNS name by publishing

> AGENT

Both fixes are now on PR #256:

1. **Web E2E fix** — Single-item folder mutations (`handleMove`, `handleRename`, `handleDelete`) now update sequence numbers in the Zustand store after successful IPNS publishes. This fixes test 5.1 "Move file via context menu" which failed because test 4.13's delete left the root folder's sequence stale → 409 on subsequent move.

2. **Windows Desktop E2E fix** — The conflict detection cleanup now wraps `Remove-Item` calls in `try/catch` blocks. On WinFSP FUSE mounts, `Remove-Item` can throw terminating .NET exceptions that `-ErrorAction SilentlyContinue` doesn't catch, causing the test suite to report 1 failure even though all actual test assertions passed.

PR: https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/256

Good — confirmed `unpinCid` is accepted in the params but never called. And there's a comment at line 448-450 explaining that cleanup is deferred to the caller. All 3 comments are about **pre-existing design gaps** that my PR didn't introduce. Let me triage:

**Triage summary:**
1. **Thread 1** (`unpinCid` unused in `deleteFolder`): Pre-existing — param was always unused. Not in scope for this PR.
2. **Thread 2** (`fileIpnsNames` dropped by folder-delete caller): Pre-existing — the single-item delete handler never used these before either. My PR only added extraction of `newSequenceNumber`.
3. **Thread 3** (`fileMetaIpnsName` dropped by file-delete caller): Same as above — pre-existing. Caller never consumed this value.

All 3 are **not applicable** to this PR's scope (sequence number tracking). Let me reply and resolve each thread.

Here are my findings on the three questions.

---

## Findings

### 1. Is there an existing helper for the "perform, catch conflict, resync, retry" pattern?

**No dedicated abstraction exists.** The identical conflict-retry pattern is copy-pasted **8 times** across two files:

- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts` -- 6 occurrences (lines 108-124, 228-242, 339-358, 556-576, 651-664, 783-798)
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts` -- 2 occurrences (lines 116-130, 272-286)

Every instance follows this exact skeleton:

```typescript
let result: T;
try {
  result = await perform();
} catch (err) {
  if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
  useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
  try {
    await resyncFolder(folder.ipnsName, folder.id);       // or resync multiple folders
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
    result = await perform();                               // single retry
  } catch (retryErr) {
    if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
      throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
    }
    throw retryErr;
  } finally {
    useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
  }
}
```

There **is** an existing `withRetry` in `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/upload.service.ts` (line 24), but it is a different pattern -- exponential backoff for network errors on IPFS uploads, not conflict-resync-retry. It is also not exported (it is a module-private `async function`), so it cannot be reused.

The existing `resyncFolder` helper in `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/folder-helpers.ts` (line 13) handles the "resolve IPNS, fetch metadata, update store" portion, but the surrounding try/catch/setConflict/clearConflict/jitter-delay/rethrow boilerplate is not abstracted.

**Reuse opportunity:** A generic `withConflictRetry<T>` function could be extracted into `folder-helpers.ts` (or a new `lib/conflict-retry.ts`). It would accept:
- `perform: () => Promise<T>` -- the mutation to attempt
- `resync: () => Promise<void>` -- the resync step (single folder or `Promise.all` for moves)
- Optional `preRetry?: () => void` -- for cases like batch move that call `validateBatchMove()` before retry

Two of the 8 call sites (single-folder move at line 339 and batch move at line 556) need to resync **two** folders instead of one, and the batch move additionally calls `validateBatchMove()` before retrying. These small variations could be handled by the `resync` and `preRetry` parameters.

### 2. Is `updateFolderSequence` called elsewhere in a different way?

`updateFolderSequence` is called in **12 distinct locations** across 4 files, and the pattern is always the same -- `useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(folderId, sequenceNumber)`:

| File | Lines | Context |
|------|-------|---------|
| `apps/web/src/hooks/folder-helpers.ts` | 27 | Inside `resyncFolder()` after IPNS resolve |
| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts` | 141, 262, 396, 399, 583, 584, 690, 804 | After each mutation's conflict-retry block |
| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts` | 144, 299, 472 | After file upload/add and lazy IPNS key migration |
| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileVersions.ts` | 133, 253 | After lazy IPNS key migration (fire-and-forget `.then()`) |
| `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/FileBrowser.tsx` | 152 | After sync/poll resolves IPNS |

The call signature and semantics are completely consistent everywhere. The store action itself is defined once at `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/stores/folder.store.ts` line 98. There is no alternative way of updating sequences -- it is always through this single Zustand action.

### 3. Is there a shared type for `{ newSequenceNumber: bigint }` or `{ destSequenceNumber: bigint; sourceSequenceNumber: bigint }`?

**No shared type exists.** These types are always written inline:

- `{ newSequenceNumber: bigint }` appears as an inline type annotation at:
  - `useFolderMutations.ts` lines 206, 224, 626, 647, 755
  - `useFileOperations.ts` lines 93, 252
  - `folder.service.ts` lines 244, 296, 354, 406, 473, 528, 595, 897 (as part of return type signatures)

- `{ destSequenceNumber: bigint; sourceSequenceNumber: bigint }` appears as an inline type annotation at:
  - `useFolderMutations.ts` lines 311-313, 335
  - `folder.service.ts` lines 759, 834

There is no `types/` directory under `apps/web/src/`. There is no type alias like `SequenceResult`, `MutationResult`, or similar anywhere in the web app source.

**Reuse opportunity:** Two named type aliases could be defined (e.g., in `folder.service.ts` or a shared types file):

```typescript
export type FolderMutationResult = { newSequenceNumber: bigint };
export type FolderMoveMutationResult = { destSequenceNumber: bigint; sourceSequenceNumber: bigint };
```

These would replace approximately 15 inline type annotations and make the return type contract explicit.

---

## Summary

| Question | Finding | Reuse Opportunity |
|----------|---------|-------------------|
| Conflict-retry wrapper | No abstraction exists; 8 copy-pasted instances of the same ~15-line pattern | High -- extract a `withConflictRetry<T>()` utility |
| `updateFolderSequence` usage | Consistent everywhere via `useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence()`; no divergent patterns | None needed (already consistent) |
| Shared sequence types | All inline, no named type aliases | Medium -- define `FolderMutationResult` and `FolderMoveMutationResult` types to replace ~15 inline annotations |
