## Code Review Findings

### Finding 1: Copy-paste conflict-retry scaffold (Medium -- worth noting, not urgent)

All three hooks (`handleRename`, `handleMove`, `handleDelete`) share an identical conflict-retry scaffold:

```typescript
let result: { ... };
try {
  result = await performX();
} catch (err) {
  if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
  useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
  try {
    await resyncFolder(/* ... */);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
    result = await performX();
  } catch (retryErr) {
    if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
      throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
    }
    throw retryErr;
  } finally {
    useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
  }
}
```

This pattern was already duplicated before this diff (the `main` branch has the same three `await performX()` / catch / resync / retry blocks, just without capturing the result). The new diff only adds `let result` and the assignment on both the happy path and the retry path. So this is **pre-existing duplication that the diff makes slightly worse** by adding the `result` capture in each copy.

A helper like `withConflictRetry<T>(perform: () => Promise<T>, resync: () => Promise<void>): Promise<T>` could absorb all six variations. However, the `handleMove` variant has a meaningfully different resync step (it resyncs two folders in parallel with a conditional), so a generic helper would need a `resync` callback anyway. This is worth extracting but not a blocking issue for this diff -- it is incremental growth of pre-existing duplication, not newly introduced duplication.

### Finding 2: Redundant destructure-rewrap in `performDelete` (Low -- genuine but cosmetic)

At lines 631-637 and 639-643 of `useFolderMutations.ts`:

```typescript
// folder branch
const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.deleteFolder({ ... });
return { newSequenceNumber };

// file branch
const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.deleteFileFromFolder({ ... });
return { newSequenceNumber };
```

This destructures `newSequenceNumber` from the service result and immediately re-wraps it in a new object literal with the same shape. This is **functionally redundant** for `deleteFileFromFolder` (which returns `{ fileMetaIpnsName, newSequenceNumber }`), but for `deleteFolder` (which returns `{ fileIpnsNames, newSequenceNumber }`) it serves as a **narrowing operation** -- stripping `fileIpnsNames` from the return type so `performDelete` returns a uniform `{ newSequenceNumber: bigint }`.

The intent is deliberate: make both branches return the same shape so the caller doesn't need to know which service was called. But the implementation could be cleaner:

```typescript
// Option A: Just return the service result and widen performDelete's return type
// Option B: Use a pick utility or just inline the type assertion
```

**Verdict:** The destructure-rewrap is not truly redundant; it is a type-narrowing adapter. But the fact that `fileIpnsNames` is silently discarded here (see Finding 3) means this narrowing may be masking a real problem.

### Finding 3: Silently discarded `fileIpnsNames` (Medium -- potential bug)

`folderService.deleteFolder()` returns `{ fileIpnsNames: string[]; newSequenceNumber: bigint }`. The `fileIpnsNames` array contains IPNS names of files nested inside the deleted folder that need cleanup (unpinning from IPFS, TEE unenrollment). The service itself documents this:

```typescript
// 5. File CID unpinning and TEE unenrollment deferred to caller
// The caller (useFolder hook) resolves fileMetaIpnsName -> CID for unpinning.
// TODO: Phase 14 should add batch unenrollment for orphaned file IPNS records.
```

Yet in `performDelete`, the `fileIpnsNames` are destructured away and never used. This was already the case on `main` (where `deleteFolder` was called with `await` and no result capture at all), so this is a **pre-existing issue**, not introduced by this diff. However, the diff had an opportunity to surface these values alongside `newSequenceNumber` and chose not to.

Similarly, `deleteFileFromFolder` returns `fileMetaIpnsName` which is also discarded.

This is not a regression -- both values were already being dropped -- but it is worth flagging as existing technical debt that becomes more visible now that the hooks explicitly type their return values.

### Finding 4: `let result` with definite assignment via try/catch (Low -- acceptable pattern)

The pattern:

```typescript
let renameResult: { newSequenceNumber: bigint };
try {
  renameResult = await performRename();
} catch (err) {
  // ...
  renameResult = await performRename(); // retry path
}
// use renameResult below
```

TypeScript's control flow analysis understands that if execution reaches the code after the try/catch block, `renameResult` must have been assigned (because every `catch` path either assigns it or re-throws). So there is no risk of using an uninitialized variable.

The alternative would be:

```typescript
const renameResult = await withConflictRetry(() => performRename(), () => resync());
```

But absent the helper function (Finding 1), the `let` + try/catch pattern is the straightforward way to do this. This is **acceptable** and not a code smell by itself.

### Finding 5: No concerns with the service-layer return type changes

The service functions (`renameFolder`, `renameFile`, `moveFolder`, `moveFile`, `deleteFolder`, `deleteFileFromFolder`) all get their `newSequenceNumber` from `updateFolderMetadata()`. The return types are appropriate:

- Single-folder operations return `{ newSequenceNumber }` -- clear and minimal.
- Move operations return `{ destSequenceNumber, sourceSequenceNumber }` -- correctly models that two IPNS records were updated with potentially different sequence numbers.
- Delete operations return the sequence number plus cleanup metadata (`fileIpnsNames`, `fileMetaIpnsName`) -- the return types are well-designed even though the caller currently discards the cleanup data.

### Summary

| # | Finding | Severity | Introduced by this diff? |
|---|---------|----------|--------------------------|
| 1 | Triplicated conflict-retry scaffold | Medium | No (pre-existing, marginally worse) |
| 2 | Destructure-rewrap in performDelete | Low | Yes, but serves as intentional type narrowing |
| 3 | `fileIpnsNames` / `fileMetaIpnsName` silently discarded | Medium | No (pre-existing) |
| 4 | `let result` before try/catch | Low | Yes, acceptable pattern |
| 5 | Service return types | N/A | Clean, no issues |

**Overall assessment:** The diff is a clean, mechanical change that threads sequence numbers through the existing mutation pipeline. It does not introduce new architectural problems. The main actionable items are (1) extracting the conflict-retry pattern into a shared helper (pre-existing debt) and (3) handling the discarded file cleanup IPNS names (also pre-existing debt). Neither is a reason to block this diff.

Here is the complete efficiency review.

---

## Efficiency Review: `fix/folder-mutation-seq-tracking`

### 1. `useFolderStore.getState()` -- Is it expensive?

**Finding: No issue.** `getState()` is a synchronous property read on the Zustand store object (line 72 in `folder.store.ts` -- it is the `get` parameter passed by Zustand's `create()`). It returns the current state reference with zero computation, zero copying. Calling it multiple times in quick succession (e.g., lines 140-156 in `useFolderMutations.ts` where `handleCreate` calls `updateFolderChildren`, `updateFolderSequence`, then `setFolder` sequentially) is effectively free. This is idiomatic Zustand usage outside of React render context, as documented in the project's own memory notes about stale closures.

### 2. Hot-path bloat from `updateFolderSequence`

**Finding: Negligible overhead.** `updateFolderSequence` (lines 98-109 of `folder.store.ts`) does:
- One object lookup (`state.folders[folderId]`)
- One shallow copy of the `folders` record (`...state.folders`)
- One shallow copy of the target folder node (`...folder, sequenceNumber`)

This is the exact same cost as every other store updater (`updateFolderChildren`, `updateFolderName`, etc.). The real cost in these mutation handlers is the `updateFolderMetadata` call which does AES-GCM encryption, IPFS upload, and IPNS publish -- three network round-trips. A synchronous object spread is ~microseconds compared to those ~seconds. No concern here.

### 3. Missed concurrency: sequential `updateFolderMetadata` in `moveFolder`/`moveFile`

**Finding: The sequential ordering is INTENTIONAL and CANNOT be parallelized.** This is the "add-before-remove" pattern, explicitly documented in the code:

- **`moveFolder`** (lines 783-815): Comment at line 783 says *"ADD to destination FIRST (add-before-remove pattern)"*, then line 801 says *"REMOVE from source AFTER destination confirmed"*.
- **`moveFile`** (lines 846-878): Same pattern -- add to dest first, remove from source after.

The rationale is crash safety: if the process fails between the two operations, the item exists in both places (duplicated but not lost) rather than in neither place (data loss). Running them in parallel would defeat this guarantee -- if the source removal succeeds but destination addition fails, the item is gone.

**However, there is a real bug in `moveFolder` and `moveFile` for the source===dest edge case.**

The `handleMoveItems` batch function (line 546) correctly handles the case where source and destination are the same folder:
```typescript
sequenceNumber: freshSource.id === freshDest.id ? destSeq : freshSource.sequenceNumber,
```

But the single-item `moveFolder` (line 812) and `moveFile` (line 875) in `folder.service.ts` do NOT have this guard:
```typescript
sequenceNumber: params.sourceFolderState.sequenceNumber,  // stale if source === dest!
```

If someone moves a file/folder within the same parent (e.g., reordering), the first `updateFolderMetadata` call bumps the sequence number on the dest (which is also the source). The second call then uses the *stale* `params.sourceFolderState.sequenceNumber` which is now behind by 1, triggering a **spurious 409 conflict**. This is not an efficiency issue per se, but it is a correctness bug that the batch variant already solved. This may be masked in practice if the UI disallows same-parent moves, but the service layer has no such guard.

### 4. N+1 re-render patterns from `updateFolderSequence`

**Finding: There is a double-render issue, but it is minor for this use case.**

Each Zustand `set()` call produces a new top-level state object, which triggers re-renders in any component subscribed to the affected slice. Looking at subscribers:

- `useFolderNavigation.ts:114` -- subscribes to `state.folders` (the whole record)
- `MoveDialog.tsx:184` -- subscribes to `state.folders` (the whole record)

When `handleRename` runs (lines 258-262), it calls:
1. `updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren)` -- creates new `folders` object, triggers re-render
2. `updateFolderSequence(parentId, renameResult.newSequenceNumber)` -- creates ANOTHER new `folders` object, triggers second re-render

Similarly, `handleMove` (lines 382-399) makes up to 5 sequential `set()` calls: `setFolder`, `updateFolderChildren` (source), `updateFolderChildren` (dest), `updateFolderSequence` (dest), `updateFolderSequence` (source). Each creates a new state object.

**Mitigation in practice:** React batches state updates within the same synchronous microtask. Since all these calls happen synchronously (no `await` between them), React 18+ automatic batching should collapse them into a single re-render. This means the multiple `set()` calls produce multiple Zustand state snapshots, but React only re-renders once at the end of the synchronous block.

**Potential improvement (not a bug):** The `updateFolderChildren` and `updateFolderSequence` calls could be merged into a single `set()` call that updates both `children` and `sequenceNumber` atomically. This would halve the number of intermediate state objects created and avoid any edge cases where a component reads between the two updates (seeing updated children but stale sequence). For instance:

```typescript
// Instead of two separate calls:
store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, children);
store.updateFolderSequence(parentId, seq);

// A combined action:
store.updateFolderWithSequence(parentId, children, seq);
```

This is an optimization opportunity, not a bug -- the current code is functionally correct because no component reads `sequenceNumber` during render (it is only read via `getState()` in mutation handlers).

### 5. `handleMoveItems` stores snapshot, then reads `getState()` again

**Finding: Correct but slightly wasteful.** At line 580-584:
```typescript
const store = useFolderStore.getState();
store.updateFolderChildren(sourceParentId, result.sourceChildren);
store.updateFolderChildren(destParentId, result.destChildren);
store.updateFolderSequence(destParentId, result.destSeq);
store.updateFolderSequence(sourceParentId, result.sourceSeq);
```

Then at line 588:
```typescript
const movedFolder = useFolderStore.getState().folders[item.id];
```

The `useFolderStore.getState()` on line 588 is necessary because the `store` variable captured on line 580 holds the *actions* (which are stable references) but the `folders` property would be stale after the `updateFolderChildren` calls modified it. This is correct behavior. The cost is one additional `getState()` call, which as established is effectively free.

---

## Summary

| # | Concern | Severity | Verdict |
|---|---------|----------|---------|
| 1 | `getState()` called multiple times | None | Free synchronous read; idiomatic |
| 2 | `updateFolderSequence` on hot path | None | Microseconds vs. seconds of network I/O |
| 3 | Sequential moves cannot be parallelized | None | Intentional add-before-remove for crash safety |
| 3b | **Single-item `moveFolder`/`moveFile` missing source===dest sequence guard** | **Medium (correctness bug)** | Batch variant handles it (line 546); single-item variants at `folder.service.ts:812` and `:875` do not |
| 4 | Multiple `set()` calls cause intermediate states | Low | React 18 batching mitigates; could merge into single updater for cleanliness |
| 5 | Extra `getState()` after store mutations | None | Necessary for fresh data; effectively free |

The only actionable finding is **3b**: `moveFolder()` and `moveFile()` in `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/folder.service.ts` (lines 806-813 and 869-876) will trigger a spurious 409 conflict when source and destination are the same folder, because they pass the stale pre-increment sequence number to the second `updateFolderMetadata` call. The batch variant `handleMoveItems` already has the fix at line 546. The single-item variants need the same `source.id === dest.id ? destSequenceNumber : source.sequenceNumber` guard.

> AGENT

Done. Here's the `/simplify` summary:

## Results

**3 agents found 1 actionable issue — fixed and pushed.**

**Fixed:** `moveFolder()` and `moveFile()` in `folder.service.ts` had a source===dest sequence bug. When moving within the same folder, the second `updateFolderMetadata` call used the stale pre-increment sequence number instead of the freshly incremented one, which would trigger a spurious 409 conflict. The batch variant already had this guard — aligned the single-item variants to match.

**Skipped (pre-existing, out of scope):**
- Conflict-retry scaffold duplicated 8x across hooks — valid extraction target but not introduced by this PR
- `fileIpnsNames`/`fileMetaIpnsName` silently discarded in delete handlers — pre-existing cleanup debt
- Unused `unpinCid` parameter — pre-existing

**No issues found with:**
- `getState()` call frequency (free synchronous read)
- `updateFolderSequence` overhead (microseconds vs seconds of network I/O)
- Sequential move operations (intentional add-before-remove for crash safety)
- React re-render batching (React 18 collapses synchronous `set()` calls)

   360→
   361→        // Save the moved item BEFORE updating source children (otherwise it's already gone)
   362→        const freshSourceBeforeUpdate = getSourceFolder();
   363→        const movedItem = freshSourceBeforeUpdate?.children.find((c) => c.id === itemId);
   364→
   365→        if (itemType === 'folder') {
   366→          // Update the moved folder's parentId in local state
   367→          const freshFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   368→          const movedFolder = freshFolders[itemId];
   369→          if (movedFolder) {
   370→            useFolderStore.getState().setFolder({
   371→              ...movedFolder,
   372→              parentId: destParentId,
   373→            });
   374→          }
   375→        }
   376→
   377→        // Update source folder's children (remove item)
   378→        if (freshSourceBeforeUpdate) {
   379→          const updatedSourceChildren = freshSourceBeforeUpdate.children.filter(
   380→            (c) => c.id !== itemId
   381→          );
   382→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(sourceParentId, updatedSourceChildren);
   383→        }
   384→
   385→        // Update dest folder's children (add item)
   386→        const freshDest = getDestFolder();
   387→        if (movedItem && freshDest) {
   388→          const updatedDestChildren = [
   389→            ...freshDest.children,
   390→            { ...movedItem, modifiedAt: Date.now() },
   391→          ];
   392→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(destParentId, updatedDestChildren);
   393→        }
   394→
   395→        // Persist the new sequence numbers so subsequent operations use the correct values
   396→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(destParentId, moveResult.destSequenceNumber);
   397→        useFolderStore
   398→          .getState()
   399→          .updateFolderSequence(sourceParentId, moveResult.sourceSequenceNumber);
   400→
   401→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   402→      } catch (err) {
   403→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to move';
   404→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   405→        throw err;
   406→      }
   407→    },
   408→    []
   409→  );
   410→
   411→  /**
   412→   * Move multiple files/folders to a destination in a single batch.
   413→   *
   414→   * Uses add-before-remove pattern. Publishes IPNS once for the destination
   415→   * and once for the source (2 total), regardless of how many items are moved.
   416→   *
   417→   * @param items - Array of { id, type } to move
   418→   * @param sourceParentId - Current parent folder ID (all items must share the same parent)
   419→   * @param destParentId - Destination parent folder ID
   420→   */
   421→  const handleMoveItems = useCallback(
   422→    async (
   423→      items: Array<{ id: string; type: 'file' | 'folder' }>,
   424→      sourceParentId: string,
   425→      destParentId: string
   426→    ): Promise<void> => {
   427→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   428→      try {
   429→        const getSourceFolder = () => {
   430→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   431→          return sourceParentId === 'root'
   432→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   433→            : freshState.folders[sourceParentId];
   434→        };
   435→        const getDestFolder = () => {
   436→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   437→          return destParentId === 'root'
   438→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   439→            : freshState.folders[destParentId];
   440→        };
   441→
   442→        const sourceFolder = getSourceFolder();
   443→        const destFolder = getDestFolder();
   444→
   445→        if (!sourceFolder || !destFolder) {
   446→          throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   447→        }
   448→
   449→        const itemIds = new Set(items.map((i) => i.id));
   450→        const now = Date.now();
   451→
   452→        // Validate batch move preconditions against current folder state.
   453→        // Must be called before each attempt (initial + retry after resync)
   454→        // because concurrent changes may introduce name collisions or depth violations.
   455→        const validateBatchMove = () => {
   456→          const currentSource = getSourceFolder();
   457→          const currentDest = getDestFolder();
   458→          if (!currentSource || !currentDest) {
   459→            throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   460→          }
   461→
   462→          const currentMovedChildren = currentSource.children.filter((c) => itemIds.has(c.id));
   463→          if (currentMovedChildren.length !== itemIds.size) {
   464→            throw new Error(
   465→              'One or more selected items no longer exist. Please refresh and retry.'
   466→            );
   467→          }
   468→          const batchNames = new Set<string>();
   469→          for (const child of currentMovedChildren) {
   470→            // Intra-batch duplicate name check
   471→            if (batchNames.has(child.name)) {
   472→              throw new Error(`Multiple selected items share the name "${child.name}"`);
   473→            }
   474→            batchNames.add(child.name);
   475→
   476→            // Name collision check against destination
   477→            const nameExists = currentDest.children.some((c) => c.name === child.name);
   478→            if (nameExists) {
   479→              throw new Error(`An item named "${child.name}" already exists in the destination`);
   480→            }
   481→
   482→            if (child.type === 'folder') {
   483→              // Prevent moving folder into itself or descendant
   484→              const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   485→              if (folderService.isDescendantOf(currentDest.id, child.id, folders)) {
   486→                throw new Error(`Cannot move "${child.name}" into itself or its subfolder`);
   487→              }
   488→
   489→              // Depth limit check
   490→              const destDepth = folderService.getDepth(currentDest.id, folders);
   491→              const subtreeDepth = folderService.calculateSubtreeDepth(child.id, folders);
   492→              if (destDepth + 1 + subtreeDepth > MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH) {
   493→                throw new Error(
   494→                  `Cannot move "${child.name}": would exceed maximum folder depth of ${MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH}`
   495→                );
   496→              }
   497→            }
   498→          }
   499→        };
   500→
   501→        validateBatchMove();
   502→
   503→        const performBatchMove = async (): Promise<{
   504→          destSeq: bigint;
   505→          sourceSeq: bigint;
   506→          destChildren: FolderNode['children'];
   507→          sourceChildren: FolderNode['children'];
   508→        }> => {
   509→          const freshSource = getSourceFolder();
   510→          const freshDest = getDestFolder();
   511→          if (!freshSource || !freshDest) throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   512→
   513→          // Rebuild moved children from fresh source state
   514→          const freshMoved = freshSource.children.filter((c) => itemIds.has(c.id));
   515→          if (freshMoved.length !== itemIds.size) {
   516→            throw new Error(
   517→              'One or more selected items no longer exist. Please refresh and retry.'
   518→            );
   519→          }
   520→
   521→          // ADD all to destination FIRST (add-before-remove pattern)
   522→          const destChildren = [
   523→            ...freshDest.children,
   524→            ...freshMoved.map((c) => ({ ...c, modifiedAt: now })),
   525→          ];
   526→
   527→          const { newSequenceNumber: destSeq } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   528→            folderId: freshDest.id,
   529→            children: destChildren,
   530→            folderKey: freshDest.folderKey,
   531→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshDest.ipnsPrivateKey,
   532→            ipnsName: freshDest.ipnsName,
   533→            sequenceNumber: freshDest.sequenceNumber,
   534→          });
   535→
   536→          // REMOVE all from source AFTER destination confirmed
   537→          const sourceChildren = freshSource.children.filter((c) => !itemIds.has(c.id));
   538→
   539→          const { newSequenceNumber: sourceSeq } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   540→            folderId: freshSource.id,
   541→            children: sourceChildren,
   542→            folderKey: freshSource.folderKey,
   543→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshSource.ipnsPrivateKey,
   544→            ipnsName: freshSource.ipnsName,
   545→            // Re-read sequence in case source === dest parent was updated above
   546→            sequenceNumber: freshSource.id === freshDest.id ? destSeq : freshSource.sequenceNumber,
   547→          });
   548→
   549→          return { destSeq, sourceSeq, destChildren, sourceChildren };
   550→        };
   551→
   552→        let result: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof performBatchMove>>;
   553→        try {
   554→          result = await performBatchMove();
   555→        } catch (err) {
   556→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   557→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   558→          try {
   559→            await Promise.all([
   560→              resyncFolder(sourceFolder.ipnsName, sourceFolder.id),
   561→              sourceFolder.id !== destFolder.id
   562→                ? resyncFolder(destFolder.ipnsName, destFolder.id)
   563→                : Promise.resolve(),
   564→            ]);
   565→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   566→            // Re-validate after resync — destination may have new children or depth changes
   567→            validateBatchMove();
   568→            result = await performBatchMove();
   569→          } catch (retryErr) {
   570→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   571→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   572→            }
   573→            throw retryErr;
   574→          } finally {
   575→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   576→          }
   577→        }
   578→
   579→        // Update local state
   580→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   581→        store.updateFolderChildren(sourceParentId, result.sourceChildren);
   582→        store.updateFolderChildren(destParentId, result.destChildren);
   583→        store.updateFolderSequence(destParentId, result.destSeq);
   584→        store.updateFolderSequence(sourceParentId, result.sourceSeq);
   585→
   586→        for (const item of items) {
   587→          if (item.type === 'folder') {
   588→            const movedFolder = useFolderStore.getState().folders[item.id];
   589→            if (movedFolder) {
   590→              store.setFolder({ ...movedFolder, parentId: destParentId });
   591→            }
   592→          }
   593→        }
   594→
   595→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   596→      } catch (err) {
   597→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to move items';
   598→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   599→        throw err;
   600→      }
   601→    },
   602→    []
   603→  );
   604→
   605→  /**
   606→   * Delete a file or folder.
   607→   *
   608→   * @param itemId - ID of item to delete
   609→   * @param itemType - 'file' or 'folder'
   610→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID
   611→   */
   612→  const handleDelete = useCallback(
   613→    async (itemId: string, itemType: 'file' | 'folder', parentId: string): Promise<void> => {
   614→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   615→      try {
   616→        const getParentFolder = () => {
   617→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   618→          return parentId === 'root'
   619→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   620→            : freshState.folders[parentId];
   621→        };
   622→
   623→        const parentFolder = getParentFolder();
   624→        if (!parentFolder) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   625→
   626→        const performDelete = async (): Promise<{ newSequenceNumber: bigint }> => {
   627→          const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   628→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   629→          if (itemType === 'folder') {
   630→            const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   631→            const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.deleteFolder({
   632→              folderId: itemId,
   633→              parentFolderState: freshParent,
   634→              getFolderState: (id) => folders[id],
   635→              unpinCid: unpinFromIpfs,
   636→            });
   637→            return { newSequenceNumber };
   638→          } else {
   639→            const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.deleteFileFromFolder({
   640→              fileId: itemId,
   641→              parentFolderState: freshParent,
   642→            });
   643→            return { newSequenceNumber };
   644→          }
   645→        };
   646→
   647→        let deleteResult: { newSequenceNumber: bigint };
   648→        try {
   649→          deleteResult = await performDelete();
   650→        } catch (err) {
   651→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   652→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   653→          try {
   654→            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);
   655→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   656→            deleteResult = await performDelete();
   657→          } catch (retryErr) {
   658→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   659→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   660→            }
   661→            throw retryErr;
   662→          } finally {
   663→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   664→          }
   665→        }
   666→
   667→        if (itemType === 'folder') {
   668→          // Remove folder and all loaded descendants from local state
   669→          const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   670→          const removeRecursive = (folderId: string) => {
   671→            const node = store.folders[folderId];
   672→            if (node) {
   673→              for (const child of node.children) {
   674→                if (child.type === 'folder') removeRecursive(child.id);
   675→              }
   676→            }
   677→            store.removeFolder(folderId);
   678→          };
   679→          removeRecursive(itemId);
   680→        }
   681→
   682→        // Update parent's children (remove item) using fresh state
   683→        const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   684→        if (freshParent) {
   685→          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.filter((c) => c.id !== itemId);
   686→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   687→        }
   688→
   689→        // Persist the new sequence number so subsequent operations use the correct value
   690→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentId, deleteResult.newSequenceNumber);
   691→
   692→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   693→      } catch (err) {
   694→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete';
   695→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   696→        throw err;
   697→      }
   698→    },
   699→    []
   700→  );
   701→
   702→  /**
   703→   * Delete multiple files/folders in a single IPNS publish.
   704→   *
   705→   * Removes all items from parent's children array, collects CIDs to unpin,
   706→   * then publishes metadata and IPNS once for the entire batch.
   707→   *
   708→   * @param items - Array of { id, type } to delete
   709→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID (all items must share the same parent)
   710→   */
   711→  const handleDeleteItems = useCallback(
   712→    async (
   713→      items: Array<{ id: string; type: 'file' | 'folder' }>,
   714→      parentId: string
   715→    ): Promise<void> => {
   716→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   717→      try {
   718→        const getParentFolder = () => {
   719→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   720→          return parentId === 'root'
   721→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   722→            : freshState.folders[parentId];
   723→        };
   724→
   725→        const parentFolder = getParentFolder();
   726→        if (!parentFolder) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   727→
   728→        const itemIds = new Set(items.map((i) => i.id));
   729→
   730→        // Collect nested folder IDs to remove from store
   731→        // In v2, file children are FilePointers (no inline CID). File IPNS/TEE
   732→        // enrollments will expire naturally (24h IPNS lifetime). Phase 14 adds cleanup.
   733→        const folderIdsToRemove: string[] = [];
   734→
   735→        const collectFolderIds = (folderId: string) => {
   736→          folderIdsToRemove.push(folderId);
   737→          const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   738→          const folder = folders[folderId];
   739→          if (!folder) return;
   740→          for (const child of folder.children) {
   741→            if (child.type === 'folder') {
   742→              collectFolderIds(child.id);
   743→            }
   744→          }
   745→        };
   746→
   747→        for (const item of items) {
   748→          if (item.type === 'folder') {
   749→            collectFolderIds(item.id);
   750→          }
   751→        }
   752→
   753→        const performBatchDelete = async (): Promise<{
   754→          updatedChildren: typeof parentFolder.children;
   755→          newSequenceNumber: bigint;
   756→        }> => {
   757→          const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   758→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   759→
   760→          // Remove all items from parent's children in one pass
   761→          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.filter((c) => !itemIds.has(c.id));
   762→
   763→          // Single IPNS publish for the entire batch
   764→          const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   765→            folderId: freshParent.id,
   766→            children: updatedChildren,
   767→            folderKey: freshParent.folderKey,
   768→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshParent.ipnsPrivateKey,
   769→            ipnsName: freshParent.ipnsName,
   770→            sequenceNumber: freshParent.sequenceNumber,
   771→          });
   772→
   773→          return { updatedChildren, newSequenceNumber };
   774→        };
   775→
   776→        let updatedChildren: typeof parentFolder.children;
   777→        let newSequenceNumber = parentFolder.sequenceNumber;
   778→        try {
   779→          const result = await performBatchDelete();
   780→          updatedChildren = result.updatedChildren;
   781→          newSequenceNumber = result.newSequenceNumber;
   782→        } catch (err) {
   783→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   784→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   785→          try {
   786→            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);
   787→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   788→            const result = await performBatchDelete();
   789→            updatedChildren = result.updatedChildren;
   790→            newSequenceNumber = result.newSequenceNumber;
   791→          } catch (retryErr) {
   792→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   793→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   794→            }
   795→            throw retryErr;
   796→          } finally {
   797→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   798→          }
   799→        }
   800→
   801→        // Update local state -- remove all nested folders from store
   802→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   803→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   804→        store.updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   805→        for (const folderId of folderIdsToRemove) {
   806→          store.removeFolder(folderId);
   807→        }
   808→
   809→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   810→      } catch (err) {
   811→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete items';
   812→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   813→        throw err;
   814→      }
   815→    },
   816→    []
   817→  );
   818→
   819→  return {
   820→    ...state,
   821→    createFolder: handleCreate,
   822→    renameItem: handleRename,
   823→    moveItem: handleMove,
   824→    moveItems: handleMoveItems,
   825→    deleteItem: handleDelete,
   826→    deleteItems: handleDeleteItems,
   827→  };
   828→}
   829→

   101→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   102→
   103→          return folderService.addFileToFolder({
   104→            parentFolderState: freshParent,
   105→            fileId,
   106→            name: fileData.originalName,
   107→            fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
   108→            ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   109→          });
   110→        };
   111→
   112→        let addResult: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof performAddFile>>;
   113→        try {
   114→          addResult = await performAddFile();
   115→        } catch (err) {
   116→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   117→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   118→          try {
   119→            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);
   120→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   121→            addResult = await performAddFile();
   122→          } catch (retryErr) {
   123→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   124→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   125→            }
   126→            throw retryErr;
   127→          } finally {
   128→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   129→          }
   130→        }
   131→
   132→        const { filePointer, newSequenceNumber } = addResult;
   133→
   134→        // 3. Update local state with new child and sequence number
   135→        const freshFolders2 = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   136→        const freshVault2 = useVaultStore.getState();
   137→        const freshParent2 =
   138→          parentId === 'root'
   139→            ? getRootFolderState(freshVault2, freshFolders2)
   140→            : freshFolders2[parentId];
   141→        const updatedChildren: FolderChild[] = [...(freshParent2?.children ?? []), filePointer];
   142→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   143→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   144→        store.updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   145→
   146→        // 4. Post-upload: re-wrap file key for share recipients (fire-and-forget)
   147→        // Unwrap the fileKey from the owner-wrapped key, then delegate to reWrapForRecipients
   148→        (async () => {
   149→          try {
   150→            const authState = useAuthStore.getState();
   151→            if (!authState.vaultKeypair) return;
   152→            const fileKey = await unwrapKey(
   153→              hexToBytes(fileData.wrappedKey),
   154→              authState.vaultKeypair.privateKey
   155→            );
   156→            try {
   157→              await reWrapForRecipients({
   158→                folderIpnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   159→                folders: useFolderStore.getState().folders,
   160→                currentFolderId: parentFolder.id,
   161→                newItems: [{ keyType: 'file', itemId: fileId, plaintextKey: fileKey }],
   162→              });
   163→            } finally {
   164→              fileKey.fill(0);
   165→            }
   166→          } catch (err) {
   167→            console.warn('[share] Post-upload file re-wrapping failed:', err);
   168→          }
   169→        })();
   170→
   171→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   172→        return filePointer;
   173→      } catch (err) {
   174→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to add file';
   175→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   176→        throw err;
   177→      }
   178→    },
   179→    []
   180→  );
   181→
   182→  /**
   183→   * Add multiple files to a folder after upload (v2 batch).
   184→   *
   185→   * Creates per-file IPNS metadata for each file, then registers all
   186→   * FilePointers via a single batch IPNS publish.
   187→   *
   188→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID ('root' or folder UUID)
   189→   * @param filesData - Array of uploaded file data from upload service
   190→   * @returns Created file pointers
   191→   */
   192→  const handleAddFiles = useCallback(
   193→    async (
   194→      parentId: string,
   195→      filesData: Array<{
   196→        cid: string;
   197→        wrappedKey: string;
   198→        iv: string;
   199→        originalName: string;
   200→        originalSize: number;
   201→        mimeType?: string;
   202→        encryptionMode?: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
   203→      }>
   204→    ): Promise<FilePointer[]> => {
   205→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   206→      try {
   207→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   208→        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
   209→        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   210→
   211→        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
   212→          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
   213→        }
   214→
   215→        // Get parent folder state
   216→        const parentFolder =
   217→          parentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[parentId];
   218→
   219→        if (!parentFolder) {
   220→          throw new Error('Parent folder not found or vault not initialized');
   221→        }
   222→
   223→        // 1. Create per-file IPNS metadata for each file
   224→        const userPublicKey = auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey;
   225→        const filesWithRecords = await Promise.all(
   226→          filesData.map(async (f) => {
   227→            const fileId = crypto.randomUUID();
   228→            const { ipnsRecord, ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } = await createFileMetadata({
   229→              fileId,
   230→              cid: f.cid,
   231→              fileKeyEncrypted: f.wrappedKey,
   232→              fileIv: f.iv,
   233→              size: f.originalSize,
   234→              mimeType: f.mimeType ?? 'application/octet-stream',
   235→              folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   236→              userPublicKey,
   237→              encryptionMode: f.encryptionMode,
   238→            });
   239→            return {
   240→              fileId,
   241→              name: f.originalName,
   242→              fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
   243→              ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   244→            };
   245→          })
   246→        );
   247→
   248→        // 2. Register FilePointers in folder (batch publishes all IPNS records)
   249→        //    On 409 conflict, re-sync the parent folder and retry once.
   250→        const performAddFiles = async (): Promise<{
   251→          filePointers: FilePointer[];
   252→          newSequenceNumber: bigint;
   253→        }> => {
   254→          const freshFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   255→          const freshVault = useVaultStore.getState();
   256→          const freshParent =
   257→            parentId === 'root'
   258→              ? getRootFolderState(freshVault, freshFolders)
   259→              : freshFolders[parentId];
   260→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   261→
   262→          return folderService.addFilesToFolder({
   263→            parentFolderState: freshParent,
   264→            files: filesWithRecords,
   265→          });
   266→        };
   267→
   268→        let addResult: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof performAddFiles>>;
   269→        try {
   270→          addResult = await performAddFiles();
   271→        } catch (err) {
   272→          if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
   273→          useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
   274→          try {
   275→            await resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id);
   276→            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
   277→            addResult = await performAddFiles();
   278→          } catch (retryErr) {
   279→            if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
   280→              throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
   281→            }
   282→            throw retryErr;
   283→          } finally {
   284→            useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
   285→          }
   286→        }
   287→
   288→        const { filePointers, newSequenceNumber } = addResult;
   289→
   290→        // 3. Update local state with new children and sequence number
   291→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   292→        const freshFolders2 = store.folders;
   293→        const freshVault2 = useVaultStore.getState();
   294→        const freshParent2 =
   295→          parentId === 'root'
   296→            ? getRootFolderState(freshVault2, freshFolders2)
   297→            : freshFolders2[parentId];
   298→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, [...(freshParent2?.children ?? []), ...filePointers]);
   299→        store.updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   300→
   301→        // 4. Post-upload: re-wrap file keys for share recipients (fire-and-forget)
   302→        (async () => {
   303→          const newItems: Array<{
   304→            keyType: 'file' | 'folder';
   305→            itemId: string;
   306→            plaintextKey: Uint8Array;
   307→          }> = [];
   308→          try {
   309→            const authState = useAuthStore.getState();
   310→            if (!authState.vaultKeypair) return;
   311→            const privateKey = authState.vaultKeypair.privateKey;
   312→            for (let i = 0; i < filesData.length; i++) {
   313→              const fileKey = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(filesData[i].wrappedKey), privateKey);
   314→              newItems.push({
   315→                keyType: 'file',
   316→                itemId: filesWithRecords[i].fileId,
   317→                plaintextKey: fileKey,
   318→              });
   319→            }
   320→            await reWrapForRecipients({
   321→              folderIpnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   322→              folders: useFolderStore.getState().folders,
   323→              currentFolderId: parentFolder.id,
   324→              newItems,
   325→            });
   326→          } catch (err) {
   327→            console.warn('[share] Post-upload batch file re-wrapping failed:', err);
   328→          } finally {
   329→            for (const item of newItems) {
   330→              item.plaintextKey.fill(0);
   331→            }
   332→          }
   333→        })();
   334→
   335→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   336→        return filePointers;
   337→      } catch (err) {
   338→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to add files';
   339→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   340→        throw err;
   341→      }
   342→    },
   343→    []
   344→  );
   345→
   346→  /**
   347→   * Update a file's content in-place (v2: re-encrypt, update file IPNS, folder untouched).
   348→   *
   349→   * Old CIDs are preserved as version history (VER-01). Only pruned versions
   350→   * (excess beyond max 10) have their CIDs unpinned.
   351→   *
   352→   * NOTE: No conflict detection here -- handleUpdateFile publishes only the
   353→   * per-file IPNS record. Folder metadata is NOT touched, so no 409 is possible.
   354→   *
   355→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID ('root' or folder UUID)
   356→   * @param fileData - New file data after re-encryption
   357→   * @returns Resolves when the update is complete
   358→   */
   359→  const handleUpdateFile = useCallback(
   360→    async (
   361→      parentId: string,
   362→      fileData: {
   363→        fileId: string;
   364→        newCid: string;
   365→        newFileKeyEncrypted: string;
   366→        newFileIv: string;
   367→        newSize: number;
   368→        forceVersion?: boolean; // true for web re-upload, false/undefined for text editor
   369→      }
   370→    ): Promise<void> => {
   371→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   372→      try {
   373→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   374→        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
   375→        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   376→
   377→        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
   378→          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
   379→        }
   380→
   381→        // Get parent folder state
   382→        const parentFolder =
   383→          parentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[parentId];
   384→
   385→        if (!parentFolder) {
   386→          throw new Error('Parent folder not found or vault not initialized');
   387→        }
   388→
   389→        // 1. Find the FilePointer in parent's children
   390→        const filePointer = parentFolder.children.find(
   391→          (c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === fileData.fileId
   392→        ) as FilePointer | undefined;
   393→
   394→        if (!filePointer) {
   395→          throw new Error('File not found in folder');
   396→        }
   397→
   398→        // 2. Resolve current file metadata from IPNS
   399→        const { metadata: currentMetadata } = await resolveFileMetadata(
   400→          filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
   401→          parentFolder.folderKey
   402→        );
   403→
   404→        // 3. Decrypt the file IPNS private key from FilePointer (or HKDF fallback)
   405→        const { privateKey: fileIpnsPrivateKey, migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } =
   406→          await getFileIpnsPrivateKey(
   407→            filePointer,
   408→            auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   409→            auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey
   410→          );
   411→
   412→        // 4. Determine whether to create a version entry
   413→        const createVersion = shouldCreateVersion(currentMetadata, fileData.forceVersion ?? false);
   414→
   415→        let ipnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
   416→        let prunedCids: string[];
   417→        try {
   418→          // 5. Update file metadata and publish new IPNS record
   419→          ({ ipnsRecord, prunedCids } = await updateFileMetadata({
   420→            fileIpnsPrivateKey,
   421→            fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
   422→            folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   423→            currentMetadata,
   424→            updates: {
   425→              cid: fileData.newCid,
   426→              fileKeyEncrypted: fileData.newFileKeyEncrypted,
   427→              fileIv: fileData.newFileIv,
   428→              size: fileData.newSize,
   429→            },
   430→            createVersion,
   431→          }));
   432→        } finally {
   433→          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
   434→        }
   435→
   436→        // 6. Publish only the file IPNS record (folder metadata untouched!)
   437→        await folderService.replaceFileInFolder({
   438→          fileId: fileData.fileId,
   439→          fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
   440→          parentFolderState: parentFolder,
   441→        });
   442→
   443→        // 7. Update local state -- touch modifiedAt and persist migrated IPNS key if applicable
   444→        const updatedChildren = parentFolder.children.map((child) => {
   445→          if (child.type === 'file' && child.id === fileData.fileId) {
   446→            return {
   447→              ...child,
   448→              modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   449→              ...(migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted
   450→                ? { ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted }
   451→                : {}),
   452→            };
   453→          }
   454→          return child;
   455→        });
   456→
   457→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   458→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   459→
   460→        // 7b. Lazy migration: persist wrapped IPNS key to folder metadata on IPFS
   461→        if (migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted) {
   462→          folderService
   463→            .updateFolderMetadata({
   464→              folderId: parentFolder.id,
   465→              children: updatedChildren,
   466→              folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   467→              ipnsPrivateKey: parentFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,
   468→              ipnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   469→              sequenceNumber: parentFolder.sequenceNumber,
   470→            })
   471→            .then(({ newSequenceNumber }) => {
   472→              useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   473→            })
   474→            .catch((err) => {
   475→              console.warn('Lazy IPNS key migration: folder re-publish failed, will retry:', err);
   476→            });
   477→        }
   478→
   479→        // 8. Only unpin CIDs of pruned versions (excess beyond max 10)
   480→        // Old CIDs stay pinned as version history (VER-01)
   481→        for (const prunedCid of prunedCids) {
   482→          unpinFromIpfs(prunedCid).catch(() => {});
   483→        }
   484→
   485→        // Refresh quota
   486→        useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();
   487→
   488→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   489→      } catch (err) {
   490→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update file';
   491→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   492→        throw err;
   493→      }
   494→    },
   495→    []
   496→  );
   497→
   498→  return {
   499→    ...state,
   500→    addFile: handleAddFile,
   501→    addFiles: handleAddFiles,
   502→    updateFile: handleUpdateFile,
   503→  };
   504→}
   505→

     1→import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
     2→import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';
     3→import type { FolderNode } from '../stores/folder.store';
     4→import * as folderService from '../services/folder.service';
     5→import { resolveIpnsRecord } from '../services/ipns.service';
     6→
     7→/**
     8→ * Re-sync a specific folder after a 409 conflict.
     9→ *
    10→ * Resolves the folder's IPNS name to get fresh CID + sequenceNumber,
    11→ * fetches and decrypts the metadata, and updates the folder store.
    12→ */
    13→export async function resyncFolder(folderIpnsName: string, folderId: string): Promise<void> {
    14→  const store = useFolderStore.getState();
    15→  const folderNode = store.folders[folderId];
    16→  if (!folderNode) return;
    17→
    18→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderIpnsName);
    19→  if (!resolved) return;
    20→
    21→  const remoteMetadata = await folderService.fetchAndDecryptMetadata(
    22→    resolved.cid,
    23→    folderNode.folderKey
    24→  );
    25→
    26→  store.updateFolderChildren(folderId, remoteMetadata.children ?? []);
    27→  store.updateFolderSequence(folderId, resolved.sequenceNumber);
    28→}
    29→
    30→/** Maximum folder nesting depth per FOLD-03 */
    31→export const MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH = 20;
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * State for folder operations.
    35→ */
    36→export type FolderOperationState = {
    37→  isLoading: boolean;
    38→  error: string | null;
    39→};
    40→
    41→/**
    42→ * Get the root folder state from vault and folder stores.
    43→ * Root folder uses vault keys directly.
    44→ */
    45→export function getRootFolderState(
    46→  vaultStore: ReturnType<typeof useVaultStore.getState>,
    47→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
    48→): FolderNode | null {
    49→  if (!vaultStore.rootFolderKey || !vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair || !vaultStore.rootIpnsName) {
    50→    return null;
    51→  }
    52→
    53→  // If we have an explicit root folder in the tree, use it
    54→  const existingRoot = folders['root'];
    55→  if (existingRoot) return existingRoot;
    56→
    57→  // Otherwise construct from vault state
    58→  return {
    59→    id: 'root',
    60→    name: 'My Vault',
    61→    ipnsName: vaultStore.rootIpnsName,
    62→    parentId: null,
    63→    children: [],
    64→    isLoaded: false,
    65→    isLoading: false,
    66→    sequenceNumber: 0n,
    67→    folderKey: vaultStore.rootFolderKey,
    68→    ipnsPrivateKey: vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey,
    69→  };
    70→}
    71→
    72→/**
    73→ * Calculate folder depth by walking up the tree.
    74→ */
    75→export function calculateFolderDepth(
    76→  folderId: string,
    77→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
    78→): number {
    79→  return folderService.getDepth(folderId, folders);
    80→}
    81→

     1→/**
     2→ * Error utilities for CipherBox API error handling.
     3→ *
     4→ * The Orval-generated custom instance (custom-instance.ts) throws plain Error
     5→ * objects with a `.status` property attached:
     6→ *
     7→ *   const err = new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
     8→ *   (err as Error & { status: number }).status = response.status;
     9→ *   throw err;
    10→ *
    11→ * These utilities detect and extract data from such errors.
    12→ */
    13→
    14→/**
    15→ * Detect HTTP 409 Conflict errors from the API.
    16→ * Used for IPNS publish conflict detection (sequence number mismatch).
    17→ */
    18→export function isConflictError(error: unknown): boolean {
    19→  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') return false;
    20→  // Orval custom-instance throws Error objects with .status attached
    21→  const e = error as Record<string, unknown>;
    22→  if (e.status === 409) return true;
    23→  return false;
    24→}
    25→
    26→/**
    27→ * Extract the current server sequence number from a 409 conflict error body.
    28→ *
    29→ * NOTE: The current custom-instance does not parse the error body --
    30→ * it only attaches the status code. The server sends currentSequenceNumber
    31→ * in the 409 JSON body, but the client cannot read it from the thrown Error.
    32→ * Future improvement: parse the response body in custom-instance and attach
    33→ * it to the error. For now, callers can re-sync via IPNS resolution.
    34→ */
    35→export function getConflictSequenceNumber(_error: unknown): string | undefined {
    36→  // The custom-instance does not attach the response body to the thrown error.
    37→  // Callers should resolve fresh state via resolveIpnsRecord() instead.
    38→  return undefined;
    39→}
    40→

  "old_string": "/** Maximum folder nesting depth per FOLD-03 */\nexport const MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH = 20;",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Execute an operation with single-retry conflict resolution.\n *\n * On 409 conflict: shows sync banner, runs resync, adds jitter delay,\n * optionally runs pre-retry validation, then retries once.\n * If the retry also returns 409, throws a user-facing error.\n */\nexport async function withConflictRetry<T>(\n  perform: () => Promise<T>,\n  resync: () => Promise<void>,\n  preRetry?: () => void\n): Promise<T> {\n  try {\n    return await perform();\n  } catch (err) {\n    if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;\n    useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');\n    try {\n      await resync();\n      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));\n      if (preRetry) preRetry();\n      return await perform();\n    } catch (retryErr) {\n      if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {\n        throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');\n      }\n      throw retryErr;\n    } finally {\n      useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** Maximum folder nesting depth per FOLD-03 */\nexport const MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH = 20;"
}
```

  "new_string": "          resolveIpnsRecord(ipnsName)\n            .then((resolved) => resolved?.cid && unpinFromIpfs(resolved.cid))\n            .catch(() => {});"

     1→import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
     2→import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';
     3→import type { FolderNode } from '../stores/folder.store';
     4→import { useSyncStore } from '../stores/sync.store';
     5→import { isConflictError } from '../lib/errors';
     6→import * as folderService from '../services/folder.service';
     7→import { resolveIpnsRecord } from '../services/ipns.service';
     8→
     9→/**
    10→ * Re-sync a specific folder after a 409 conflict.
    11→ *
    12→ * Resolves the folder's IPNS name to get fresh CID + sequenceNumber,
    13→ * fetches and decrypts the metadata, and updates the folder store.
    14→ */
    15→export async function resyncFolder(folderIpnsName: string, folderId: string): Promise<void> {
    16→  const store = useFolderStore.getState();
    17→  const folderNode = store.folders[folderId];
    18→  if (!folderNode) return;
    19→
    20→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderIpnsName);
    21→  if (!resolved) return;
    22→
    23→  const remoteMetadata = await folderService.fetchAndDecryptMetadata(
    24→    resolved.cid,
    25→    folderNode.folderKey
    26→  );
    27→
    28→  store.updateFolderChildren(folderId, remoteMetadata.children ?? []);
    29→  store.updateFolderSequence(folderId, resolved.sequenceNumber);
    30→}
    31→
    32→/**
    33→ * Execute an operation with single-retry conflict resolution.
    34→ *
    35→ * On 409 conflict: shows sync banner, runs resync, adds jitter delay,
    36→ * optionally runs pre-retry validation, then retries once.
    37→ * If the retry also returns 409, throws a user-facing error.
    38→ */
    39→export async function withConflictRetry<T>(
    40→  perform: () => Promise<T>,
    41→  resync: () => Promise<void>,
    42→  preRetry?: () => void
    43→): Promise<T> {
    44→  try {
    45→    return await perform();
    46→  } catch (err) {
    47→    if (!isConflictError(err)) throw err;
    48→    useSyncStore.getState().setConflict('Folder updated by another device, re-syncing...');
    49→    try {
    50→      await resync();
    51→      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400));
    52→      if (preRetry) preRetry();
    53→      return await perform();
    54→    } catch (retryErr) {
    55→      if (isConflictError(retryErr)) {
    56→        throw new Error('Conflict persists after re-sync. Please try again.');
    57→      }
    58→      throw retryErr;
    59→    } finally {
    60→      useSyncStore.getState().clearConflict();
    61→    }
    62→  }
    63→}
    64→
    65→/** Maximum folder nesting depth per FOLD-03 */
    66→export const MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH = 20;
    67→
    68→/**
    69→ * State for folder operations.
    70→ */
    71→export type FolderOperationState = {
    72→  isLoading: boolean;
    73→  error: string | null;
    74→};
    75→
    76→/**
    77→ * Get the root folder state from vault and folder stores.
    78→ * Root folder uses vault keys directly.
    79→ */
    80→export function getRootFolderState(
    81→  vaultStore: ReturnType<typeof useVaultStore.getState>,
    82→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
    83→): FolderNode | null {
    84→  if (!vaultStore.rootFolderKey || !vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair || !vaultStore.rootIpnsName) {
    85→    return null;
    86→  }
    87→
    88→  // If we have an explicit root folder in the tree, use it
    89→  const existingRoot = folders['root'];
    90→  if (existingRoot) return existingRoot;
    91→
    92→  // Otherwise construct from vault state
    93→  return {
    94→    id: 'root',
    95→    name: 'My Vault',
    96→    ipnsName: vaultStore.rootIpnsName,
    97→    parentId: null,
    98→    children: [],
    99→    isLoaded: false,
   100→    isLoading: false,
   101→    sequenceNumber: 0n,
   102→    folderKey: vaultStore.rootFolderKey,
   103→    ipnsPrivateKey: vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey,
   104→  };
   105→}
   106→
   107→/**
   108→ * Calculate folder depth by walking up the tree.
   109→ */
   110→export function calculateFolderDepth(
   111→  folderId: string,
   112→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
   113→): number {
   114→  return folderService.getDepth(folderId, folders);
   115→}
   116→
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's a summary of all changes in this commit:

**`withConflictRetry<T>()` helper** (`folder-helpers.ts`):
- Extracted the 15-line conflict-retry scaffold into a single generic function
- Accepts `perform`, `resync`, and optional `preRetry` callbacks
- Handles all variations: single-folder resync, multi-folder resync, pre-retry validation

**8 call sites simplified** across `useFolderMutations.ts` and `useFileOperations.ts`:
- `handleCreate`, `handleRename`, `handleMove`, `handleMoveItems`, `handleDelete`, `handleDeleteItems` in folder mutations
- `handleAddFile`, `handleAddFiles` in file operations
- Each went from ~15 lines of try/catch/resync/retry to a single `withConflictRetry()` call

**Dead code removed**:
- `unpinCid` parameter from `deleteFolder` service (was declared but never called)
- `useSyncStore` and `isConflictError` imports from both hooks (now internal to the helper)

**Orphaned file cleanup** (`handleDelete`):
- `performDelete` now propagates `orphanedIpnsNames` from `deleteFolder`/`deleteFileFromFolder`
- After delete: fire-and-forget resolve+unpin for orphaned metadata CIDs

**Net result**: -97 lines, 4 files changed.

> AGENT

One unresolved thread — CodeRabbit flagging that `unpinFromIpfs` inside the `.then()` can create unhandled rejections. This is valid — the outer `.catch(() => {})` catches errors from `resolveIpnsRecord` and from the `.then()` callback itself, but if `unpinFromIpfs` returns a rejected Promise that isn't returned from the `.then()`, it becomes an unhandled rejection.
